FILE 2 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\App.config
====================================================================================================
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8" ?>
<configuration>
  <startup>
    <supportedRuntime version="v4.0" sku=".NETFramework,Version=v4.5.2" />
  </startup>
  <appSettings>
    <!--
      Microsoft Access database used by the program. A relative path is searched for next
      to the program and then in the folders above it, so when you run from Visual Studio
      (bin\Debug) the program uses Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb in the project folder - the same
      file you can open in Microsoft Access. If the file does not exist, the program creates
      it with all tables and the sample data (see TreeYield.Core\Data\DatabaseInitializer.vb).

      A full path also works, for example:  C:\TreeYield\TreeYieldDb.mdb
      After building, this file is copied next to the program as
      "Interactive Tree Database.exe.config".
    -->
    <add key="DatabaseFile" value="Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb" />

    <!--
      OLE DB provider. Microsoft.Jet.OLEDB.4.0 is built into Windows (32-bit only, which is
      why the project's Platform target is x86). Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0 also works if the
      Access Database Engine is installed in the same bitness as the program.
    -->
    <add key="DatabaseProvider" value="Microsoft.Jet.OLEDB.4.0" />
  </appSettings>
</configuration>

FILE 4 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\ApplicationEvents.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Configuration
Imports Microsoft.VisualBasic.ApplicationServices

Namespace My

    ' Events of the Visual Basic application framework (Project > Properties >
    ' Application > View Application Events):
    '   Startup            - raised before the main form is created.
    '   UnhandledException - raised when an error is not caught anywhere else.
    Partial Friend Class MyApplication

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Reads the settings and builds the services before MainForm is created.
        ''' If the settings file is broken the program explains why and stops.
        ''' </summary>
        Private Sub MyApplication_Startup(sender As Object, e As StartupEventArgs) Handles Me.Startup
            ' Errors on background tasks and non-UI threads are at least written to the log.
            AddHandler AppDomain.CurrentDomain.UnhandledException,
                Sub(s, args) ErrorReporter.Log(TryCast(args.ExceptionObject, Exception))
            AddHandler TaskScheduler.UnobservedTaskException,
                Sub(s, args)
                    ErrorReporter.Log(args.Exception)
                    args.SetObserved()
                End Sub

            Try
                Global.TreeYield.WinForms.AppServices.Current = Global.TreeYield.WinForms.AppServices.FromConfiguration()
            Catch ex As Exception When TypeOf ex Is ConfigurationErrorsException OrElse TypeOf ex Is ArgumentException
                ErrorReporter.Log(ex)
                MessageBox.Show(
                    "The program settings could not be read." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & ex.Message & vbCrLf & vbCrLf &
                    "Check the DatabaseFile setting in ""Interactive Tree Database.exe.config"".",
                    "Tree Yield & Revenue Calculator", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error)
                e.Cancel = True
            End Try
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Last line of defense: any error that no other code caught is logged and
        ''' shown as a friendly message instead of crashing the program.
        ''' (Visual Studio's debugger stops on the error first, so this runs when the
        ''' program is started without debugging.)
        ''' </summary>
        Private Sub MyApplication_UnhandledException(sender As Object, e As UnhandledExceptionEventArgs) Handles Me.UnhandledException
            ErrorReporter.HandleUnexpected(e.Exception)
            e.ExitApplication = False
        End Sub

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 5 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\AppServices.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Configuration
Imports TreeYield.Core.Data
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services

''' <summary>
''' The "composition root": creates every repository and service once and hands
''' them to the forms. Forms never create database objects themselves, which keeps
''' the presentation layer separate from the data layer.
''' </summary>
Friend NotInheritable Class AppServices

    ''' <summary>appSettings key with the path of the Access database file.</summary>
    Public Const DatabaseFileSetting As String = "DatabaseFile"

    ''' <summary>appSettings key with the OLE DB provider name.</summary>
    Public Const DatabaseProviderSetting As String = "DatabaseProvider"

    ''' <summary>Used when App.config does not name a database file.</summary>
    Public Const DefaultDatabaseFile As String = "Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb"

    ''' <summary>
    ''' The services for the running program. Set once at startup by
    ''' ApplicationEvents.vb, before the main window is created.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Shared Property Current As AppServices

    ''' <summary>Builds all services for one Access database file.</summary>
    ''' <param name="databasePath">Full path of the .mdb file (created if missing).</param>
    ''' <param name="provider">OLE DB provider, normally Microsoft.Jet.OLEDB.4.0.</param>
    Public Sub New(databasePath As String, provider As String)
        ConnectionFactory = New AccessConnectionFactory(databasePath, provider)

        Dim speciesRepository As New SpeciesRepository(ConnectionFactory)
        Dim categoryRepository As New CategoryRepository(ConnectionFactory)
        Dim scenarioRepository As New ScenarioRepository(ConnectionFactory)
        Dim userRepository As New UserRepository(ConnectionFactory)
        Dim auditRepository As New AuditLogRepository(ConnectionFactory)

        Initializer = New DatabaseInitializer(ConnectionFactory)
        Species = New SpeciesService(speciesRepository, categoryRepository, scenarioRepository, auditRepository, Initializer)
        Scenarios = New ScenarioService(scenarioRepository)
        Auth = New AuthService(userRepository, auditRepository)
        Session = New AdminSession()
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Opens connections to the Access database file.</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property ConnectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory

    ''' <summary>Creates the database file and its tables when needed.</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property Initializer As DatabaseInitializer

    ''' <summary>Species records (read for everyone, write for administrators).</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property Species As SpeciesService

    ''' <summary>Saved scenarios.</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property Scenarios As ScenarioService

    ''' <summary>Administrator login and passwords.</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property Auth As AuthService

    ''' <summary>Who is signed in as administrator right now.</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property Session As AdminSession

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Reads the database settings from "Interactive Tree Database.exe.config" and finds
    ''' the database file. A relative path is looked up next to the program first and then
    ''' in the folders above it, so running from Visual Studio (bin\Debug) uses the copy in
    ''' the project folder - the same file you open in Microsoft Access.
    ''' </summary>
    ''' <exception cref="ConfigurationErrorsException">The settings file cannot be read.</exception>
    Public Shared Function FromConfiguration() As AppServices
        Dim databaseFile As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings(DatabaseFileSetting)
        If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(databaseFile) Then
            databaseFile = DefaultDatabaseFile
        End If

        Dim provider As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings(DatabaseProviderSetting)
        If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(provider) Then
            provider = AccessConnectionFactory.DefaultProvider
        End If

        Dim databasePath As String = DatabaseFileLocator.Locate(Application.StartupPath, databaseFile.Trim())
        Return New AppServices(databasePath, provider.Trim())
    End Function

    ''' <summary>Creates the database if needed and makes sure an administrator account exists.</summary>
    Public Async Function InitializeDatabaseAsync() As Task
        Await Initializer.InitializeAsync()
        Await Auth.EnsureDefaultAdminAsync()
    End Function

End Class


====================================================================================================
FILE 6 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\Controls\ChipButton.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.ComponentModel
Imports System.Drawing.Drawing2D

''' <summary>Visual style of a <see cref="ChipButton"/>.</summary>
Public Enum ChipStyle
    ''' <summary>Filter chip: gray, or light blue when <see cref="ChipButton.Selected"/>.</summary>
    Toggle

    ''' <summary>Main action: solid blue with white text.</summary>
    Primary

    ''' <summary>Other actions: white with a gray border.</summary>
    Secondary

    ''' <summary>Destructive action (delete): red text and border.</summary>
    Danger
End Enum

''' <summary>
''' A pill-shaped button used for the filter chips ("All Species", "Fruit-Bearing" ...)
''' and for action buttons. It inherits from Button, so it keeps keyboard support
''' (Tab, Enter, Space), AcceptButton/CancelButton support and screen-reader support;
''' only the painting is custom.
''' </summary>
<ToolboxItem(True)>
<Description("Pill-shaped button for filter chips and actions.")>
Public Class ChipButton
    Inherits Button

    Private _selected As Boolean
    Private _style As ChipStyle = ChipStyle.Toggle
    Private _hovering As Boolean
    Private _pressed As Boolean

    ''' <summary>Creates the button.</summary>
    Public Sub New()
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint Or ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint Or
                 ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer Or ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, True)
        FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat
        FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0
        UseVisualStyleBackColor = False
        Cursor = Cursors.Hand
        Padding = New Padding(18, 0, 18, 0)
        Size = New Size(120, 36)
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>For <see cref="ChipStyle.Toggle"/>: whether this chip is the active choice in its group.</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <DefaultValue(False)>
    <Description("Whether the chip is the active choice in its group.")>
    Public Property Selected As Boolean
        Get
            Return _selected
        End Get
        Set(value As Boolean)
            If _selected = value Then
                Return
            End If

            _selected = value
            Invalidate()
            ' Lets screen readers announce the new checked state.
            AccessibilityNotifyClients(AccessibleEvents.StateChange, -1)
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Which color scheme the button uses.</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <DefaultValue(GetType(ChipStyle), "Toggle")>
    <Description("Toggle (filter chip), Primary, Secondary or Danger.")>
    Public Property ChipStyle As ChipStyle
        Get
            Return _style
        End Get
        Set(value As ChipStyle)
            _style = value
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Width that fits the text plus padding; used when AutoSize is True.</summary>
    Public Overrides Function GetPreferredSize(proposedSize As Size) As Size
        Dim textSize As Size = TextRenderer.MeasureText(If(Text, String.Empty), Font)
        Return New Size(textSize.Width + Padding.Horizontal, Math.Max(textSize.Height + 16, 32))
    End Function

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseEnter(e As EventArgs)
        _hovering = True
        Invalidate()
        MyBase.OnMouseEnter(e)
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseLeave(e As EventArgs)
        _hovering = False
        _pressed = False
        Invalidate()
        MyBase.OnMouseLeave(e)
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseDown(mevent As MouseEventArgs)
        If mevent.Button = MouseButtons.Left Then
            _pressed = True
            Invalidate()
        End If

        MyBase.OnMouseDown(mevent)
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseUp(mevent As MouseEventArgs)
        _pressed = False
        Invalidate()
        MyBase.OnMouseUp(mevent)
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnEnabledChanged(e As EventArgs)
        Invalidate()
        MyBase.OnEnabledChanged(e)
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Draws the pill, the text and (when focused by keyboard) a focus ring.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Sub OnPaint(pevent As PaintEventArgs)
        Dim g As Graphics = pevent.Graphics
        g.Clear(DrawingHelpers.GetParentBackColor(Me))
        g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias

        Dim back As Color
        Dim fore As Color
        Dim border As Color
        GetColors(back, fore, border)

        Dim bounds As New RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 2.0F, Height - 2.0F)
        Using path As GraphicsPath = DrawingHelpers.RoundedRectangle(bounds, bounds.Height / 2.0F)
            Using brush As New SolidBrush(back)
                g.FillPath(brush, path)
            End Using

            If Not border.IsEmpty Then
                Using pen As New Pen(border)
                    g.DrawPath(pen, path)
                End Using
            End If
        End Using

        Dim flags As TextFormatFlags = TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter Or TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter Or
                                       TextFormatFlags.SingleLine Or TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis
        If Not ShowKeyboardCues Then
            flags = flags Or TextFormatFlags.HidePrefix
        End If

        Dim textBounds As New Rectangle(Padding.Left \ 2, 0, Width - Padding.Horizontal \ 2, Height)
        TextRenderer.DrawText(g, Text, Font, textBounds, fore, flags)

        ' Visible focus indicator for keyboard users (WCAG 2.4.7).
        If Focused AndAlso ShowFocusCues Then
            Dim ring As New RectangleF(2.0F, 2.0F, Width - 5.0F, Height - 5.0F)
            Using path As GraphicsPath = DrawingHelpers.RoundedRectangle(ring, ring.Height / 2.0F),
                  pen As New Pen(Theme.Accent, 2.0F)
                g.DrawPath(pen, path)
            End Using
        End If
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Reports "checked" to screen readers when a toggle chip is selected.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Function CreateAccessibilityInstance() As AccessibleObject
        Return New ChipAccessibleObject(Me)
    End Function

    Private Sub GetColors(ByRef back As Color, ByRef fore As Color, ByRef border As Color)
        border = Color.Empty

        If Not Enabled Then
            back = Color.FromArgb(241, 241, 241)
            fore = Theme.TextDisabled
            Return
        End If

        Select Case _style
            Case ChipStyle.Primary
                back = If(_pressed OrElse _hovering, Theme.AccentHover, Theme.Accent)
                fore = Color.White

            Case ChipStyle.Secondary
                back = If(_pressed, Theme.ChipHover, If(_hovering, Color.FromArgb(243, 243, 243), Theme.Surface))
                fore = Theme.TextPrimary
                border = Theme.Border

            Case ChipStyle.Danger
                back = If(_pressed OrElse _hovering, Theme.DangerSoft, Theme.Surface)
                fore = Theme.Danger
                border = Color.FromArgb(240, 180, 176)

            Case Else
                If _selected Then
                    back = If(_hovering, Color.FromArgb(207, 226, 252), Theme.AccentSoft)
                    fore = Theme.AccentText
                Else
                    back = If(_pressed OrElse _hovering, Theme.ChipHover, Theme.Chip)
                    fore = Theme.TextPrimary
                End If
        End Select
    End Sub

    Private NotInheritable Class ChipAccessibleObject
        Inherits ButtonBaseAccessibleObject

        Public Sub New(owner As ChipButton)
            MyBase.New(owner)
        End Sub

        Public Overrides ReadOnly Property State As AccessibleStates
            Get
                Dim chip As ChipButton = DirectCast(Owner, ChipButton)
                Dim baseState As AccessibleStates = MyBase.State
                If chip.ChipStyle = ChipStyle.Toggle AndAlso chip.Selected Then
                    Return baseState Or AccessibleStates.Checked
                End If

                Return baseState
            End Get
        End Property
    End Class

End Class


====================================================================================================
FILE 7 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\Controls\FlatSlider.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.ComponentModel
Imports System.Drawing.Drawing2D
Imports System.Globalization

''' <summary>
''' A flat slider in the style of the design mock-up: a thick dark track up to the
''' thumb, a thin gray rail after it, and a narrow vertical thumb. Works with the
''' mouse (click, drag, wheel) and the keyboard (arrows, Page Up/Down, Home/End).
''' </summary>
<ToolboxItem(True)>
<DefaultEvent("ValueChanged")>
<DefaultProperty("Value")>
<Description("Flat slider for choosing a whole number.")>
Public Class FlatSlider
    Inherits Control

    Private Const EdgePadding As Integer = 8

    Private _minimum As Integer = 1
    Private _maximum As Integer = 1000
    Private _value As Integer = 25
    Private _smallChange As Integer = 1
    Private _largeChange As Integer = 10
    Private _dragging As Boolean

    ''' <summary>Raised whenever <see cref="Value"/> changes.</summary>
    <Category("Action")>
    <Description("Occurs when the value changes.")>
    Public Event ValueChanged As EventHandler

    ''' <summary>Creates the slider.</summary>
    Public Sub New()
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint Or ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint Or ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer Or
                 ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw Or ControlStyles.Selectable Or ControlStyles.StandardClick, True)
        TabStop = True
        Cursor = Cursors.Hand
        Size = New Size(240, 32)
        AccessibleRole = AccessibleRole.Slider
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Lowest value.</summary>
    <Category("Behavior")>
    <DefaultValue(1)>
    Public Property Minimum As Integer
        Get
            Return _minimum
        End Get
        Set(value As Integer)
            _minimum = value
            If _maximum < _minimum Then
                _maximum = _minimum
            End If

            Me.Value = _value
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Highest value.</summary>
    <Category("Behavior")>
    <DefaultValue(1000)>
    Public Property Maximum As Integer
        Get
            Return _maximum
        End Get
        Set(value As Integer)
            _maximum = value
            If _minimum > _maximum Then
                _minimum = _maximum
            End If

            Me.Value = _value
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Current value, always kept between <see cref="Minimum"/> and <see cref="Maximum"/>.</summary>
    <Category("Behavior")>
    <DefaultValue(25)>
    Public Property Value As Integer
        Get
            Return _value
        End Get
        Set(value As Integer)
            Dim clamped As Integer = Math.Max(_minimum, Math.Min(_maximum, value))
            If clamped = _value Then
                Return
            End If

            _value = clamped
            Invalidate()
            AccessibilityNotifyClients(AccessibleEvents.ValueChange, -1)
            OnValueChanged(EventArgs.Empty)
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Step for arrow keys and the mouse wheel.</summary>
    <Category("Behavior")>
    <DefaultValue(1)>
    Public Property SmallChange As Integer
        Get
            Return _smallChange
        End Get
        Set(value As Integer)
            _smallChange = Math.Max(1, value)
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Step for Page Up / Page Down.</summary>
    <Category("Behavior")>
    <DefaultValue(10)>
    Public Property LargeChange As Integer
        Get
            Return _largeChange
        End Get
        Set(value As Integer)
            _largeChange = Math.Max(1, value)
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Raises <see cref="ValueChanged"/>.</summary>
    Protected Overridable Sub OnValueChanged(e As EventArgs)
        RaiseEvent ValueChanged(Me, e)
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Arrow keys normally move focus between controls; here they move the slider.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Function IsInputKey(keyData As Keys) As Boolean
        Select Case keyData And Keys.KeyCode
            Case Keys.Left, Keys.Right, Keys.Up, Keys.Down
                Return True
            Case Else
                Return MyBase.IsInputKey(keyData)
        End Select
    End Function

    Protected Overrides Sub OnKeyDown(e As KeyEventArgs)
        Select Case e.KeyCode
            Case Keys.Left, Keys.Down
                Value -= _smallChange
            Case Keys.Right, Keys.Up
                Value += _smallChange
            Case Keys.PageDown
                Value -= _largeChange
            Case Keys.PageUp
                Value += _largeChange
            Case Keys.Home
                Value = _minimum
            Case Keys.End
                Value = _maximum
            Case Else
                MyBase.OnKeyDown(e)
                Return
        End Select

        e.Handled = True
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseDown(e As MouseEventArgs)
        MyBase.OnMouseDown(e)
        If e.Button <> MouseButtons.Left Then
            Return
        End If

        Focus()
        _dragging = True
        Capture = True
        Value = ValueFromX(e.X)
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseMove(e As MouseEventArgs)
        MyBase.OnMouseMove(e)
        If _dragging Then
            Value = ValueFromX(e.X)
        End If
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseUp(e As MouseEventArgs)
        MyBase.OnMouseUp(e)
        _dragging = False
        Capture = False
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnMouseWheel(e As MouseEventArgs)
        MyBase.OnMouseWheel(e)
        Value += Math.Sign(e.Delta) * _smallChange

        ' Stop the wheel from also scrolling the parent.
        Dim handled As HandledMouseEventArgs = TryCast(e, HandledMouseEventArgs)
        If handled IsNot Nothing Then
            handled.Handled = True
        End If
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnGotFocus(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnGotFocus(e)
        Invalidate()
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnLostFocus(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnLostFocus(e)
        Invalidate()
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnEnabledChanged(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnEnabledChanged(e)
        Invalidate()
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Draws the track, rail, end dot and thumb.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Sub OnPaint(e As PaintEventArgs)
        Dim g As Graphics = e.Graphics
        g.Clear(DrawingHelpers.GetParentBackColor(Me))
        g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias

        Dim trackColor As Color = If(Enabled, Theme.SliderTrack, Theme.TextDisabled)
        Dim left As Single = EdgePadding
        Dim right As Single = Width - EdgePadding
        Dim centerY As Single = Height / 2.0F
        Dim thumbX As Single = GetThumbX()

        Using rail As New Pen(Theme.SliderRail, 2.0F)
            rail.StartCap = LineCap.Round
            rail.EndCap = LineCap.Round
            g.DrawLine(rail, thumbX, centerY, right, centerY)
        End Using

        Using track As New Pen(trackColor, 4.0F)
            track.StartCap = LineCap.Round
            track.EndCap = LineCap.Round
            g.DrawLine(track, left, centerY, thumbX, centerY)
        End Using

        ' Small dot that marks the end of the range.
        Using dot As New SolidBrush(trackColor)
            g.FillEllipse(dot, right - 2.0F, centerY - 2.0F, 4.0F, 4.0F)
        End Using

        Dim thumbHeight As Single = Math.Min(22.0F, Height - 6.0F)
        Dim thumb As New RectangleF(thumbX - 2.5F, centerY - thumbHeight / 2.0F, 5.0F, thumbHeight)

        If Focused Then
            ' Soft halo shows which control has keyboard focus.
            Dim halo As RectangleF = RectangleF.Inflate(thumb, 5.0F, 3.0F)
            Using path As GraphicsPath = DrawingHelpers.RoundedRectangle(halo, 5.0F),
                  brush As New SolidBrush(Theme.AccentSoft)
                g.FillPath(brush, path)
            End Using
        End If

        Using path As GraphicsPath = DrawingHelpers.RoundedRectangle(thumb, 2.5F),
              brush As New SolidBrush(trackColor)
            g.FillPath(brush, path)
        End Using
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Gives screen readers the current value.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Function CreateAccessibilityInstance() As AccessibleObject
        Return New SliderAccessibleObject(Me)
    End Function

    Private Function GetThumbX() As Single
        Dim usable As Single = Width - 2 * EdgePadding
        If _maximum = _minimum OrElse usable <= 0 Then
            Return EdgePadding
        End If

        Return EdgePadding + usable * (_value - _minimum) / CSng(_maximum - _minimum)
    End Function

    Private Function ValueFromX(x As Integer) As Integer
        Dim usable As Single = Width - 2 * EdgePadding
        If usable <= 0 Then
            Return _minimum
        End If

        Dim fraction As Single = Math.Max(0.0F, Math.Min(1.0F, (x - EdgePadding) / usable))
        Return _minimum + CInt(Math.Round(fraction * (_maximum - _minimum)))
    End Function

    Private NotInheritable Class SliderAccessibleObject
        Inherits ControlAccessibleObject

        Public Sub New(owner As FlatSlider)
            MyBase.New(owner)
        End Sub

        Public Overrides Property Value As String
            Get
                Return DirectCast(Owner, FlatSlider).Value.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)
            End Get
            Set(value As String)
                Dim parsed As Integer
                If Integer.TryParse(value, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, parsed) Then
                    DirectCast(Owner, FlatSlider).Value = parsed
                End If
            End Set
        End Property

        Public Overrides ReadOnly Property Role As AccessibleRole
            Get
                Return AccessibleRole.Slider
            End Get
        End Property
    End Class

End Class

FILE 8 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\Controls\RoundedPanel.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.ComponentModel
Imports System.Drawing.Drawing2D

''' <summary>
''' A Panel with rounded corners and an optional 1-pixel border. BackColor is the
''' fill color; the area outside the corners shows the parent's background.
''' </summary>
<ToolboxItem(True)>
<Description("Panel with rounded corners and an optional border.")>
Public Class RoundedPanel
    Inherits Panel

    Private _cornerRadius As Integer = 14
    Private _borderColor As Color = Color.Empty

    ''' <summary>Creates the panel.</summary>
    Public Sub New()
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint Or ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint Or
                 ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer Or ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, True)
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Radius of the corners in pixels.</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <DefaultValue(14)>
    <Description("Radius of the rounded corners, in pixels.")>
    Public Property CornerRadius As Integer
        Get
            Return _cornerRadius
        End Get
        Set(value As Integer)
            _cornerRadius = If(value < 0, 0, value)
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Border color. Color.Empty means no border.</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <Description("Color of the 1-pixel border. Leave empty for no border.")>
    Public Property BorderColor As Color
        Get
            Return _borderColor
        End Get
        Set(value As Color)
            _borderColor = value
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ' Tells the designer when BorderColor differs from its default (so it is saved).
    Private Function ShouldSerializeBorderColor() As Boolean
        Return Not _borderColor.IsEmpty
    End Function

    Private Sub ResetBorderColor()
        BorderColor = Color.Empty
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Paints the parent color, then the rounded fill and border.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Sub OnPaintBackground(e As PaintEventArgs)
        Dim g As Graphics = e.Graphics
        g.Clear(DrawingHelpers.GetParentBackColor(Me))
        g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias

        Dim bounds As New RectangleF(0, 0, Width - 1, Height - 1)
        Using path As GraphicsPath = DrawingHelpers.RoundedRectangle(bounds, _cornerRadius),
              fill As New SolidBrush(BackColor)
            g.FillPath(fill, path)
            If Not _borderColor.IsEmpty Then
                Using pen As New Pen(_borderColor)
                    g.DrawPath(pen, path)
                End Using
            End If
        End Using
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Scales the corner radius together with the rest of the form on high-DPI screens.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Sub ScaleControl(factor As SizeF, specified As BoundsSpecified)
        MyBase.ScaleControl(factor, specified)
        _cornerRadius = CInt(Math.Round(_cornerRadius * factor.Height))
    End Sub

End Class


====================================================================================================
FILE 9 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\Controls\StatCard.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.ComponentModel
Imports System.Drawing.Drawing2D

''' <summary>
''' A white rounded card that shows a small title, one large value and an optional
''' caption, like "Annual Yield / 7,500 kg". The value font shrinks automatically
''' when a very large number would not fit.
''' </summary>
<ToolboxItem(True)>
<Description("Card that shows a title, a large value and a caption.")>
Public Class StatCard
    Inherits Control

    Private Const Inset As Integer = 16
    Private Const MinimumValueFontSize As Single = 9.0F

    Private _title As String = "Title"
    Private _value As String = "-"
    Private _caption As String = String.Empty
    Private _valueColor As Color = Color.FromArgb(31, 31, 31)
    Private _fittedValueFont As Font

    ''' <summary>Creates the card.</summary>
    Public Sub New()
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint Or ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint Or
                 ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer Or ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, True)
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.Selectable, False)
        TabStop = False
        Size = New Size(260, 96)
        AccessibleRole = AccessibleRole.StaticText
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Small gray heading, for example "Annual Yield".</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <DefaultValue("Title")>
    Public Property Title As String
        Get
            Return _title
        End Get
        Set(value As String)
            _title = If(value, String.Empty)
            UpdateAccessibleName()
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>The large value, for example "7,500 kg".</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <DefaultValue("-")>
    Public Property Value As String
        Get
            Return _value
        End Get
        Set(value As String)
            Dim newValue As String = If(value, String.Empty)
            If newValue = _value Then
                Return
            End If

            _value = newValue
            RefitValueFont()
            UpdateAccessibleName()
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Optional small text under the value.</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    <DefaultValue("")>
    Public Property Caption As String
        Get
            Return _caption
        End Get
        Set(value As String)
            _caption = If(value, String.Empty)
            UpdateAccessibleName()
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Color of the large value (green for money).</summary>
    <Category("Appearance")>
    Public Property ValueColor As Color
        Get
            Return _valueColor
        End Get
        Set(value As Color)
            _valueColor = value
            Invalidate()
        End Set
    End Property

    Private Function ShouldSerializeValueColor() As Boolean
        Return _valueColor <> Color.FromArgb(31, 31, 31)
    End Function

    Private Sub ResetValueColor()
        ValueColor = Color.FromArgb(31, 31, 31)
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnFontChanged(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnFontChanged(e)
        RefitValueFont()
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub OnSizeChanged(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnSizeChanged(e)
        RefitValueFont()
    End Sub

    Protected Overrides Sub Dispose(disposing As Boolean)
        If disposing Then
            DisposeFittedFont()
        End If

        MyBase.Dispose(disposing)
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Draws the card background, title, value and caption.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Sub OnPaint(e As PaintEventArgs)
        Dim g As Graphics = e.Graphics
        g.Clear(DrawingHelpers.GetParentBackColor(Me))
        g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias

        Dim bounds As New RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 2.0F, Height - 2.0F)
        Using path As GraphicsPath = DrawingHelpers.RoundedRectangle(bounds, 10.0F)
            Using fill As New SolidBrush(Theme.Surface)
                g.FillPath(fill, path)
            End Using

            Using pen As New Pen(Theme.Border)
                g.DrawPath(pen, path)
            End Using
        End Using

        Const flags As TextFormatFlags = TextFormatFlags.Left Or TextFormatFlags.Top Or TextFormatFlags.SingleLine Or
                                         TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis Or TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix
        Dim textWidth As Integer = Width - 2 * Inset

        Using titleFont As New Font(Theme.FontFamily, 9.75F),
              captionFont As New Font(Theme.FontFamily, 8.25F)
            Dim valueFont As Font = If(_fittedValueFont, Font)
            Dim titleHeight As Integer = TextRenderer.MeasureText(g, "Ag", titleFont).Height
            Dim valueHeight As Integer = TextRenderer.MeasureText(g, "Ag", valueFont).Height

            Dim y As Integer = Inset - 2
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, _title, titleFont, New Rectangle(Inset, y, textWidth, titleHeight), Theme.TextSecondary, flags)

            y += titleHeight + 2
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, _value, valueFont, New Rectangle(Inset, y, textWidth, valueHeight), _valueColor, flags)

            If _caption.Length > 0 Then
                y += valueHeight
                Dim captionHeight As Integer = TextRenderer.MeasureText(g, "Ag", captionFont).Height
                TextRenderer.DrawText(g, _caption, captionFont, New Rectangle(Inset, y, textWidth, captionHeight), Theme.TextSecondary, flags)
            End If
        End Using
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Finds the largest font size (down to 9 pt) at which the value fits on one line.
    ''' Done when the value, font or size changes, not on every paint.
    ''' </summary>
    Private Sub RefitValueFont()
        DisposeFittedFont()
        Dim available As Integer = Width - 2 * Inset
        If available <= 0 OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(_value) Then
            Return
        End If

        If TextRenderer.MeasureText(_value, Font).Width <= available Then
            Return
        End If

        Dim size As Single = Font.SizeInPoints - 1.0F
        While size >= MinimumValueFontSize
            Dim candidate As New Font(Font.FontFamily, size, Font.Style)
            If TextRenderer.MeasureText(_value, candidate).Width <= available OrElse size - 1.0F < MinimumValueFontSize Then
                _fittedValueFont = candidate
                Return
            End If

            candidate.Dispose()
            size -= 1.0F
        End While
    End Sub

    Private Sub DisposeFittedFont()
        If _fittedValueFont IsNot Nothing Then
            _fittedValueFont.Dispose()
            _fittedValueFont = Nothing
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub UpdateAccessibleName()
        ' Screen readers read the whole card as one sentence, e.g. "Annual Yield: 7,500 kg".
        AccessibleName = If(_caption.Length > 0,
                            _title & ": " & _value & ". " & _caption,
                            _title & ": " & _value)
    End Sub

End Class

Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services

''' <summary>
''' Administrator screen: add, edit and delete species records, view the audit log,
''' change the password and restore the sample data. Only opened after a successful login.
''' </summary>
Public Class AdminForm

    Private Const AuditEntriesToShow As Integer = 200

    Private ReadOnly _services As AppServices
    Private _species As New List(Of TreeSpecies)()
    Private _categories As New List(Of Category)()
    Private _dataChanged As Boolean

    Friend Sub New(services As AppServices)
        If services Is Nothing Then
            Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(services))
        End If

        _services = services

        ' This call is required by the designer.
        InitializeComponent()

        Icon = DrawingHelpers.AppIcon
        GridStyler.Apply(gridSpecies)
        GridStyler.Apply(gridAudit)
        GridStyler.AlignRight(colYield, "#,##0.##")
        GridStyler.AlignRight(colPrice, Formatters.PesoSign & "#,##0.00")
        NativeMethods.SetCueBanner(txtFilter, "Filter by name or product...")

        Dim user As AppUser = _services.Session.CurrentUser
        lblSignedIn.Text = "Signed in as " & If(user Is Nothing, "-", user.Username) &
                           ". Changes are saved immediately and recorded in the audit log."
        UpdateButtons()
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>True when any species record was added, changed or deleted (the main window then reloads).</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property DataChanged As Boolean
        Get
            Return _dataChanged
        End Get
    End Property

    Private ReadOnly Property CurrentUser As AppUser
        Get
            Return _services.Session.CurrentUser
        End Get
    End Property

    Protected Overrides Async Sub OnShown(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnShown(e)
        Await LoadSpeciesAsync(Nothing)
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Called before every change. If the session timed out, the window closes so
    ''' the administrator has to sign in again.
    ''' </summary>
    Private Function EnsureSessionActive() As Boolean
        If _services.Session.IsSignedIn Then
            _services.Session.Touch()
            Return True
        End If

        ErrorReporter.ShowInfo(Me, "Your administrator session has ended. Please sign in again.")
        Close()
        Return False
    End Function

    ' ------------------------------------------------------------------
    ' Species list
    ' ------------------------------------------------------------------

    Private Async Function LoadSpeciesAsync(speciesIdToSelect As Integer?) As Task
        SetBusy(True)
        Try
            _categories = Await _services.Species.GetCategoriesAsync()
            _species = Await _services.Species.GetAllAsync()
            ApplyFilter(speciesIdToSelect)
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrorReporter.ShowError(Me, "load the species records", ex)
        Finally
            SetBusy(False)
        End Try
    End Function

    ''' <summary>Filters the loaded list in memory (the admin list is small) and fills the grid.</summary>
    Private Sub ApplyFilter(speciesIdToSelect As Integer?)
        Dim filter As String = txtFilter.Text.Trim()
        Dim visible As List(Of TreeSpecies) = _species.Where(
            Function(s) filter.Length = 0 OrElse
                        ContainsText(s.CommonName, filter) OrElse
                        ContainsText(s.ScientificName, filter) OrElse
                        ContainsText(s.ProductType, filter)).ToList()

        gridSpecies.Rows.Clear()
        For Each s As TreeSpecies In visible
            Dim rotation As Boolean = s.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation
            Dim years As String = If(rotation,
                                     s.YearsToFirstHarvest & " (rotation)",
                                     s.YearsToFirstHarvest & " / " & s.YearsToFullProduction)

            Dim index As Integer = gridSpecies.Rows.Add(s.CommonName, s.CategoryName, s.ProductType, s.YieldPerTreePerYear,
                                                        s.YieldUnit, s.PricePerUnit, If(rotation, "Rotation", "Annual"), years,
                                                        Formatters.LocalDateTime(s.UpdatedAtUtc))
            gridSpecies.Rows(index).Tag = s

            If speciesIdToSelect.HasValue AndAlso s.SpeciesId = speciesIdToSelect.Value Then
                gridSpecies.CurrentCell = gridSpecies.Rows(index).Cells(0)
            End If
        Next

        Dim empty As Boolean = visible.Count = 0
        gridSpecies.Visible = Not empty
        lblSpeciesEmpty.Visible = empty
        lblSpeciesEmpty.Text = If(_species.Count = 0,
                                  "There are no species yet. Click Add Species to create one.",
                                  "No species match """ & filter & """.")
        lblSpeciesCount.Text = String.Format("{0} of {1} records", visible.Count, _species.Count)
        UpdateButtons()
    End Sub

    Private Shared Function ContainsText(text As String, part As String) As Boolean
        Return text IsNot Nothing AndAlso text.IndexOf(part, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
    End Function

    Private Function SelectedSpecies() As TreeSpecies
        If gridSpecies.CurrentRow Is Nothing OrElse Not gridSpecies.Visible Then
            Return Nothing
        End If

        Return TryCast(gridSpecies.CurrentRow.Tag, TreeSpecies)
    End Function

    ''' <summary>ID of the selected species, or Nothing when no row is selected.</summary>
    Private Function SelectedSpeciesId() As Integer?
        Dim selected As TreeSpecies = SelectedSpecies()
        If selected Is Nothing Then
            Return Nothing
        End If

        Return selected.SpeciesId
    End Function

    Private Sub UpdateButtons()
        Dim hasSelection As Boolean = SelectedSpecies() IsNot Nothing
        btnEdit.Enabled = hasSelection
        btnDelete.Enabled = hasSelection
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtFilter_TextChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles txtFilter.TextChanged
        ApplyFilter(SelectedSpeciesId())
    End Sub

    Private Sub gridSpecies_SelectionChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles gridSpecies.SelectionChanged
        UpdateButtons()
    End Sub

    Private Async Sub gridSpecies_CellDoubleClick(sender As Object, e As DataGridViewCellEventArgs) Handles gridSpecies.CellDoubleClick
        If e.RowIndex >= 0 Then
            Await EditSpeciesAsync(SelectedSpecies())
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Async Sub gridSpecies_KeyDown(sender As Object, e As KeyEventArgs) Handles gridSpecies.KeyDown
        If e.KeyCode = Keys.Enter Then
            e.SuppressKeyPress = True
            Await EditSpeciesAsync(SelectedSpecies())
        ElseIf e.KeyCode = Keys.Delete Then
            e.SuppressKeyPress = True
            Await DeleteSpeciesAsync(SelectedSpecies())
        End If
    End Sub

    ' ------------------------------------------------------------------
    ' Add / edit / delete
    ' ------------------------------------------------------------------

    Private Async Sub btnAdd_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnAdd.Click
        If Not EnsureSessionActive() Then
            Return
        End If

        Using editor As New SpeciesEditorForm(_services.Species, _categories, Nothing, CurrentUser)
            If editor.ShowDialog(Me) = System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK Then
                _dataChanged = True
                txtFilter.Clear()
                Await LoadSpeciesAsync(editor.SavedSpeciesId)
            End If
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Async Sub btnEdit_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnEdit.Click
        Await EditSpeciesAsync(SelectedSpecies())
    End Sub

    Private Async Function EditSpeciesAsync(species As TreeSpecies) As Task
        If species Is Nothing OrElse Not EnsureSessionActive() Then
            Return
        End If

        ' The editor works on a copy, so Cancel leaves the list unchanged.
        Using editor As New SpeciesEditorForm(_services.Species, _categories, species.Clone(), CurrentUser)
            If editor.ShowDialog(Me) = System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK Then
                _dataChanged = True
                Await LoadSpeciesAsync(editor.SavedSpeciesId)
            End If
        End Using
    End Function

    Private Async Sub btnDelete_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnDelete.Click
        Await DeleteSpeciesAsync(SelectedSpecies())
    End Sub

    Private Async Function DeleteSpeciesAsync(species As TreeSpecies) As Task
        If species Is Nothing OrElse Not EnsureSessionActive() Then
            Return
        End If

        Try
            Dim scenarioCount As Integer = Await _services.Species.CountScenariosAsync(species.SpeciesId)
            Dim message As String = "Delete """ & species.CommonName & """?"
            If scenarioCount > 0 Then
                message &= vbCrLf & vbCrLf & String.Format("{0} saved scenario{1} that use{2} this species will also be deleted.",
                                                           scenarioCount, If(scenarioCount = 1, String.Empty, "s"),
                                                           If(scenarioCount = 1, "s", String.Empty))
            End If

            message &= vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "This cannot be undone."
            If Not ErrorReporter.Confirm(Me, message, "Delete Species") Then
                Return
            End If

            SetBusy(True)
            Await _services.Species.DeleteAsync(species, CurrentUser)
            _dataChanged = True
            Await LoadSpeciesAsync(Nothing)
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrorReporter.ShowError(Me, "delete the species", ex)
        Finally
            SetBusy(False)
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Async Sub btnRefresh_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnRefresh.Click
        Await LoadSpeciesAsync(SelectedSpeciesId())
        If pnlAudit.Visible Then
            Await LoadAuditAsync()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Async Sub btnResetData_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnResetData.Click
        If Not EnsureSessionActive() OrElse Not ErrorReporter.Confirm(Me,
            "Restore the original sample data?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf &
            "All species records - including ones you added or edited - and ALL saved scenarios will be deleted, " &
            "then the sample species are inserted again. User accounts and the audit log are kept.",
            "Reset Sample Data") Then
            Return
        End If

        SetBusy(True)
        Try
            Await _services.Species.ResetSampleDataAsync(CurrentUser)
            _dataChanged = True
            Await LoadSpeciesAsync(Nothing)
            ErrorReporter.ShowInfo(Me, "The sample data was restored.")
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrorReporter.ShowError(Me, "restore the sample data", ex)
        Finally
            SetBusy(False)
        End Try
    End Sub

    ' ------------------------------------------------------------------
    ' Audit log, password, sign out
    ' ------------------------------------------------------------------

    Private Async Sub SectionChip_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles chipSpecies.Click, chipAudit.Click
        Dim showAudit As Boolean = sender Is chipAudit
        chipSpecies.Selected = Not showAudit
        chipAudit.Selected = showAudit
        pnlSpecies.Visible = Not showAudit
        pnlAudit.Visible = showAudit

        If showAudit Then
            Await LoadAuditAsync()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Async Function LoadAuditAsync() As Task
        Try
            Dim entries As List(Of AuditEntry) = Await _services.Species.GetAuditLogAsync(AuditEntriesToShow)
            gridAudit.Rows.Clear()
            For Each entry As AuditEntry In entries
                Dim record As String = String.Empty
                If entry.EntityName IsNot Nothing Then
                    record = entry.EntityName & If(entry.EntityId.HasValue, " #" & entry.EntityId.Value, String.Empty)
                End If

                gridAudit.Rows.Add(Formatters.LocalDateTime(entry.OccurredAtUtc), If(entry.Username, "(unknown)"),
                                   entry.Action, record, entry.Details)
            Next
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrorReporter.ShowError(Me, "load the audit log", ex)
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Sub btnChangePassword_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnChangePassword.Click
        If Not EnsureSessionActive() Then
            Return
        End If

        Using dialog As New ChangePasswordForm(_services.Auth, CurrentUser, False)
            dialog.ShowDialog(Me)
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Async Sub btnSignOut_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnSignOut.Click
        Dim user As AppUser = CurrentUser
        _services.Session.SignOut()
        Try
            Await _services.Auth.LogoutAsync(user)
        Finally
            Close()
        End Try
    End Sub

    Private Sub SetBusy(busy As Boolean)
        pnlContent.Enabled = Not busy
        flowFooterLeft.Enabled = Not busy
        UseWaitCursor = busy
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Keyboard shortcuts: Ctrl+N adds a species, F5 refreshes.</summary>
    Protected Overrides Function ProcessCmdKey(ByRef msg As Message, keyData As Keys) As Boolean
        If keyData = (Keys.Control Or Keys.N) Then
            btnAdd.PerformClick()
            Return True
        End If

        If keyData = Keys.F5 Then
            btnRefresh.PerformClick()
            Return True
        End If

        Return MyBase.ProcessCmdKey(msg, keyData)
    End Function

End Class

Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services

''' <summary>
''' Lets the signed-in administrator choose a new password. In "forced" mode (first
''' login with the default password) the dialog explains why it appeared.
''' </summary>
Public Class ChangePasswordForm

    Private ReadOnly _auth As AuthService
    Private ReadOnly _user As AppUser

    ''' <summary>Creates the dialog.</summary>
    ''' <param name="auth">Authentication service.</param>
    ''' <param name="user">The signed-in user.</param>
    ''' <param name="forced">True when the password must be changed before continuing.</param>
    Public Sub New(auth As AuthService, user As AppUser, forced As Boolean)
        If auth Is Nothing Then
            Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(auth))
        End If

        If user Is Nothing Then
            Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(user))
        End If

        _auth = auth
        _user = user

        ' This call is required by the designer.
        InitializeComponent()

        lblPolicy.Text = PasswordPolicy.Description
        If forced Then
            lblIntro.Text = "For security, replace the default password before managing records. " &
                            "Type the current (default) password, then your new password twice."
            btnCancel.Text = "Sign Out"
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Async Sub btnSave_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnSave.Click
        lblError.Text = String.Empty
        SetBusy(True)
        Try
            Dim errorMessage As String = Await _auth.ChangePasswordAsync(_user, txtCurrent.Text, txtNew.Text, txtConfirm.Text)
            If errorMessage Is Nothing Then
                ErrorReporter.ShowInfo(Me, "Your password was changed. Use the new password the next time you sign in.")
                Me.DialogResult = System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK
                Return
            End If

            lblError.Text = errorMessage
            txtNew.Clear()
            txtConfirm.Clear()
            If errorMessage.StartsWith("The current", StringComparison.Ordinal) Then
                txtCurrent.SelectAll()
                txtCurrent.Focus()
            Else
                txtNew.Focus()
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrorReporter.ShowError(Me, "change the password", ex)
        Finally
            SetBusy(False)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Private Sub SetBusy(busy As Boolean)
        txtCurrent.Enabled = Not busy
        txtNew.Enabled = Not busy
        txtConfirm.Enabled = Not busy
        btnSave.Enabled = Not busy
        UseWaitCursor = busy
    End Sub

End Class

FILE 17 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\Forms\LoginForm.vb

Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services

''' <summary>Administrator sign-in dialog. Returns OK and <see cref="SignedInUser"/> on success.</summary>
Public Class LoginForm

    Private ReadOnly _auth As AuthService
    Private _signedInUser As AppUser

    ''' <summary>Creates the dialog.</summary>
    Public Sub New(auth As AuthService)
        If auth Is Nothing Then
            Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(auth))
        End If

        _auth = auth

        ' This call is required by the designer.
        InitializeComponent()
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>The administrator who signed in (set when the dialog returns OK).</summary>
    Public ReadOnly Property SignedInUser As AppUser
        Get
            Return _signedInUser
        End Get
    End Property

    Private Async Sub btnSignIn_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnSignIn.Click
        lblError.Text = String.Empty
        SetBusy(True)
        Try
            Dim result As LoginResult = Await _auth.LoginAsync(txtUsername.Text, txtPassword.Text)
            If result.Succeeded Then
                _signedInUser = result.User
                Me.DialogResult = System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK ' closes the dialog
                Return
            End If

            lblError.Text = result.Message
            txtPassword.Clear()
            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtUsername.Text) Then
                txtUsername.Focus()
            Else
                txtPassword.Focus()
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrorReporter.ShowError(Me, "sign in", ex)
        Finally
            SetBusy(False)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Private Sub SetBusy(busy As Boolean)
        txtUsername.Enabled = Not busy
        txtPassword.Enabled = Not busy
        btnSignIn.Enabled = Not busy
        btnSignIn.Text = If(busy, "Signing in...", "Sign In")
        UseWaitCursor = busy
    End Sub

    Private Sub chkShowPassword_CheckedChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles chkShowPassword.CheckedChanged
        txtPassword.UseSystemPasswordChar = Not chkShowPassword.Checked
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtPassword_Enter(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles txtPassword.Enter
        UpdateCapsLockWarning()
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtPassword_KeyUp(sender As Object, e As KeyEventArgs) Handles txtPassword.KeyUp
        UpdateCapsLockWarning()
    End Sub

    Private Sub UpdateCapsLockWarning()
        ' Passwords are case-sensitive; warning about Caps Lock prevents needless lockouts.
        lblCapsLock.Visible = Control.IsKeyLocked(Keys.CapsLock)
    End Sub

End Class
