FILE 39 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\UI\NativeMethods.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Runtime.InteropServices

''' <summary>Small Windows API calls that .NET Framework 4.5.2 does not wrap.</summary>
Friend NotInheritable Class NativeMethods

    ' Edit-control message that shows gray "placeholder" text in an empty TextBox.
    Private Const EM_SETCUEBANNER As Integer = &H1501

    Private Sub New()
    End Sub

    <DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet:=CharSet.Unicode)>
    Private Shared Function SendMessage(hWnd As IntPtr, msg As Integer, wParam As IntPtr, lParam As String) As IntPtr
    End Function

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Shows placeholder text in a single-line TextBox while it is empty
    ''' (like the HTML placeholder attribute). Works once the handle exists.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Shared Sub SetCueBanner(textBox As TextBox, text As String)
        If textBox.IsHandleCreated Then
            ' wParam = 1 keeps the hint visible even while the box has focus.
            SendMessage(textBox.Handle, EM_SETCUEBANNER, New IntPtr(1), text)
        Else
            AddHandler textBox.HandleCreated, Sub(sender, e) SetCueBanner(textBox, text)
        End If
    End Sub

End Class


====================================================================================================
FILE 40 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\Interactive Tree Database\UI\ReportPrinter.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Drawing.Printing
Imports System.Globalization
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services

''' <summary>
''' Draws the printable projection report with System.Drawing.Printing and shows it
''' in a print preview window (from there it can be printed or saved as PDF with
''' "Microsoft Print to PDF"). Long tables continue on extra pages.
''' </summary>
Friend NotInheritable Class ReportPrinter
    Implements IDisposable

    ' Relative widths of the projection table columns.
    Private Shared ReadOnly ColumnWeights As Single() = {0.1F, 0.18F, 0.22F, 0.25F, 0.25F}

    Private WithEvents _document As New PrintDocument()
    Private ReadOnly _result As YieldResult
    Private ReadOnly _projection As YieldProjection
    Private _nextRow As Integer
    Private _pageNumber As Integer

    ''' <summary>Prepares a report for a calculation and its projection.</summary>
    Public Sub New(result As YieldResult, projection As YieldProjection)
        If result Is Nothing Then
            Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(result))
        End If

        If projection Is Nothing Then
            Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(projection))
        End If

        _result = result
        _projection = projection
        _document.DocumentName = "Tree Yield Report - " & result.Species.CommonName
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Opens the print preview window.</summary>
    ''' <exception cref="InvalidPrinterException">No printer is installed.</exception>
    Public Sub ShowPreview(owner As IWin32Window)
        Using preview As New PrintPreviewDialog()
            preview.Document = _document
            preview.Icon = DrawingHelpers.AppIcon
            preview.UseAntiAlias = True
            preview.Width = 900
            preview.Height = 1000
            preview.Text = "Print Preview - " & _document.DocumentName
            preview.ShowDialog(owner)
        End Using
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Releases the print document.</summary>
    Public Sub Dispose() Implements IDisposable.Dispose
        _document.Dispose()
    End Sub

    Private Sub Document_BeginPrint(sender As Object, e As PrintEventArgs) Handles _document.BeginPrint
        ' Printing can run more than once (preview, then print), so start over each time.
        _nextRow = 0
        _pageNumber = 0
    End Sub

    Private Sub Document_PrintPage(sender As Object, e As PrintPageEventArgs) Handles _document.PrintPage
        _pageNumber += 1
        Dim g As Graphics = e.Graphics
        Dim area As Rectangle = e.MarginBounds
        Dim y As Single = area.Top

        Using titleFont As New Font(Theme.FontFamily, 18.0F, FontStyle.Bold),
              headingFont As New Font(Theme.SemiboldFontFamily, 11.0F),
              bodyFont As New Font(Theme.FontFamily, 10.0F),
              boldFont As New Font(Theme.SemiboldFontFamily, 10.0F),
              smallFont As New Font(Theme.FontFamily, 8.5F),
              textBrush As New SolidBrush(Theme.TextPrimary),
              mutedBrush As New SolidBrush(Theme.TextSecondary),
              linePen As New Pen(Theme.Border)

            Dim s As TreeSpecies = _result.Species

            If _pageNumber = 1 Then
                g.DrawString("Tree Yield & Revenue Projection Report", titleFont, textBrush, area.Left, y)
                y += titleFont.GetHeight(g) + 2
                g.DrawString("Generated " & DateTime.Now.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy h:mm tt", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                             smallFont, mutedBrush, area.Left, y)
                y += smallFont.GetHeight(g) + 14

                y = DrawHeading(g, "Species", headingFont, textBrush, linePen, area, y)
                Dim rotation As Boolean = s.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation
                Dim harvestText As String
                If rotation Then
                    harvestText = "Rotation - cut once at " & Formatters.Years(s.YearsToFirstHarvest)
                Else
                    harvestText = "Annual - first harvest in year " & Math.Max(1, s.YearsToFirstHarvest) &
                                  ", full production from year " & Math.Max(1, s.YearsToFullProduction)
                End If

                Dim details As New List(Of KeyValuePair(Of String, String)) From {
                    Pair("Species", s.CommonName & If(String.IsNullOrEmpty(s.ScientificName), String.Empty, " (" & s.ScientificName & ")")),
                    Pair("Category / product", s.CategoryName & " / " & s.ProductType),
                    Pair("Yield per tree per year", Formatters.Quantity(s.YieldPerTreePerYear, s.YieldUnit) & If(rotation, " (mean annual increment)", String.Empty)),
                    Pair("Price per unit", Formatters.PesoWithCentavos(s.PricePerUnit) & " per " & s.YieldUnit),
                    Pair("Harvest type", harvestText)
                }
                y = DrawPairs(g, details, bodyFont, boldFont, mutedBrush, textBrush, area, y)

                y += 8
                y = DrawHeading(g, "Results for " & _result.TreeCount.ToString("N0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) & " trees",
                                headingFont, textBrush, linePen, area, y)
                Dim results As New List(Of KeyValuePair(Of String, String)) From {
                    Pair(If(rotation, "Annualized yield", "Annual yield (full production)"), Formatters.Quantity(_result.AnnualYield, s.YieldUnit)),
                    Pair(If(rotation, "Annualized gross sales", "Estimated annual gross sales"), Formatters.Peso(_result.GrossSales)),
                    Pair(_projection.Years & "-year total", Formatters.Quantity(_projection.TotalYield, s.YieldUnit) & "  /  " & Formatters.Peso(_projection.TotalSales))
                }
                If rotation Then
                    results.Add(Pair("Harvest at year " & _result.HarvestYear,
                                     Formatters.Quantity(_result.HarvestYield, s.YieldUnit) & "  /  " & Formatters.Peso(_result.HarvestSales)))
                End If

                y = DrawPairs(g, results, bodyFont, boldFont, mutedBrush, textBrush, area, y)
                y += 8
                y = DrawHeading(g, _projection.Years & "-year projection", headingFont, textBrush, linePen, area, y)
            Else
                g.DrawString(_projection.Years & "-year projection (continued) - " & s.CommonName, headingFont, textBrush, area.Left, y)
                y += headingFont.GetHeight(g) + 8
            End If

            y = DrawTable(g, bodyFont, boldFont, textBrush, linePen, area, y)

            Dim finished As Boolean = _nextRow >= _projection.Rows.Count
            Dim footerTop As Single = area.Bottom - smallFont.GetHeight(g) * 3

            If finished Then
                Dim disclaimerBox As New RectangleF(area.Left, Math.Max(y + 12, footerTop - 34), area.Width, 40)
                g.DrawString(YieldCalculator.EstimateDisclaimer, smallFont, mutedBrush, disclaimerBox)
            End If

            g.DrawString("Page " & _pageNumber, smallFont, mutedBrush, area.Right - 60, area.Bottom + 10)
            e.HasMorePages = Not finished
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Function DrawTable(g As Graphics, bodyFont As Font, boldFont As Font, textBrush As Brush, linePen As Pen,
                               area As Rectangle, y As Single) As Single
        Dim unit As String = _result.Species.YieldUnit
        Dim headers As String() = {"Year", "Production", "Yield (" & unit & ")", "Gross sales", "Cumulative sales"}
        Dim rowHeight As Single = bodyFont.GetHeight(g) + 8
        Dim bottomLimit As Single = area.Bottom - 70

        DrawRow(g, headers, boldFont, textBrush, area, y, rowHeight)
        y += rowHeight
        g.DrawLine(linePen, area.Left, y, area.Right, y)

        While _nextRow < _projection.Rows.Count AndAlso y + rowHeight <= bottomLimit
            Dim row As ProjectionYear = _projection.Rows(_nextRow)
            Dim cells As String() = {
                row.Year.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                Formatters.ProductionStage(row, _result.Species.HarvestType),
                Formatters.Quantity(row.Yield),
                Formatters.Peso(row.GrossSales),
                Formatters.Peso(row.CumulativeSales)
            }
            DrawRow(g, cells, bodyFont, textBrush, area, y, rowHeight)
            y += rowHeight
            g.DrawLine(linePen, area.Left, y, area.Right, y)
            _nextRow += 1
        End While

        Return y
    End Function

    Private Shared Sub DrawRow(g As Graphics, cells As String(), font As Font, brush As Brush, area As Rectangle,
                               y As Single, height As Single)
        Dim x As Single = area.Left
        For i As Integer = 0 To cells.Length - 1
            Dim width As Single = area.Width * ColumnWeights(i)
            Using format As New StringFormat()
                ' Numbers are right-aligned so digits line up; the first two columns are text.
                format.Alignment = If(i >= 2, StringAlignment.Far, StringAlignment.Near)
                format.LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center
                format.Trimming = StringTrimming.EllipsisCharacter
                format.FormatFlags = StringFormatFlags.NoWrap
                g.DrawString(cells(i), font, brush, New RectangleF(x + 4, y, width - 8, height), format)
            End Using

            x += width
        Next
    End Sub

    Private Shared Function DrawHeading(g As Graphics, text As String, font As Font, brush As Brush, pen As Pen,
                                        area As Rectangle, y As Single) As Single
        g.DrawString(text, font, brush, area.Left, y)
        y += font.GetHeight(g) + 3
        g.DrawLine(pen, area.Left, y, area.Right, y)
        Return y + 6
    End Function

    Private Shared Function DrawPairs(g As Graphics, pairs As IEnumerable(Of KeyValuePair(Of String, String)), labelFont As Font,
                                      valueFont As Font, labelBrush As Brush, valueBrush As Brush, area As Rectangle,
                                      y As Single) As Single
        Dim labelWidth As Single = area.Width * 0.34F
        For Each pair As KeyValuePair(Of String, String) In pairs
            Dim valueBox As New RectangleF(area.Left + labelWidth, y, area.Width - labelWidth, 200)
            Dim valueSize As SizeF = g.MeasureString(pair.Value, valueFont, CInt(valueBox.Width))
            g.DrawString(pair.Key, labelFont, labelBrush, area.Left, y)
            g.DrawString(pair.Value, valueFont, valueBrush, valueBox)
            y += Math.Max(labelFont.GetHeight(g), valueSize.Height) + 4
        Next

        Return y
    End Function

    Private Shared Function Pair(key As String, value As String) As KeyValuePair(Of String, String)
        Return New KeyValuePair(Of String, String)(key, If(value, String.Empty))
    End Function

End Class

FILE 42 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\AccessConnectionFactory.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports System.IO

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Creates connections to the Microsoft Access database file (.mdb) through OLE DB.
    ''' Connections are opened late and closed early (inside Using blocks); OLE DB
    ''' session pooling makes re-opening them cheap.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class AccessConnectionFactory

        ''' <summary>
        ''' The Jet 4.0 engine reads and writes Access .mdb files. It is part of every
        ''' Windows installation, but only for 32-bit programs - which is why the
        ''' application and the test runner are compiled for x86.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Const DefaultProvider As String = "Microsoft.Jet.OLEDB.4.0"

        ''' <summary>Creates the factory.</summary>
        ''' <param name="databasePath">Full or relative path of the .mdb file.</param>
        ''' <param name="provider">OLE DB provider; Nothing or blank means <see cref="DefaultProvider"/>.</param>
        Public Sub New(databasePath As String, Optional provider As String = DefaultProvider)
            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(databasePath) Then
                Throw New ArgumentException("A database file path is required.", NameOf(databasePath))
            End If

            Me.DatabasePath = Path.GetFullPath(databasePath)
            Me.Provider = If(String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(provider), DefaultProvider, provider.Trim())

            Dim builder As New OleDbConnectionStringBuilder()
            builder.Provider = Me.Provider
            builder.DataSource = Me.DatabasePath
            ConnectionString = builder.ConnectionString
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Full path of the .mdb file.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property DatabasePath As String

        ''' <summary>OLE DB provider name, for example "Microsoft.Jet.OLEDB.4.0".</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Provider As String

        ''' <summary>The OLE DB connection string built from the path and provider.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property ConnectionString As String

        ''' <summary>File name only, for example "TreeYieldDb.mdb" (shown in the status bar).</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property DatabaseFileName As String
            Get
                Return Path.GetFileName(DatabasePath)
            End Get
        End Property

        ''' <summary>Opens a connection to the database file.</summary>
        ''' <exception cref="DataAccessException">The Jet provider is not available to this process.</exception>
        Public Function Open() As OleDbConnection
            Dim connection As New OleDbConnection(ConnectionString)
            Try
                connection.Open()
                Return connection
            Catch ex As InvalidOperationException When ex.Message.IndexOf("not registered", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0
                ' Happens when the program runs as 64-bit: Jet 4.0 has no 64-bit version.
                connection.Dispose()
                Throw New DataAccessException(String.Format(
                    "The database engine '{0}' is not available to this program. Jet 4.0 works only in 32-bit " &
                    "programs, so the project's Target CPU must be x86 (Project Properties > Compile).", Provider), ex)
            Catch
                ' Do not leak the connection object if opening fails.
                connection.Dispose()
                Throw
            End Try
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 43 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\AccessErrorTranslator.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Converts Microsoft Access (Jet) error numbers into friendly
    ''' <see cref="DataAccessException"/>s, so users never see raw database messages.
    ''' Jet reports its error number in <see cref="OleDbError.SQLState"/>.
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class AccessErrorTranslator

        ''' <summary>A UNIQUE index or primary key would get a duplicate value.</summary>
        Public Const DuplicateValue As String = "3022"

        ''' <summary>A CHECK constraint (Access "validation rule") was broken.</summary>
        Public Const ValidationRuleBroken As String = "3316"

        ''' <summary>A field-level validation rule was broken.</summary>
        Public Const FieldValidationRuleBroken As String = "3317"

        ''' <summary>A foreign key points to a record that does not exist.</summary>
        Public Const RelatedRecordRequired As String = "3201"

        ''' <summary>A record cannot be deleted because other records refer to it.</summary>
        Public Const RelatedRecordsExist As String = "3200"

        ''' <summary>The file is open exclusively in another program.</summary>
        Public Const FileInUse As String = "3045"

        ''' <summary>The database file does not exist.</summary>
        Public Const FileNotFound As String = "3024"

        Private Const FileInUseHelp As String =
            "The database file is in use by another program (for example Microsoft Access opened in exclusive " &
            "mode, or a table open in Design View). Close it there and try again."

        Private Const ReadOnlyHelp As String =
            "The database file cannot be changed. Make sure TreeYieldDb.mdb is not marked read-only and that " &
            "its folder can be written to."

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns the exception that should be thrown instead of <paramref name="exception"/>.</summary>
        ''' <param name="operation">What was being done, for example "load the species list".</param>
        ''' <param name="exception">The original OLE DB exception.</param>
        Public Shared Function Translate(operation As String, exception As OleDbException) As DataAccessException
            Dim code As String = String.Empty
            Dim engineMessage As String = exception.Message
            If exception.Errors.Count > 0 Then
                code = exception.Errors(0).SQLState
                engineMessage = exception.Errors(0).Message
            End If

            Return FromCode(operation, code, engineMessage, exception)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Maps one Jet error number to the exception to throw. Separate from
        ''' <see cref="Translate"/> so it can be unit-tested without a real database error.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared Function FromCode(operation As String, code As String, engineMessage As String, inner As Exception) As DataAccessException
            Select Case code
                Case DuplicateValue
                    Return New DuplicateRecordException("A record with the same name already exists.", inner)

                Case ValidationRuleBroken, FieldValidationRuleBroken
                    Return New DataAccessException(
                        "The database rejected the change because a value breaks one of its rules " &
                        "(for example a negative price or a missing name).", inner)

                Case RelatedRecordRequired
                    Return New DataAccessException(
                        "The database rejected the change because a related record (such as the category) does not exist.", inner)

                Case RelatedRecordsExist
                    Return New DataAccessException("This record cannot be deleted because other records still use it.", inner)

                Case FileInUse, "3006", "3008", "3051", "3356"
                    Return New DataAccessException(FileInUseHelp, inner)

                Case "3188", "3218", "3260"
                    Return New DataAccessException(
                        "The record is locked because it is being edited somewhere else (for example in Microsoft Access). " &
                        "Finish or cancel that edit and try again.", inner)

                Case FileNotFound, "3044"
                    Return New DataAccessException("The database file could not be found. Check the DatabaseFile setting in the program's .config file.", inner)
            End Select

            If engineMessage IsNot Nothing AndAlso engineMessage.IndexOf("updateable", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 Then
                Return New DataAccessException(ReadOnlyHelp, inner)
            End If

            Return New DataAccessException(String.Format(
                "A database error occurred while trying to {0}. (Access error {1})", operation, code), inner)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 44 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\AuditLogRepository.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports System.Globalization
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>Writes and reads the AuditLog table (history of administrator actions).</summary>
    Public Class AuditLogRepository
        Inherits RepositoryBase

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of the Details text.</summary>
        Public Const MaxDetailsLength As Integer = 500

        ''' <summary>Largest number of entries <see cref="GetRecentAsync"/> returns.</summary>
        Public Const MaxEntries As Integer = 1000

        ''' <summary>Creates the repository.</summary>
        Public Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            MyBase.New(connectionFactory)
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Adds one audit entry.</summary>
        ''' <param name="userId">Who did it (Nothing if unknown).</param>
        ''' <param name="action">Action code from <see cref="Services.AuditActions"/>.</param>
        ''' <param name="entityName">Kind of record, for example "TreeSpecies".</param>
        ''' <param name="entityId">ID of the record, if any.</param>
        ''' <param name="details">Description; cut to <see cref="MaxDetailsLength"/> characters.</param>
        Public Function AddAsync(userId As Integer?, action As String, entityName As String, entityId As Integer?, details As String) As Task
            Const sql As String =
                "INSERT INTO AuditLog (UserId, ActionCode, EntityName, EntityId, Details, OccurredAt) " &
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"

            Dim safeDetails As String = If(details IsNot Nothing AndAlso details.Length > MaxDetailsLength,
                                           details.Substring(0, MaxDetailsLength), details)

            Return ExecuteAsync("write the audit log", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddInteger(command, userId)
                    AddText(command, action, 30)
                    AddText(command, entityName, 50)
                    AddInteger(command, entityId)
                    AddMemo(command, safeDetails)
                    AddDate(command, UtcNow())
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns the most recent entries, newest first.</summary>
        ''' <param name="count">How many entries to return (1 to <see cref="MaxEntries"/>).</param>
        Public Function GetRecentAsync(count As Integer) As Task(Of List(Of AuditEntry))
            ' Access does not accept a parameter after TOP, so the number is written into
            ' the SQL - safely, because it is an Integer limited to 1..1000, not user text.
            Dim top As Integer = Math.Max(1, Math.Min(MaxEntries, count))
            Dim sql As String =
                "SELECT TOP " & top.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) & " a.AuditId, a.UserId, u.Username, " &
                "a.ActionCode, a.EntityName, a.EntityId, a.Details, a.OccurredAt " &
                "FROM AuditLog AS a LEFT JOIN Users AS u ON a.UserId = u.UserId " &
                "ORDER BY a.OccurredAt DESC, a.AuditId DESC"

            Return QueryAsync("load the audit log", Function(connection)
                Dim entries As New List(Of AuditEntry)()
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                        While reader.Read()
                            entries.Add(New AuditEntry With {
                                .AuditId = ReadInteger(reader, "AuditId"),
                                .UserId = ReadNullableInteger(reader, "UserId"),
                                .Username = ReadString(reader, "Username"),
                                .Action = ReadString(reader, "ActionCode"),
                                .EntityName = ReadString(reader, "EntityName"),
                                .EntityId = ReadNullableInteger(reader, "EntityId"),
                                .Details = ReadString(reader, "Details"),
                                .OccurredAtUtc = ReadDate(reader, "OccurredAt")
                            })
                        End While
                    End Using
                End Using

                Return entries
            End Function)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>Reads the Categories table.</summary>
    Public Class CategoryRepository
        Inherits RepositoryBase

        ''' <summary>Creates the repository.</summary>
        Public Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            MyBase.New(connectionFactory)
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns all categories in ID order.</summary>
        Public Function GetAllAsync() As Task(Of List(Of Category))
            Return QueryAsync("load the categories", Function(connection)
                Dim categories As New List(Of Category)()
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection,
                    "SELECT CategoryId, CategoryName, Description FROM Categories ORDER BY CategoryId")
                    Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                        While reader.Read()
                            categories.Add(New Category With {
                                .CategoryId = ReadInteger(reader, "CategoryId"),
                                .Name = ReadString(reader, "CategoryName"),
                                .Description = ReadString(reader, "Description")
                            })
                        End While
                    End Using
                End Using

                Return categories
            End Function)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 47 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\DatabaseFileLocator.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.IO

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Decides which .mdb file the program uses.
    ''' <para>
    ''' When the program runs from Visual Studio, the .exe is in bin\Debug, but the
    ''' database lives in the project's Database folder. Searching upward from the
    ''' program's folder finds that project copy, so the file you open in Microsoft
    ''' Access and the file the program changes are the same file.
    ''' </para>
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class DatabaseFileLocator

        ''' <summary>How many parent folders are searched (bin\Debug -> bin -> project folder).</summary>
        Public Const MaxLevelsUp As Integer = 3

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Returns the database path to use.
        ''' <list type="number">
        ''' <item>An absolute <paramref name="relativePath"/> is used as it is.</item>
        ''' <item>Otherwise the program folder and up to <see cref="MaxLevelsUp"/> parent folders are searched for an existing file.</item>
        ''' <item>If none exists, the path where a new database should be created is returned: the project folder
        ''' when running from bin\Debug or bin\Release, else the program folder.</item>
        ''' </list>
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="programFolder">Folder of the running .exe.</param>
        ''' <param name="relativePath">For example "Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb".</param>
        Public Shared Function Locate(programFolder As String, relativePath As String) As String
            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(relativePath) Then
                Throw New ArgumentException("A database file name is required.", NameOf(relativePath))
            End If

            If Path.IsPathRooted(relativePath) Then
                Return relativePath
            End If

            Dim folder As DirectoryInfo = New DirectoryInfo(programFolder)
            For level As Integer = 0 To MaxLevelsUp
                If folder Is Nothing Then
                    Exit For
                End If

                Dim candidate As String = Path.Combine(folder.FullName, relativePath)
                If File.Exists(candidate) Then
                    Return candidate
                End If

                folder = folder.Parent
            Next

            Return Path.Combine(DefaultFolder(programFolder), relativePath)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>The project folder when the program runs from ...\bin\Debug or ...\bin\Release; otherwise the program folder.</summary>
        Private Shared Function DefaultFolder(programFolder As String) As String
            Dim folder As New DirectoryInfo(programFolder)
            If folder.Parent IsNot Nothing AndAlso folder.Parent.Parent IsNot Nothing AndAlso
               String.Equals(folder.Parent.Name, "bin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) Then
                Return folder.Parent.Parent.FullName
            End If

            Return folder.FullName
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 48 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\DatabaseInitializer.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports System.IO
Imports System.Reflection
Imports System.Runtime.InteropServices
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Makes sure the Access database file exists and has the tables and sample data.
    ''' <para>
    ''' The project ships a ready-made TreeYieldDb.mdb, so normally there is nothing to do.
    ''' If the file is missing (for example deleted to start over, or a new PC), a new
    ''' .mdb is created with ADOX, the tables are created from the embedded Schema.sql,
    ''' and the sample data is inserted.
    ''' </para>
    ''' It is safe to call <see cref="InitializeAsync"/> every time the program starts.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class DatabaseInitializer
        Inherits RepositoryBase

        ''' <summary>Schema version this build of the program expects.</summary>
        Public Const CurrentSchemaVersion As Integer = 1

        ''' <summary>Embedded script that creates the tables.</summary>
        Public Const SchemaScriptName As String = "Schema.sql"

        ''' <summary>Creates the initializer.</summary>
        Public Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            MyBase.New(connectionFactory)
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Runs <see cref="Initialize"/> on a background thread.</summary>
        Public Function InitializeAsync() As Task
            Return Task.Run(AddressOf Initialize)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Creates the file, tables and sample data if they are missing. The sample data
        ''' is inserted in one transaction. Access cannot roll back CREATE TABLE, so a file
        ''' that this call created and could not finish is deleted again; the next start
        ''' then simply tries again from the beginning.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Sub Initialize()
            Dim createdNow As Boolean = False
            Try
                If Not File.Exists(ConnectionFactory.DatabasePath) Then
                    CreateDatabaseFile()
                    createdNow = True
                End If

                Using connection As OleDbConnection = ConnectionFactory.Open()
                    If TableExists(connection, "SchemaVersion") Then
                        Return
                    End If

                    SqlScriptRunner.Execute(connection, Nothing, SqlScriptRunner.LoadEmbeddedScript(SchemaScriptName))

                    Using transaction As OleDbTransaction = connection.BeginTransaction()
                        InsertMissingCategories(connection, transaction)
                        InsertSampleSpecies(connection, transaction)

                        Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection,
                            "INSERT INTO SchemaVersion (VersionNumber, AppliedAt) VALUES (?, ?)", transaction)
                            AddInteger(command, CurrentSchemaVersion)
                            AddDate(command, UtcNow())
                            command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                        End Using

                        transaction.Commit()
                    End Using
                End Using
            Catch ex As OleDbException
                If createdNow Then
                    DeleteDatabaseFile()
                End If

                Throw AccessErrorTranslator.Translate("prepare the database", ex)
            Catch ex As Exception When createdNow
                DeleteDatabaseFile()
                Throw
            End Try
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Deletes all species and saved scenarios and inserts the original sample species
        ''' again. User accounts and the audit log are kept.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Function ResetSampleDataAsync() As Task
            Return ExecuteAsync("restore the sample data", Sub(connection)
                Using transaction As OleDbTransaction = connection.BeginTransaction()
                    For Each sql As String In {"DELETE FROM Scenarios", "DELETE FROM TreeSpecies"}
                        Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql, transaction)
                            command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                        End Using
                    Next

                    InsertMissingCategories(connection, transaction)
                    InsertSampleSpecies(connection, transaction)
                    transaction.Commit()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Creates an empty .mdb file with ADOX, the "ActiveX Data Objects Extensions" that
        ''' come with Windows. It is used through late binding (by name), so the project
        ''' needs no COM reference.
        ''' </summary>
        Private Sub CreateDatabaseFile()
            Dim folder As String = Path.GetDirectoryName(ConnectionFactory.DatabasePath)
            Directory.CreateDirectory(folder)

            Dim catalogType As Type = Type.GetTypeFromProgID("ADOX.Catalog")
            If catalogType Is Nothing Then
                Throw New DataAccessException("A new database file cannot be created because ADOX is not available on this PC.", Nothing)
            End If

            Dim catalog As Object = Activator.CreateInstance(catalogType)
            Try
                ' Engine Type 5 = the Jet 4.0 file format (Access 2000-2003 .mdb).
                Dim createString As String = ConnectionFactory.ConnectionString & ";Jet OLEDB:Engine Type=5"
                catalogType.InvokeMember("Create", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, Nothing, catalog, New Object() {createString})

                ' Close the connection ADOX keeps open, otherwise the new file stays locked.
                Dim activeConnection As Object = catalogType.InvokeMember("ActiveConnection", BindingFlags.GetProperty, Nothing, catalog, Nothing)
                activeConnection.GetType().InvokeMember("Close", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, Nothing, activeConnection, Nothing)
                Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(activeConnection)
            Catch ex As TargetInvocationException
                Dim reason As Exception = If(ex.InnerException, ex)
                Throw New DataAccessException("A new database file could not be created at " &
                    ConnectionFactory.DatabasePath & ". " & reason.Message, reason)
            Finally
                Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(catalog)
            End Try
        End Sub

        Private Sub DeleteDatabaseFile()
            Try
                ' Pooled connections keep the file open; release them first.
                OleDbConnection.ReleaseObjectPool()
                File.Delete(ConnectionFactory.DatabasePath)
            Catch ex As IOException
                Trace.TraceWarning("Could not delete the unfinished database file: " & ex.Message)
            Catch ex As UnauthorizedAccessException
                Trace.TraceWarning("Could not delete the unfinished database file: " & ex.Message)
            End Try
        End Sub

        Private Shared Function TableExists(connection As OleDbConnection, tableName As String) As Boolean
            ' Restrictions: catalog, schema, table name, table type.
            Dim tables As DataTable = connection.GetOleDbSchemaTable(OleDbSchemaGuid.Tables,
                New Object() {Nothing, Nothing, tableName, "TABLE"})
            Return tables IsNot Nothing AndAlso tables.Rows.Count > 0
        End Function

        Private Shared Sub InsertMissingCategories(connection As OleDbConnection, transaction As OleDbTransaction)
            For Each category As Category In SeedData.Categories
                Using check As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Categories WHERE CategoryName = ?", transaction)
                    AddText(check, category.Name, 50)
                    If Convert.ToInt32(check.ExecuteScalar()) > 0 Then
                        Continue For
                    End If
                End Using

                Using insert As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection,
                    "INSERT INTO Categories (CategoryName, Description) VALUES (?, ?)", transaction)
                    AddText(insert, category.Name, 50)
                    AddText(insert, category.Description, 200)
                    insert.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            Next
        End Sub

        Private Shared Sub InsertSampleSpecies(connection As OleDbConnection, transaction As OleDbTransaction)
            ' The AutoNumber IDs are only known after the categories are inserted, so look them up by name.
            Dim categoryIds As New Dictionary(Of String, Integer)(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "SELECT CategoryId, CategoryName FROM Categories", transaction)
                Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                    While reader.Read()
                        categoryIds(ReadString(reader, "CategoryName")) = ReadInteger(reader, "CategoryId")
                    End While
                End Using
            End Using

            Dim now As DateTime = UtcNow()
            For Each species As TreeSpecies In SeedData.Species
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection,
                    "INSERT INTO TreeSpecies (CommonName, ScientificName, CategoryId, ProductType, YieldUnit, " &
                    "YieldPerTreePerYear, PricePerUnit, HarvestType, YearsToFirstHarvest, YearsToFullProduction, " &
                    "HarvestSeason, Notes, CreatedAt, UpdatedAt) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", transaction)
                    AddText(command, species.CommonName, 100)
                    AddText(command, species.ScientificName, 150)
                    AddInteger(command, categoryIds(species.CategoryName))
                    AddText(command, species.ProductType, 60)
                    AddText(command, species.YieldUnit, 20)
                    AddDecimal(command, species.YieldPerTreePerYear)
                    AddDecimal(command, species.PricePerUnit)
                    AddText(command, species.HarvestType.ToString(), 10)
                    AddInteger(command, species.YearsToFirstHarvest)
                    AddInteger(command, species.YearsToFullProduction)
                    AddText(command, species.HarvestSeason, 100)
                    AddMemo(command, species.Notes)
                    AddDate(command, now)
                    AddDate(command, now)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            Next
        End Sub

    End Class

End Namespace

====================================================================================================
FILE 50 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\RepositoryBase.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data
Imports System.Data.OleDb

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Shared plumbing for every repository: opening a connection, translating Access
    ''' errors, and adding typed parameters. Every query in this application uses
    ''' parameters (the ? marks) instead of joining user input into the SQL text,
    ''' which is what prevents SQL injection.
    ''' </summary>
    Public MustInherit Class RepositoryBase

        ''' <summary>Creates the repository.</summary>
        Protected Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            If connectionFactory Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(connectionFactory))
            End If

            Me.ConnectionFactory = connectionFactory
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Opens connections to the database file.</summary>
        Protected ReadOnly Property ConnectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Runs <paramref name="work"/> on a background thread with an open connection,
        ''' then closes the connection. Access (OLE DB) has no real asynchronous calls, so
        ''' Task.Run keeps the window responsive while the file is read.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="operation">Short description used in error messages, for example "save the species".</param>
        ''' <param name="work">The database work to run on the open connection.</param>
        Protected Function QueryAsync(Of T)(operation As String, work As Func(Of OleDbConnection, T)) As Task(Of T)
            Return Task.Run(Function() Run(operation, work))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Same as <see cref="QueryAsync"/> for work that returns no value.</summary>
        Protected Function ExecuteAsync(operation As String, work As Action(Of OleDbConnection)) As Task
            Return QueryAsync(operation, Function(connection)
                                             work(connection)
                                             Return True
                                         End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Runs the work synchronously and converts any <see cref="OleDbException"/> into a <see cref="DataAccessException"/>.</summary>
        Protected Function Run(Of T)(operation As String, work As Func(Of OleDbConnection, T)) As T
            Try
                Using connection As OleDbConnection = ConnectionFactory.Open()
                    Return work(connection)
                End Using
            Catch ex As OleDbException
                Throw AccessErrorTranslator.Translate(operation, ex)
            End Try
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Creates a text command on an open connection (optionally inside a transaction).</summary>
        Protected Shared Function CreateCommand(connection As OleDbConnection, sql As String, Optional transaction As OleDbTransaction = Nothing) As OleDbCommand
            Dim command As OleDbCommand = connection.CreateCommand()
            command.CommandText = sql
            command.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            command.Transaction = transaction
            Return command
        End Function

        ' ------------------------------------------------------------------
        ' Parameters. In Access the names are ignored: values are matched to the
        ' ? marks by POSITION, so they must be added in the same order as in the SQL.
        ' ------------------------------------------------------------------

        ''' <summary>Adds a short text parameter (TEXT(n) column). Nothing is sent as NULL.</summary>
        Protected Shared Sub AddText(command As OleDbCommand, value As String, size As Integer)
            command.Parameters.Add(New OleDbParameter("?", OleDbType.VarWChar, size) With {.Value = DbValue(value)})
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Adds a long text parameter (MEMO column). Nothing is sent as NULL.</summary>
        Protected Shared Sub AddMemo(command As OleDbCommand, value As String)
            command.Parameters.Add(New OleDbParameter("?", OleDbType.LongVarWChar) With {.Value = DbValue(value)})
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Adds an INTEGER parameter. Nothing is sent as NULL.</summary>
        Protected Shared Sub AddInteger(command As OleDbCommand, value As Integer?)
            command.Parameters.Add(New OleDbParameter("?", OleDbType.Integer) With {
                .Value = If(value.HasValue, CObj(value.Value), DBNull.Value)})
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Adds a DECIMAL(12,2) parameter, matching the money and quantity columns.</summary>
        Protected Shared Sub AddDecimal(command As OleDbCommand, value As Decimal)
            command.Parameters.Add(New OleDbParameter("?", OleDbType.Decimal) With {.Precision = 12, .Scale = 2, .Value = value})
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Adds a DATETIME parameter. Nothing is sent as NULL.</summary>
        Protected Shared Sub AddDate(command As OleDbCommand, value As DateTime?)
            command.Parameters.Add(New OleDbParameter("?", OleDbType.Date) With {
                .Value = If(value.HasValue, CObj(value.Value), DBNull.Value)})
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Adds a Yes/No (BIT) parameter.</summary>
        Protected Shared Sub AddBoolean(command As OleDbCommand, value As Boolean)
            command.Parameters.Add(New OleDbParameter("?", OleDbType.Boolean) With {.Value = value})
        End Sub

        Private Shared Function DbValue(value As String) As Object
            Return If(value Is Nothing, CObj(DBNull.Value), value)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' The current UTC time without milliseconds. Access stores times to the second,
        ''' so dropping the milliseconds keeps saved and re-read values equal.
        ''' </summary>
        Protected Shared Function UtcNow() As DateTime
            Dim now As DateTime = DateTime.UtcNow
            Return New DateTime(now.Ticks - (now.Ticks Mod TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond), DateTimeKind.Utc)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns the AutoNumber value created by the last INSERT on this connection.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function LastInsertedId(connection As OleDbConnection, Optional transaction As OleDbTransaction = Nothing) As Integer
            Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "SELECT @@IDENTITY", transaction)
                Return Convert.ToInt32(command.ExecuteScalar())
            End Using
        End Function

        ' ------------------------------------------------------------------
        ' Reading columns.
        ' ------------------------------------------------------------------

        ''' <summary>Reads a nullable text column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadString(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As String
            Dim ordinal As Integer = reader.GetOrdinal(column)
            Return If(reader.IsDBNull(ordinal), Nothing, Convert.ToString(reader.GetValue(ordinal)))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads an INTEGER or AutoNumber column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadInteger(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As Integer
            Return Convert.ToInt32(reader.GetValue(reader.GetOrdinal(column)))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads a nullable INTEGER column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadNullableInteger(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As Integer?
            Dim ordinal As Integer = reader.GetOrdinal(column)
            Return If(reader.IsDBNull(ordinal), CType(Nothing, Integer?), Convert.ToInt32(reader.GetValue(ordinal)))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads a DECIMAL column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadDecimal(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As Decimal
            Return Convert.ToDecimal(reader.GetValue(reader.GetOrdinal(column)))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads a DATETIME column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadDate(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As DateTime
            Return Convert.ToDateTime(reader.GetValue(reader.GetOrdinal(column)))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads a nullable DATETIME column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadNullableDate(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As DateTime?
            Dim ordinal As Integer = reader.GetOrdinal(column)
            Return If(reader.IsDBNull(ordinal), CType(Nothing, DateTime?), Convert.ToDateTime(reader.GetValue(ordinal)))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads a Yes/No column.</summary>
        Protected Shared Function ReadBoolean(reader As OleDbDataReader, column As String) As Boolean
            Return Convert.ToBoolean(reader.GetValue(reader.GetOrdinal(column)))
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 51 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\ScenarioRepository.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>Reads and writes the Scenarios table (saved calculations).</summary>
    Public Class ScenarioRepository
        Inherits RepositoryBase

        ''' <summary>Creates the repository.</summary>
        Public Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            MyBase.New(connectionFactory)
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns all scenarios, newest first, with the species name joined in.</summary>
        Public Function GetAllAsync() As Task(Of List(Of Scenario))
            Const sql As String =
                "SELECT sc.ScenarioId, sc.ScenarioName, sc.SpeciesId, sp.CommonName, " &
                "sc.TreeCount, sc.ProjectionYears, sc.Notes, sc.CreatedAt " &
                "FROM Scenarios AS sc INNER JOIN TreeSpecies AS sp ON sc.SpeciesId = sp.SpeciesId " &
                "ORDER BY sc.CreatedAt DESC, sc.ScenarioId DESC"

            Return QueryAsync("load the saved scenarios", Function(connection)
                Dim scenarios As New List(Of Scenario)()
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                        While reader.Read()
                            scenarios.Add(New Scenario With {
                                .ScenarioId = ReadInteger(reader, "ScenarioId"),
                                .Name = ReadString(reader, "ScenarioName"),
                                .SpeciesId = ReadInteger(reader, "SpeciesId"),
                                .SpeciesName = ReadString(reader, "CommonName"),
                                .TreeCount = ReadInteger(reader, "TreeCount"),
                                .ProjectionYears = ReadInteger(reader, "ProjectionYears"),
                                .Notes = ReadString(reader, "Notes"),
                                .CreatedAtUtc = ReadDate(reader, "CreatedAt")
                            })
                        End While
                    End Using
                End Using

                Return scenarios
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Saves a new scenario and returns its generated ID.</summary>
        Public Function InsertAsync(scenario As Scenario) As Task(Of Integer)
            Const sql As String =
                "INSERT INTO Scenarios (ScenarioName, SpeciesId, TreeCount, ProjectionYears, Notes, CreatedAt) " &
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"

            Return QueryAsync("save the scenario", Function(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddText(command, scenario.Name, 100)
                    AddInteger(command, scenario.SpeciesId)
                    AddInteger(command, scenario.TreeCount)
                    AddInteger(command, scenario.ProjectionYears)
                    AddMemo(command, scenario.Notes)
                    AddDate(command, UtcNow())
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using

                Return LastInsertedId(connection)
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Deletes one scenario.</summary>
        Public Function DeleteAsync(scenarioId As Integer) As Task
            Return ExecuteAsync("delete the scenario", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "DELETE FROM Scenarios WHERE ScenarioId = ?")
                    AddInteger(command, scenarioId)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Counts the scenarios that use a species (shown before deleting the species).</summary>
        Public Function CountBySpeciesAsync(speciesId As Integer) As Task(Of Integer)
            Return QueryAsync("count the saved scenarios", Function(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Scenarios WHERE SpeciesId = ?")
                    AddInteger(command, speciesId)
                    Return Convert.ToInt32(command.ExecuteScalar())
                End Using
            End Function)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 52 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\Scripts\Schema.sql
====================================================================================================
-- =====================================================================
-- Interactive Tree Database & Yield Calculator
-- Schema.sql - the Microsoft Access (Jet 4.0) tables, keys and rules.
--
-- The program runs this script when it has to create a new TreeYieldDb.mdb
-- (see DatabaseInitializer.vb). Each statement ends with a semicolon.
-- Lines starting with -- are comments and are removed before running,
-- because Access SQL does not support comments.
--
-- Design notes
--   * 3rd normal form: category names live only in Categories; species
--     point to them with a foreign key (a Relationship in Access).
--   * CHECK constraints repeat the rules of SpeciesValidator.vb. Access
--     enforces them as "validation rules", even when someone types data
--     straight into a table in Microsoft Access.
--   * All times are stored in UTC and shown in local time by the program.
--   * Column names avoid Access reserved words (Name, Action, Version).
-- =====================================================================

CREATE TABLE SchemaVersion (
    VersionNumber INTEGER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_SchemaVersion PRIMARY KEY,
    AppliedAt DATETIME NOT NULL
);

-- Lookup table for the filter chips (Fruit-Bearing / Non-Fruit-Bearing).
CREATE TABLE Categories (
    CategoryId COUNTER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_Categories PRIMARY KEY,
    CategoryName TEXT(50) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Categories_Name UNIQUE,
    Description TEXT(200),
    CONSTRAINT CK_Categories_Name CHECK (CategoryName <> '')
);

-- One row per tree species: the data the calculator works with.
CREATE TABLE TreeSpecies (
    SpeciesId COUNTER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_TreeSpecies PRIMARY KEY,
    CommonName TEXT(100) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_TreeSpecies_CommonName UNIQUE,
    ScientificName TEXT(150),
    CategoryId INTEGER NOT NULL,
    ProductType TEXT(60) NOT NULL,
    YieldUnit TEXT(20) NOT NULL,
    YieldPerTreePerYear DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    PricePerUnit DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    HarvestType TEXT(10) NOT NULL,
    YearsToFirstHarvest INTEGER NOT NULL,
    YearsToFullProduction INTEGER NOT NULL,
    HarvestSeason TEXT(100),
    Notes MEMO,
    CreatedAt DATETIME NOT NULL,
    UpdatedAt DATETIME NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT FK_TreeSpecies_Categories FOREIGN KEY (CategoryId) REFERENCES Categories (CategoryId),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_CommonName CHECK (CommonName <> ''),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_Yield CHECK (YieldPerTreePerYear > 0 AND YieldPerTreePerYear <= 100000),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_Price CHECK (PricePerUnit >= 0 AND PricePerUnit <= 1000000),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_HarvestType CHECK (HarvestType IN ('Annual', 'Rotation')),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_FirstHarvest CHECK (YearsToFirstHarvest BETWEEN 0 AND 60),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_FullProduction CHECK (YearsToFullProduction BETWEEN 0 AND 60),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_YearOrder CHECK (YearsToFullProduction >= YearsToFirstHarvest),
    CONSTRAINT CK_TreeSpecies_RotationAge CHECK (HarvestType <> 'Rotation' OR YearsToFirstHarvest >= 1)
);

-- Administrator accounts. Only a salted PBKDF2 hash of the password is stored (as Base64 text).
CREATE TABLE Users (
    UserId COUNTER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_Users PRIMARY KEY,
    Username TEXT(50) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Users_Username UNIQUE,
    PasswordHash TEXT(100) NOT NULL,
    PasswordSalt TEXT(50) NOT NULL,
    Iterations INTEGER NOT NULL,
    MustChangePassword BIT NOT NULL,
    FailedLoginCount INTEGER NOT NULL,
    LockoutUntil DATETIME,
    LastLoginAt DATETIME,
    CreatedAt DATETIME NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT CK_Users_Username CHECK (Username <> ''),
    CONSTRAINT CK_Users_Iterations CHECK (Iterations > 0),
    CONSTRAINT CK_Users_FailedLoginCount CHECK (FailedLoginCount >= 0)
);

-- Saved calculations. Deleting a species deletes its scenarios (ON DELETE CASCADE).
CREATE TABLE Scenarios (
    ScenarioId COUNTER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_Scenarios PRIMARY KEY,
    ScenarioName TEXT(100) NOT NULL,
    SpeciesId INTEGER NOT NULL,
    TreeCount INTEGER NOT NULL,
    ProjectionYears INTEGER NOT NULL,
    Notes MEMO,
    CreatedAt DATETIME NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT FK_Scenarios_TreeSpecies FOREIGN KEY (SpeciesId) REFERENCES TreeSpecies (SpeciesId) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT CK_Scenarios_Name CHECK (ScenarioName <> ''),
    CONSTRAINT CK_Scenarios_TreeCount CHECK (TreeCount BETWEEN 1 AND 100000),
    CONSTRAINT CK_Scenarios_ProjectionYears CHECK (ProjectionYears BETWEEN 1 AND 30)
);

-- History of administrator actions. Details stores the record name as text,
-- so the history still makes sense after a record is deleted.
CREATE TABLE AuditLog (
    AuditId COUNTER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_AuditLog PRIMARY KEY,
    UserId INTEGER,
    ActionCode TEXT(30) NOT NULL,
    EntityName TEXT(50),
    EntityId INTEGER,
    Details MEMO,
    OccurredAt DATETIME NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT FK_AuditLog_Users FOREIGN KEY (UserId) REFERENCES Users (UserId)
);

-- Speeds up "newest first" in the audit log screen.
CREATE INDEX IX_AuditLog_OccurredAt ON AuditLog (OccurredAt);

Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' The categories and 22 sample species inserted into a new database and by
    ''' "Reset Sample Data".
    ''' <para>
    ''' IMPORTANT: yields and prices are illustrative planning estimates for a school
    ''' demonstration. Real yields depend on variety, age, spacing, soil and care, and
    ''' farm-gate prices change with season and market. Verify with the Department of
    ''' Agriculture (DA), the Philippine Statistics Authority (PSA) or local buyers.
    ''' </para>
    ''' Units: YieldPerTreePerYear is per mature tree per year. For rotation species it
    ''' is the mean annual increment in board feet; the trees are cut once at rotation age.
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class SeedData

                               .Description = "Trees grown mainly for fruit, nuts or beans that are harvested every year."},

                               .Description = "Trees grown mainly for timber, pulpwood or latex instead of fruit."}
        }

            Timber("Mahogany", "Swietenia macrophylla", "Timber", 8D, 70D, 25,
                   "Yield is mean annual increment in board feet; the tree is cut once at rotation age. Sample estimate."),
            Timber("Gmelina (Yemane)", "Gmelina arborea", "Timber", 15D, 35D, 10,
                   "Fast-growing plantation species for lumber and furniture. Sample estimate."),
            Timber("Falcata", "Falcataria falcata", "Peeler logs / timber", 20D, 25D, 8,

            Timber("Teak", "Tectona grandis", "Timber", 6D, 120D, 20,
                   "Premium, durable hardwood. Long rotation but high price. Sample estimate."),
            Timber("Acacia Mangium", "Acacia mangium", "Pulpwood / timber", 15D, 25D, 8,
                   "Fast-growing; improves poor soils by fixing nitrogen. Sample estimate."),
            Timber("Bagras (Rainbow Eucalyptus)", "Eucalyptus deglupta", "Timber / pulpwood", 12D, 30D, 12,
                   "Native to Mindanao; used for pulp, plywood and light construction. Sample estimate."),
            New TreeSpecies With {
                .CommonName = "Rubber", .ScientificName = "Hevea brasiliensis", .CategoryName = Category.NonFruitBearing,
                .ProductType = "Cup lump (latex)", .YieldUnit = "kg", .YieldPerTreePerYear = 4D, .PricePerUnit = 35D,
                .HarvestType = HarvestType.Annual, .YearsToFirstHarvest = 6, .YearsToFullProduction = 9,
                .HarvestSeason = "Year-round tapping (rest when leaves fall)",
                .Notes = "Tapped for latex every year once the trunk is thick enough. Sample estimate."}
        }

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>The two categories.</summary>
        Public Shared ReadOnly Property Categories As IReadOnlyList(Of Category)
            Get
                Return _categories
            End Get
        End Property

        ''' <summary>
        ''' The sample species. <see cref="TreeSpecies.CategoryName"/> is filled in;
        ''' the CategoryId is looked up when the rows are inserted.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared ReadOnly Property Species As IReadOnlyList(Of TreeSpecies)
            Get
                Return _species
            End Get
        End Property

        Private Shared Function Fruit(name As String, scientific As String, product As String, unit As String,
                                      yieldPerTree As Decimal, price As Decimal, firstHarvest As Integer,
                                      fullProduction As Integer, season As String, notes As String) As TreeSpecies
            Return New TreeSpecies With {
                .CommonName = name, .ScientificName = scientific, .CategoryName = Category.FruitBearing,
                .ProductType = product, .YieldUnit = unit, .YieldPerTreePerYear = yieldPerTree, .PricePerUnit = price,
                .HarvestType = HarvestType.Annual, .YearsToFirstHarvest = firstHarvest,
                .YearsToFullProduction = fullProduction, .HarvestSeason = season, .Notes = notes}
        End Function

        Private Shared Function Timber(name As String, scientific As String, product As String,
                                       yieldPerTree As Decimal, price As Decimal, rotationAge As Integer,
                                       notes As String) As TreeSpecies
            Return New TreeSpecies With {
                .CommonName = name, .ScientificName = scientific, .CategoryName = Category.NonFruitBearing,
                .ProductType = product, .YieldUnit = "board ft", .YieldPerTreePerYear = yieldPerTree, .PricePerUnit = price,
                .HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation, .YearsToFirstHarvest = rotationAge,
                .YearsToFullProduction = rotationAge, .HarvestSeason = "At rotation age", .Notes = notes}
        End Function

    End Class
