FILE 54 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\SpeciesRepository.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports System.Text
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>Create, read, update and delete (CRUD) for the TreeSpecies table.</summary>
    Public Class SpeciesRepository
        Inherits RepositoryBase

        ' One column list shared by every SELECT so the Map function always finds its columns.
        Private Const SelectColumns As String =
            "SELECT s.SpeciesId, s.CommonName, s.ScientificName, s.CategoryId, c.CategoryName, " &
            "s.ProductType, s.YieldUnit, s.YieldPerTreePerYear, s.PricePerUnit, s.HarvestType, " &
            "s.YearsToFirstHarvest, s.YearsToFullProduction, s.HarvestSeason, s.Notes, s.CreatedAt, s.UpdatedAt " &
            "FROM TreeSpecies AS s INNER JOIN Categories AS c ON s.CategoryId = c.CategoryId"

        ''' <summary>Creates the repository.</summary>
        Public Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            MyBase.New(connectionFactory)
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns every species sorted by common name.</summary>
        Public Function GetAllAsync() As Task(Of List(Of TreeSpecies))
            Return SearchAsync(Nothing, Nothing)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Searches by name, scientific name or product, optionally limited to one category.
        ''' The search text is passed as a parameter, so input such as <c>' OR 1=1 --</c>
        ''' is only ever compared as text and can never change the query.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="searchText">Text to look for (case-insensitive, matches anywhere). Nothing or blank means no text filter.</param>
        ''' <param name="categoryId">Category to keep, or Nothing for all categories.</param>
        Public Function SearchAsync(searchText As String, categoryId As Integer?) As Task(Of List(Of TreeSpecies))
            Dim trimmed As String = If(searchText Is Nothing, Nothing, searchText.Trim())
            Dim pattern As String = If(String.IsNullOrEmpty(trimmed), Nothing, "%" & SqlText.EscapeLikePattern(trimmed) & "%")

            Return QueryAsync("search the species list", Function(connection)
                Dim results As New List(Of TreeSpecies)()
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, String.Empty)
                    ' Only the SHAPE of the WHERE clause depends on the input (is there a
                    ' category? a search text?). The values themselves are always parameters.
                    Dim conditions As New List(Of String)()
                    If categoryId.HasValue Then
                        conditions.Add("s.CategoryId = ?")
                        AddInteger(command, categoryId.Value)
                    End If

                    If pattern IsNot Nothing Then
                        conditions.Add("(s.CommonName LIKE ? OR s.ScientificName LIKE ? OR s.ProductType LIKE ?)")
                        AddText(command, pattern, 255)
                        AddText(command, pattern, 255)
                        AddText(command, pattern, 255)
                    End If

                    Dim sql As New StringBuilder(SelectColumns)
                    If conditions.Count > 0 Then
                        sql.Append(" WHERE ").Append(String.Join(" AND ", conditions))
                    End If

                    sql.Append(" ORDER BY s.CommonName")
                    command.CommandText = sql.ToString()

                    Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                        While reader.Read()
                            results.Add(Map(reader))
                        End While
                    End Using
                End Using

                Return results
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns one species, or Nothing if it does not exist.</summary>
        Public Function GetByIdAsync(speciesId As Integer) As Task(Of TreeSpecies)
            Return QueryAsync("load the species", Function(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, SelectColumns & " WHERE s.SpeciesId = ?")
                    AddInteger(command, speciesId)
                    Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                        Return If(reader.Read(), Map(reader), Nothing)
                    End Using
                End Using
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Inserts a new species and returns its generated ID.</summary>
        ''' <exception cref="DuplicateRecordException">Another species already has the same common name.</exception>
        Public Function InsertAsync(species As TreeSpecies) As Task(Of Integer)
            Const sql As String =
                "INSERT INTO TreeSpecies (CommonName, ScientificName, CategoryId, ProductType, YieldUnit, " &
                "YieldPerTreePerYear, PricePerUnit, HarvestType, YearsToFirstHarvest, YearsToFullProduction, " &
                "HarvestSeason, Notes, CreatedAt, UpdatedAt) " &
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"

            Return QueryAsync("add the species", Function(connection)
                Dim now As DateTime = UtcNow()
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddSpeciesValues(command, species)
                    AddDate(command, now)
                    AddDate(command, now)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using

                Return LastInsertedId(connection)
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Saves changes to an existing species.</summary>
        ''' <exception cref="DuplicateRecordException">Another species already has the same common name.</exception>
        ''' <exception cref="DataAccessException">The species was deleted in the meantime.</exception>
        Public Function UpdateAsync(species As TreeSpecies) As Task
            Const sql As String =
                "UPDATE TreeSpecies SET CommonName = ?, ScientificName = ?, CategoryId = ?, ProductType = ?, " &
                "YieldUnit = ?, YieldPerTreePerYear = ?, PricePerUnit = ?, HarvestType = ?, " &
                "YearsToFirstHarvest = ?, YearsToFullProduction = ?, HarvestSeason = ?, Notes = ?, UpdatedAt = ? " &
                "WHERE SpeciesId = ?"

            Return ExecuteAsync("update the species", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddSpeciesValues(command, species)
                    AddDate(command, UtcNow())
                    AddInteger(command, species.SpeciesId)

                    If command.ExecuteNonQuery() = 0 Then
                        Throw New DataAccessException("This species no longer exists. It may have been deleted.", Nothing)
                    End If
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Deletes a species. Its saved scenarios are deleted with it by the
        ''' ON DELETE CASCADE rule of the Scenarios relationship.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Function DeleteAsync(speciesId As Integer) As Task
            Return ExecuteAsync("delete the species", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "DELETE FROM TreeSpecies WHERE SpeciesId = ?")
                    AddInteger(command, speciesId)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Adds the 12 editable columns in the order used by the INSERT and UPDATE statements.</summary>
        Private Shared Sub AddSpeciesValues(command As OleDbCommand, s As TreeSpecies)
            AddText(command, s.CommonName, 100)
            AddText(command, s.ScientificName, 150)
            AddInteger(command, s.CategoryId)
            AddText(command, s.ProductType, 60)
            AddText(command, s.YieldUnit, 20)
            AddDecimal(command, s.YieldPerTreePerYear)
            AddDecimal(command, s.PricePerUnit)
            AddText(command, s.HarvestType.ToString(), 10)
            AddInteger(command, s.YearsToFirstHarvest)
            AddInteger(command, s.YearsToFullProduction)
            AddText(command, s.HarvestSeason, 100)
            AddMemo(command, s.Notes)
        End Sub

        Private Shared Function Map(reader As OleDbDataReader) As TreeSpecies
            Dim kind As HarvestType
            If Not [Enum].TryParse(ReadString(reader, "HarvestType"), True, kind) Then
                kind = HarvestType.Annual
            End If

            Return New TreeSpecies With {
                .SpeciesId = ReadInteger(reader, "SpeciesId"),
                .CommonName = ReadString(reader, "CommonName"),
                .ScientificName = ReadString(reader, "ScientificName"),
                .CategoryId = ReadInteger(reader, "CategoryId"),
                .CategoryName = ReadString(reader, "CategoryName"),
                .ProductType = ReadString(reader, "ProductType"),
                .YieldUnit = ReadString(reader, "YieldUnit"),
                .YieldPerTreePerYear = ReadDecimal(reader, "YieldPerTreePerYear"),
                .PricePerUnit = ReadDecimal(reader, "PricePerUnit"),
                .HarvestType = kind,
                .YearsToFirstHarvest = ReadInteger(reader, "YearsToFirstHarvest"),
                .YearsToFullProduction = ReadInteger(reader, "YearsToFullProduction"),
                .HarvestSeason = ReadString(reader, "HarvestSeason"),
                .Notes = ReadString(reader, "Notes"),
                .CreatedAtUtc = ReadDate(reader, "CreatedAt"),
                .UpdatedAtUtc = ReadDate(reader, "UpdatedAt")
            }
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 55 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\SqlScriptRunner.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports System.IO
Imports System.Reflection

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>Loads the .sql file embedded in this assembly and runs it statement by statement.</summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class SqlScriptRunner

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Splits a script into statements. Access runs only one statement per command and
        ''' does not understand comments, so lines that start with -- are removed and the
        ''' rest is split at each semicolon. (The schema script has no semicolons inside text.)
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared Function SplitStatements(script As String) As IList(Of String)
            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(script) Then
                Return New List(Of String)()
            End If

            Dim codeLines As IEnumerable(Of String) = script.Split(ControlChars.Lf).
                Select(Function(line) line.TrimEnd(ControlChars.Cr)).
                Where(Function(line) Not line.TrimStart().StartsWith("--", StringComparison.Ordinal))

            Return String.Join(vbLf, codeLines).
                Split(";"c).
                Select(Function(statement) statement.Trim()).
                Where(Function(statement) statement.Length > 0).
                ToList()
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Reads an embedded script, for example "Schema.sql".</summary>
        Public Shared Function LoadEmbeddedScript(fileName As String) As String
            Dim assembly As Assembly = GetType(SqlScriptRunner).Assembly

            ' Visual Basic names embedded resources RootNamespace + file name, e.g. "TreeYield.Core.Schema.sql".
            Dim resourceName As String = assembly.GetManifestResourceNames().
                FirstOrDefault(Function(name) name.EndsWith("." & fileName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))

            If resourceName Is Nothing Then
                Throw New InvalidOperationException("The embedded SQL script '" & fileName & "' was not found.")
            End If

            Using stream As Stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(resourceName)
                Using reader As New StreamReader(stream)
                    Return reader.ReadToEnd()
                End Using
            End Using
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Runs every statement of a script on an open connection.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub Execute(connection As OleDbConnection, transaction As OleDbTransaction, script As String)
            For Each statement As String In SplitStatements(script)
                Using command As New OleDbCommand(statement, connection, transaction)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            Next
        End Sub

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 56 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\SqlText.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Text

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>Small helpers for building safe Access SQL text.</summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class SqlText

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Makes the LIKE wildcards %, _ and [ match literally by putting each one in
        ''' brackets (Access SQL has no ESCAPE clause, but [%] means "a real % sign").
        ''' The value is still sent as a parameter; this only stops "50%" from meaning
        ''' "50 followed by anything".
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared Function EscapeLikePattern(text As String) As String
            If text Is Nothing Then
                Return Nothing
            End If

            Dim escaped As New StringBuilder(text.Length + 8)
            For Each c As Char In text
                Select Case c
                    Case "%"c, "_"c, "["c
                        escaped.Append("["c).Append(c).Append("]"c)
                    Case Else
                        escaped.Append(c)
                End Select
            Next

            Return escaped.ToString()
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 57 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Data\UserRepository.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Data

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Reads and writes the Users table (administrator accounts). The hash and salt
    ''' are byte arrays in the program and Base64 text in the database, which keeps
    ''' the table readable in Microsoft Access.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class UserRepository
        Inherits RepositoryBase

        ''' <summary>Creates the repository.</summary>
        Public Sub New(connectionFactory As AccessConnectionFactory)
            MyBase.New(connectionFactory)
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Finds a user by name (Access compares text case-insensitively). Returns Nothing if not found.</summary>
        Public Function GetByUsernameAsync(username As String) As Task(Of AppUser)
            Const sql As String =
                "SELECT UserId, Username, PasswordHash, PasswordSalt, Iterations, MustChangePassword, " &
                "FailedLoginCount, LockoutUntil, LastLoginAt FROM Users WHERE Username = ?"

            Return QueryAsync("load the user account", Function(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddText(command, username, 50)
                    Using reader As OleDbDataReader = command.ExecuteReader()
                        If Not reader.Read() Then
                            Return Nothing
                        End If

                        Return New AppUser With {
                            .UserId = ReadInteger(reader, "UserId"),
                            .Username = ReadString(reader, "Username"),
                            .PasswordHash = Convert.FromBase64String(ReadString(reader, "PasswordHash")),
                            .PasswordSalt = Convert.FromBase64String(ReadString(reader, "PasswordSalt")),
                            .Iterations = ReadInteger(reader, "Iterations"),
                            .MustChangePassword = ReadBoolean(reader, "MustChangePassword"),
                            .FailedLoginCount = ReadInteger(reader, "FailedLoginCount"),
                            .LockoutUntilUtc = ReadNullableDate(reader, "LockoutUntil"),
                            .LastLoginAtUtc = ReadNullableDate(reader, "LastLoginAt")
                        }
                    End Using
                End Using
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns the number of user accounts.</summary>
        Public Function CountAsync() As Task(Of Integer)
            Return QueryAsync("count the user accounts", Function(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Users")
                    Return Convert.ToInt32(command.ExecuteScalar())
                End Using
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Creates a user and returns the generated ID.</summary>
        Public Function CreateAsync(username As String, passwordHash As Byte(), passwordSalt As Byte(),
                                    iterations As Integer, mustChangePassword As Boolean) As Task(Of Integer)
            Const sql As String =
                "INSERT INTO Users (Username, PasswordHash, PasswordSalt, Iterations, MustChangePassword, " &
                "FailedLoginCount, CreatedAt) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)"

            Return QueryAsync("create the user account", Function(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddText(command, username, 50)
                    AddText(command, Convert.ToBase64String(passwordHash), 100)
                    AddText(command, Convert.ToBase64String(passwordSalt), 50)
                    AddInteger(command, iterations)
                    AddBoolean(command, mustChangePassword)
                    AddDate(command, UtcNow())
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using

                Return LastInsertedId(connection)
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Adds one failed attempt and locks the account when the limit is reached.
        ''' The counter and the lock are changed by ONE UPDATE statement (inside a
        ''' transaction with the read-back), so two quick attempts cannot both read the
        ''' old count. Returns the lockout end time, or Nothing if the account is not locked.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="userId">The account.</param>
        ''' <param name="maxAttempts">Failures allowed before locking.</param>
        ''' <param name="lockoutMinutes">How long the lock lasts.</param>
        Public Function RecordFailedLoginAsync(userId As Integer, maxAttempts As Integer, lockoutMinutes As Integer) As Task(Of DateTime?)
            ' LockoutUntil is set first, from the count as it was BEFORE this failure,
            ' so the result is the same whichever order Access evaluates the SET list in.
            Const sql As String =
                "UPDATE Users SET LockoutUntil = IIf(FailedLoginCount + 1 >= ?, ?, Null), " &
                "FailedLoginCount = FailedLoginCount + 1 WHERE UserId = ?"

            Return QueryAsync("record the failed login", Function(connection)
                Using transaction As OleDbTransaction = connection.BeginTransaction()
                    Using update As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql, transaction)
                        AddInteger(update, maxAttempts)
                        AddDate(update, UtcNow().AddMinutes(lockoutMinutes))
                        AddInteger(update, userId)
                        update.ExecuteNonQuery()
                    End Using

                    Dim lockedUntil As DateTime?
                    Using read As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, "SELECT LockoutUntil FROM Users WHERE UserId = ?", transaction)
                        AddInteger(read, userId)
                        Dim value As Object = read.ExecuteScalar()
                        lockedUntil = If(value Is Nothing OrElse value Is DBNull.Value, CType(Nothing, DateTime?), Convert.ToDateTime(value))
                    End Using

                    transaction.Commit()
                    Return lockedUntil
                End Using
            End Function)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Clears the failure counter and lock after a successful login, and stamps the login time.</summary>
        Public Function RecordSuccessfulLoginAsync(userId As Integer) As Task
            Return ExecuteAsync("record the login", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection,
                    "UPDATE Users SET FailedLoginCount = 0, LockoutUntil = Null, LastLoginAt = ? WHERE UserId = ?")
                    AddDate(command, UtcNow())
                    AddInteger(command, userId)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Clears an expired lock so the user gets a fresh set of attempts.</summary>
        Public Function ClearLockoutAsync(userId As Integer) As Task
            Return ExecuteAsync("clear the account lock", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection,
                    "UPDATE Users SET FailedLoginCount = 0, LockoutUntil = Null WHERE UserId = ?")
                    AddInteger(command, userId)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Stores a new password hash and clears the "must change password" flag.</summary>
        Public Function UpdatePasswordAsync(userId As Integer, passwordHash As Byte(), passwordSalt As Byte(), iterations As Integer) As Task
            Const sql As String =
                "UPDATE Users SET PasswordHash = ?, PasswordSalt = ?, Iterations = ?, MustChangePassword = False " &
                "WHERE UserId = ?"

            Return ExecuteAsync("change the password", Sub(connection)
                Using command As OleDbCommand = CreateCommand(connection, sql)
                    AddText(command, Convert.ToBase64String(passwordHash), 100)
                    AddText(command, Convert.ToBase64String(passwordSalt), 50)
                    AddInteger(command, iterations)
                    AddInteger(command, userId)
                    command.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End Using
            End Sub)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

    ''' <summary>

        ''' <summary>If set and in the future, the account is locked until this time (UTC).</summary>

        ''' <summary>Time of the last successful login (UTC).</summary>

Namespace Models

    ''' <summary>
    ''' How a species produces income. The yield calculator uses a different
    ''' formula for each type (see <see cref="Services.YieldCalculator"/>).
    ''' </summary>
    Public Enum HarvestType

        ''' <summary>
        ''' The tree is harvested every year once it starts bearing
        ''' (fruit, nuts, beans, latex). Yield ramps up until full production.
        ''' </summary>
        Annual = 0

        ''' <summary>
        ''' The tree is cut once at rotation age (timber, pulpwood). The yearly
        ''' figure is the mean annual increment; income happens at harvest.
        ''' </summary>
        Rotation = 1

    End Enum

====================================================================================================
FILE 62 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Models\LoginResult.vb
====================================================================================================
Namespace Models

    ''' <summary>Outcome of a login attempt.</summary>
    Public Enum LoginStatus

        ''' <summary>Username and password were correct.</summary>
        Success

        ''' <summary>The username or password box was empty.</summary>
        MissingInput

        ''' <summary>Unknown username or wrong password (deliberately not told apart).</summary>
        InvalidCredentials

        ''' <summary>Too many failed attempts; the account is temporarily locked.</summary>
        LockedOut

    End Enum

    ''' <summary>Result returned by <see cref="Services.AuthService.LoginAsync"/>.</summary>
    Public Class LoginResult

        Private Sub New(status As LoginStatus, message As String, user As AppUser)
            Me.Status = status
            Me.Message = message
            Me.User = user
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>What happened.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Status As LoginStatus

        ''' <summary>Message to show the user. Nothing on success.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Message As String

        ''' <summary>The signed-in user. Only set when <see cref="Status"/> is Success.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property User As AppUser

        ''' <summary>True when the login succeeded.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Succeeded As Boolean
            Get
                Return Status = LoginStatus.Success
            End Get
        End Property

        ''' <summary>Creates a successful result.</summary>
        Public Shared Function Success(user As AppUser) As LoginResult
            Return New LoginResult(LoginStatus.Success, Nothing, user)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Creates a failed result with a message for the user.</summary>
        Public Shared Function Fail(status As LoginStatus, message As String) As LoginResult
            Return New LoginResult(status, message, Nothing)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

Namespace Models

    ''' <summary>
    ''' One tree species record from the TreeSpecies table: the agronomic and
    ''' market figures that the yield and revenue calculator needs.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class TreeSpecies

        ''' <summary>Primary key. 0 means the record has not been saved yet.</summary>
        Public Property SpeciesId As Integer

        ''' <summary>Common or local name shown in the UI, for example "Carabao Mango". Unique.</summary>
        Public Property CommonName As String

        ''' <summary>Botanical name, for example "Mangifera indica". Optional.</summary>
        Public Property ScientificName As String

        ''' <summary>Foreign key to the Categories table.</summary>
        Public Property CategoryId As Integer

        ''' <summary>Category name loaded through a JOIN. Display only; not saved.</summary>
        Public Property CategoryName As String

        ''' <summary>What is sold, for example "Fresh fruit", "Timber" or "Cup lump".</summary>
        Public Property ProductType As String

        ''' <summary>Unit the yield is measured in, for example "kg", "pcs" or "board ft".</summary>
        Public Property YieldUnit As String

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Annual species: harvest of one mature tree in one year.
        ''' Rotation species: mean annual increment (growth per tree per year) that is
        ''' realized when the tree is harvested at rotation age.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Property YieldPerTreePerYear As Decimal

        ''' <summary>Estimated farm-gate price in Philippine pesos for one <see cref="YieldUnit"/>.</summary>
        Public Property PricePerUnit As Decimal

        ''' <summary>Whether the species is harvested every year or once at rotation age.</summary>
        Public Property HarvestType As HarvestType

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Annual: number of years after planting until the first harvest.
        ''' Rotation: rotation age, the number of years until the one-time harvest.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Property YearsToFirstHarvest As Integer

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Annual: year in which the tree reaches full production.
        ''' Rotation: always equal to <see cref="YearsToFirstHarvest"/>.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Property YearsToFullProduction As Integer

        ''' <summary>Usual harvest months, for example "March - June". Optional.</summary>
        Public Property HarvestSeason As String

        ''' <summary>Free-text remarks such as data sources or growing tips. Optional.</summary>
        Public Property Notes As String

        ''' <summary>When the record was created (UTC).</summary>
        Public Property CreatedAtUtc As DateTime

        ''' <summary>When the record was last changed (UTC).</summary>
        Public Property UpdatedAtUtc As DateTime

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Creates a shallow copy. The editor form works on a copy so that pressing
        ''' Cancel never changes the original object.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Function Clone() As TreeSpecies
            Return DirectCast(MemberwiseClone(), TreeSpecies)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns the common name so the object can be shown directly in a ComboBox.</summary>
        Public Overrides Function ToString() As String
            Return If(CommonName, String.Empty)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

====================================================================================================
FILE 70 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\AuthService.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports TreeYield.Core.Data
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Administrator authentication: login with account lockout, password changes,
    ''' and creation of the default account on first run.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class AuthService

        ''' <summary>Username of the account created on first run.</summary>
        Public Const DefaultAdminUsername As String = "admin"

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Password of the account created on first run. The account is flagged
        ''' "must change password", so this value only works for the very first login.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Const DefaultAdminPassword As String = "Admin@123"

        ''' <summary>Failed attempts allowed before the account is locked.</summary>
        Public Const MaxFailedAttempts As Integer = 5

        ''' <summary>How long a locked account stays locked.</summary>
        Public Const LockoutMinutes As Integer = 5

        ' One message for "unknown user" and "wrong password", so an attacker cannot
        ' find out which usernames exist.
        Private Const InvalidCredentialsMessage As String = "Invalid username or password."

        ' Used to spend the same hashing time when the username does not exist,
        ' so response time does not reveal whether a username is valid.
        Private Shared ReadOnly DummySalt(PasswordHasher.SaltSize - 1) As Byte

        Private ReadOnly _users As UserRepository
        Private ReadOnly _audit As AuditLogRepository

        ''' <summary>Creates the service.</summary>
        Public Sub New(users As UserRepository, audit As AuditLogRepository)
            If users Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(users))
            End If

            If audit Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(audit))
            End If

            _users = users
            _audit = audit
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Checks a username and password.
        ''' <list type="number">
        ''' <item>Empty input is rejected.</item>
        ''' <item>An unknown user gets the generic message (after a dummy hash).</item>
        ''' <item>A locked account is refused until the lock expires.</item>
        ''' <item>A wrong password adds a failure; the 5th failure locks the account for 5 minutes.</item>
        ''' <item>A correct password resets the counter.</item>
        ''' </list>
        ''' </summary>
        Public Async Function LoginAsync(username As String, password As String) As Task(Of LoginResult)
            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username) OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(password) Then
                Return LoginResult.Fail(LoginStatus.MissingInput, "Enter your username and password.")
            End If

            Dim name As String = username.Trim()
            Dim user As AppUser = Await _users.GetByUsernameAsync(name).ConfigureAwait(False)

            If user Is Nothing Then
                Await Task.Run(Function() PasswordHasher.Hash(password, DummySalt, PasswordHasher.DefaultIterations)).ConfigureAwait(False)
                Await TryAuditAsync(Nothing, AuditActions.LoginFailed, "Unknown username: " & Shorten(name, 50)).ConfigureAwait(False)
                Return LoginResult.Fail(LoginStatus.InvalidCredentials, InvalidCredentialsMessage)
            End If

            If user.LockoutUntilUtc.HasValue Then
                If user.LockoutUntilUtc.Value > DateTime.UtcNow Then
                    Return LoginResult.Fail(LoginStatus.LockedOut, LockedMessage(user.LockoutUntilUtc.Value))
                End If

                ' The lock has expired: start again with a fresh set of attempts.
                Await _users.ClearLockoutAsync(user.UserId).ConfigureAwait(False)
            End If

            ' PBKDF2 is slow on purpose (about 0.1 s), so it runs on a background thread.
            Dim passwordOk As Boolean = Await Task.Run(Function() PasswordHasher.Verify(password, user.PasswordSalt, user.Iterations, user.PasswordHash)).ConfigureAwait(False)

            If Not passwordOk Then
                Dim lockedUntil As DateTime? = Await _users.RecordFailedLoginAsync(user.UserId, MaxFailedAttempts, LockoutMinutes).ConfigureAwait(False)

                If lockedUntil.HasValue Then
                    Await TryAuditAsync(user.UserId, AuditActions.Lockout,
                        String.Format("Account locked for {0} minutes after {1} failed attempts.", LockoutMinutes, MaxFailedAttempts)).ConfigureAwait(False)
                    Return LoginResult.Fail(LoginStatus.LockedOut, LockedMessage(lockedUntil.Value))
                End If

                Await TryAuditAsync(user.UserId, AuditActions.LoginFailed, "Wrong password.").ConfigureAwait(False)
                Return LoginResult.Fail(LoginStatus.InvalidCredentials, InvalidCredentialsMessage)
            End If

            Await _users.RecordSuccessfulLoginAsync(user.UserId).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Await TryAuditAsync(user.UserId, AuditActions.Login, "Signed in.").ConfigureAwait(False)

            user.FailedLoginCount = 0
            user.LockoutUntilUtc = Nothing
            user.LastLoginAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow
            Return LoginResult.Success(user)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Changes the password of a signed-in user. Returns Nothing on success,
        ''' otherwise a message explaining why the password was not changed.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Async Function ChangePasswordAsync(user As AppUser, currentPassword As String, newPassword As String, confirmPassword As String) As Task(Of String)
            If user Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(user))
            End If

            Dim currentOk As Boolean = Await Task.Run(Function() PasswordHasher.Verify(If(currentPassword, String.Empty), user.PasswordSalt, user.Iterations, user.PasswordHash)).ConfigureAwait(False)

            If Not currentOk Then
                Return "The current password is incorrect."
            End If

            If Not String.Equals(newPassword, confirmPassword, StringComparison.Ordinal) Then
                Return "The new password and the confirmation do not match."
            End If

            If String.Equals(newPassword, currentPassword, StringComparison.Ordinal) Then
                Return "The new password must be different from the current password."
            End If

            Dim policyError As String = PasswordPolicy.Validate(newPassword, user.Username)
            If policyError IsNot Nothing Then
                Return policyError
            End If

            Dim salt As Byte() = PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt()
            Dim hash As Byte() = Await Task.Run(Function() PasswordHasher.Hash(newPassword, salt, PasswordHasher.DefaultIterations)).ConfigureAwait(False)

            Await _users.UpdatePasswordAsync(user.UserId, hash, salt, PasswordHasher.DefaultIterations).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Await TryAuditAsync(user.UserId, AuditActions.PasswordChange, "Password changed.").ConfigureAwait(False)

            user.PasswordHash = hash
            user.PasswordSalt = salt
            user.Iterations = PasswordHasher.DefaultIterations
            user.MustChangePassword = False
            Return Nothing
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Writes a LOGOUT entry to the audit log.</summary>
        Public Function LogoutAsync(user As AppUser) As Task
            If user Is Nothing Then
                Return Task.FromResult(0)
            End If

            Return TryAuditAsync(user.UserId, AuditActions.Logout, "Signed out.")
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Creates the default administrator account if the Users table is empty.
        ''' The password is hashed in code because PBKDF2 cannot be computed in SQL.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Async Function EnsureDefaultAdminAsync() As Task
            Dim userCount As Integer = Await _users.CountAsync().ConfigureAwait(False)
            If userCount > 0 Then
                Return
            End If

            Dim salt As Byte() = PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt()
            Dim hash As Byte() = Await Task.Run(Function() PasswordHasher.Hash(DefaultAdminPassword, salt, PasswordHasher.DefaultIterations)).ConfigureAwait(False)

            Await _users.CreateAsync(DefaultAdminUsername, hash, salt, PasswordHasher.DefaultIterations, True).ConfigureAwait(False)
        End Function

        Private Shared Function LockedMessage(lockedUntilUtc As DateTime) As String
            Dim minutes As Integer = CInt(Math.Ceiling((lockedUntilUtc - DateTime.UtcNow).TotalMinutes))
            If minutes < 1 Then
                minutes = 1
            End If

            Return String.Format(
                "This account is locked because of too many failed sign-in attempts. Try again in {0} minute{1}.",
                minutes, If(minutes = 1, String.Empty, "s"))
        End Function

        Private Shared Function Shorten(text As String, maxLength As Integer) As String
            Return If(text.Length <= maxLength, text, text.Substring(0, maxLength) & "...")
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Audit logging is "best effort": if writing the log fails, the login itself
        ''' should still work, so the error is only traced instead of shown to the user.
        ''' </summary>
        Private Async Function TryAuditAsync(userId As Integer?, action As String, details As String) As Task
            Try
                Await _audit.AddAsync(userId, action, "User", userId, details).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Catch ex As DataAccessException
                Trace.TraceWarning("Audit log write failed: " & ex.ToString())
            End Try
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 71 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\CsvExporter.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Globalization
Imports System.Text
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Builds the CSV (comma-separated values) report that opens in Excel.
    ''' Follows RFC 4180 quoting and blocks "CSV formula injection".
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class CsvExporter

        ' Characters that make Excel treat a cell as a formula, e.g. =HYPERLINK("http://evil").
        Private Const FormulaTriggers As String = "=+-@" & ControlChars.Tab & ControlChars.Cr

        Private Shared ReadOnly CharactersNeedingQuotes As Char() = {","c, """"c, ControlChars.Cr, ControlChars.Lf}

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Makes one value safe for a CSV cell:
        ''' <list type="number">
        ''' <item>If it starts with a formula character, a leading apostrophe is added so Excel shows it as text.</item>
        ''' <item>If it contains a comma, quote or line break, it is wrapped in quotes and inner quotes are doubled.</item>
        ''' </list>
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared Function EscapeField(value As String) As String
            If String.IsNullOrEmpty(value) Then
                Return String.Empty
            End If

            Dim safe As String = If(FormulaTriggers.IndexOf(value(0)) >= 0, "'" & value, value)

            If safe.IndexOfAny(CharactersNeedingQuotes) >= 0 Then
                safe = """" & safe.Replace("""", """""") & """"
            End If

            Return safe
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Joins escaped values into one CSV line (without the line break).</summary>
        Public Shared Function BuildRow(ParamArray fields As String()) As String
            If fields Is Nothing Then
                Return String.Empty
            End If

            Return String.Join(",", fields.Select(AddressOf EscapeField))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Builds the full report: species details, inputs, results and the projection table.</summary>
        ''' <param name="result">Snapshot from <see cref="YieldCalculator.Calculate"/>.</param>
        ''' <param name="projection">Projection from <see cref="YieldCalculator.Project"/>.</param>
        ''' <param name="generatedAt">Time written in the report header (local time).</param>
        Public Shared Function BuildReport(result As YieldResult, projection As YieldProjection, generatedAt As DateTime) As String
            If result Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(result))
            End If

            If projection Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(projection))
            End If

            Dim s As TreeSpecies = result.Species
            Dim unit As String = s.YieldUnit
            Dim isRotation As Boolean = s.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation
            Dim csv As New StringBuilder()

            AppendRow(csv, "Tree Yield & Revenue Projection Report")
            AppendRow(csv, "Generated", generatedAt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
            AppendRow(csv)

            AppendRow(csv, "Field", "Value")
            AppendRow(csv, "Species", s.CommonName)
            AppendRow(csv, "Scientific name", s.ScientificName)
            AppendRow(csv, "Category", s.CategoryName)
            AppendRow(csv, "Product", s.ProductType)
            AppendRow(csv, "Harvest type", If(isRotation, "Rotation (one-time harvest)", "Annual"))
            AppendRow(csv, "Yield per tree per year (" & unit & ")", Number(s.YieldPerTreePerYear))
            AppendRow(csv, "Price per " & unit & " (PHP)", Number(s.PricePerUnit))
            AppendRow(csv, If(isRotation, "Rotation age (years)", "Years to first harvest"), Whole(s.YearsToFirstHarvest))
            If Not isRotation Then
                AppendRow(csv, "Years to full production", Whole(s.YearsToFullProduction))
            End If

            AppendRow(csv, "Tree count", Whole(result.TreeCount))
            AppendRow(csv, If(isRotation, "Annualized yield (", "Annual yield at full production (") & unit & ")", Number(result.AnnualYield))
            AppendRow(csv, If(isRotation, "Annualized gross sales (PHP)", "Estimated annual gross sales (PHP)"), Number(result.GrossSales))
            If isRotation Then
                AppendRow(csv, "Harvest year", Whole(result.HarvestYear))
                AppendRow(csv, "Harvest yield (" & unit & ")", Number(result.HarvestYield))
                AppendRow(csv, "Harvest gross sales (PHP)", Number(result.HarvestSales))
            End If

            AppendRow(csv)
            AppendRow(csv, Whole(projection.Years) & "-year projection")
            AppendRow(csv, "Year", "Production", "Yield (" & unit & ")", "Gross sales (PHP)",
                "Cumulative yield (" & unit & ")", "Cumulative gross sales (PHP)")

            For Each row As ProjectionYear In projection.Rows
                AppendRow(csv,
                    Whole(row.Year),
                    Formatters.ProductionStage(row, s.HarvestType),
                    Number(row.Yield),
                    Number(row.GrossSales),
                    Number(row.CumulativeYield),
                    Number(row.CumulativeSales))
            Next

            AppendRow(csv, "Total", String.Empty, Number(projection.TotalYield), Number(projection.TotalSales))
            AppendRow(csv)
            AppendRow(csv, "Note", YieldCalculator.EstimateDisclaimer)

            Return csv.ToString()
        End Function

        Private Shared Sub AppendRow(csv As StringBuilder, ParamArray fields As String())
            ' RFC 4180 uses CRLF line endings.
            csv.Append(BuildRow(fields)).Append(ControlChars.CrLf)
        End Sub

        Private Shared Function Number(value As Decimal) As String
            ' No thousands separator, so Excel reads the cell as a number.
            Return value.ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
        End Function

        Private Shared Function Whole(value As Integer) As String
            Return value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 72 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\Formatters.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Globalization
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Turns numbers into the text shown on screen. Keeping all formatting in one
    ''' place makes the cards, grids, CSV file and printed report consistent.
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class Formatters

        ''' <summary>The Philippine peso sign, written with ChrW so the source file stays plain ASCII.</summary>
        Public Const PesoSign As String = ChrW(&H20B1)

        ''' <summary>A middle dot used as a separator in captions.</summary>
        Public Const Separator As String = "  " & ChrW(&HB7) & "  "

        ' Invariant culture gives "1,234.5" on every PC, whatever its regional settings are.
        Private Shared ReadOnly Culture As CultureInfo = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Formats whole pesos, for example 600000 becomes "(peso sign)600,000".</summary>
        Public Shared Function Peso(amount As Decimal) As String
            Return PesoSign & RoundToWholePeso(amount).ToString("#,##0", Culture)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Formats pesos with centavos, for example 80 becomes "(peso sign)80.00".</summary>
        Public Shared Function PesoWithCentavos(amount As Decimal) As String
            Return PesoSign & amount.ToString("#,##0.00", Culture)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Rounds to the nearest peso (0.5 rounds up, like a cashier would).</summary>
        Public Shared Function RoundToWholePeso(amount As Decimal) As Decimal
            Return Math.Round(amount, 0, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Formats a quantity with up to 2 decimals, for example 7500 becomes "7,500" and 37.5 becomes "37.5".</summary>
        Public Shared Function Quantity(value As Decimal) As String
            Return Math.Round(value, 2, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero).ToString("#,##0.##", Culture)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Formats a quantity followed by its unit, for example "7,500 kg".</summary>
        Public Shared Function Quantity(value As Decimal, unit As String) As String
            Dim number As String = Quantity(value)
            Return If(String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(unit), number, number & " " & unit.Trim())
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Formats a 0-1 fraction as a percentage, for example 0.1667 becomes "16.7%".</summary>
        Public Shared Function Percent(fraction As Decimal) As String
            Return Math.Round(fraction * 100D, 1, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero).ToString("0.#", Culture) & "%"
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Text for the "Production" column of the projection table.</summary>
        Public Shared Function ProductionStage(row As ProjectionYear, kind As HarvestType) As String
            If row Is Nothing Then
                Return String.Empty
            End If

            If kind = HarvestType.Rotation Then
                Return If(row.IsHarvestYear, "Harvest", "Growing")
            End If

            Return If(row.ProductionFactor <= 0D, "Not bearing yet", Percent(row.ProductionFactor))
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Formats a number of years, for example "1 year" or "10 years".</summary>
        Public Shared Function Years(count As Integer) As String
            Return If(count = 1, "1 year", count.ToString(Culture) & " years")
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Converts a UTC time from the database to local time text, for example "Oct 26, 2026 3:45 PM".</summary>
        Public Shared Function LocalDateTime(utc As DateTime) As String
            Return DateTime.SpecifyKind(utc, DateTimeKind.Utc).ToLocalTime().ToString("MMM d, yyyy h:mm tt", Culture)
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 73 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\PasswordHasher.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Security.Cryptography

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Hashes and verifies passwords with PBKDF2 (RFC 2898). Passwords are never stored;
    ''' only a random salt and the derived hash. Many iterations make each guess slow,
    ''' which protects the passwords even if the database file is copied.
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class PasswordHasher

        ''' <summary>Salt length in bytes (128 bits).</summary>
        Public Const SaltSize As Integer = 16

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Hash length in bytes. 20 bytes is the output size of HMAC-SHA1, the function
        ''' .NET Framework 4.5.2 uses inside PBKDF2, so one block is computed per iteration.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Const HashSize As Integer = 20

        ''' <summary>Iteration count for new hashes. Stored per user so it can be raised later.</summary>
        Public Const DefaultIterations As Integer = 100000

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Creates a new cryptographically random salt.</summary>
        Public Shared Function GenerateSalt() As Byte()
            Dim salt(SaltSize - 1) As Byte
            Using random As New RNGCryptoServiceProvider()
                random.GetBytes(salt)
            End Using

            Return salt
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Derives the PBKDF2 hash of a password.</summary>
        ''' <param name="password">The plain-text password.</param>
        ''' <param name="salt">The salt (at least 8 bytes).</param>
        ''' <param name="iterations">Number of PBKDF2 iterations.</param>
        Public Shared Function Hash(password As String, salt As Byte(), iterations As Integer) As Byte()
            If password Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(password))
            End If

            If salt Is Nothing OrElse salt.Length < 8 Then
                Throw New ArgumentException("The salt must be at least 8 bytes long.", NameOf(salt))
            End If

            If iterations < 1 Then
                Throw New ArgumentOutOfRangeException(NameOf(iterations))
            End If

            Using pbkdf2 As New Rfc2898DeriveBytes(password, salt, iterations)
                Return pbkdf2.GetBytes(HashSize)
            End Using
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns True when the password produces the expected hash.</summary>
        Public Shared Function Verify(password As String, salt As Byte(), iterations As Integer, expectedHash As Byte()) As Boolean
            If password Is Nothing OrElse salt Is Nothing OrElse expectedHash Is Nothing Then
                Return False
            End If

            Dim actualHash As Byte() = Hash(password, salt, iterations)
            Return FixedTimeEquals(actualHash, expectedHash)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Compares two byte arrays in constant time. A normal comparison stops at the first
        ''' different byte, and an attacker could measure that timing difference.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared Function FixedTimeEquals(left As Byte(), right As Byte()) As Boolean
            If left Is Nothing OrElse right Is Nothing OrElse left.Length <> right.Length Then
                Return False
            End If

            Dim difference As Integer = 0
            For i As Integer = 0 To left.Length - 1
                difference = difference Or (CInt(left(i)) Xor CInt(right(i)))
            Next

            Return difference = 0
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 74 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\PasswordPolicy.vb
====================================================================================================
Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>Rules a new administrator password must follow.</summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class PasswordPolicy

        ''' <summary>Minimum password length.</summary>
        Public Const MinLength As Integer = 8

        ''' <summary>Maximum password length (keeps hashing time bounded).</summary>
        Public Const MaxLength As Integer = 128

        ''' <summary>Text shown under the password box.</summary>
        Public Const Description As String = "At least 8 characters, with at least one letter and one number."

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Checks a proposed password. Returns Nothing when it is acceptable,
        ''' otherwise a message explaining what to change.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="password">The proposed password.</param>
        ''' <param name="username">The account name; the password may not equal it.</param>
        Public Shared Function Validate(password As String, username As String) As String
            If String.IsNullOrEmpty(password) Then
                Return "Enter a new password."
            End If

            If password.Length < MinLength Then
                Return String.Format("The password must be at least {0} characters long.", MinLength)
            End If

            If password.Length > MaxLength Then
                Return String.Format("The password must be {0} characters or fewer.", MaxLength)
            End If

            If Not password.Any(AddressOf Char.IsLetter) OrElse Not password.Any(AddressOf Char.IsDigit) Then
                Return "The password must contain at least one letter and one number."
            End If

            If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(username) AndAlso String.Equals(password, username, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) Then
                Return "The password must not be the same as the username."
            End If

            If String.Equals(password, AuthService.DefaultAdminPassword, StringComparison.Ordinal) Then
                Return "Choose a password other than the default password."
            End If

            Return Nothing
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 75 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\ScenarioService.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports TreeYield.Core.Data
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>Saves, lists and deletes scenarios (saved calculations).</summary>
    Public Class ScenarioService

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of a scenario name.</summary>
        Public Const MaxNameLength As Integer = 100

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of scenario notes.</summary>
        Public Const MaxNotesLength As Integer = 500

        Private ReadOnly _scenarios As ScenarioRepository

        ''' <summary>Creates the service.</summary>
        Public Sub New(scenarios As ScenarioRepository)
            If scenarios Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(scenarios))
            End If

            _scenarios = scenarios
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns all scenarios, newest first.</summary>
        Public Function GetAllAsync() As Task(Of List(Of Scenario))
            Return _scenarios.GetAllAsync()
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Validates and saves a new scenario. Returns its ID.</summary>
        ''' <exception cref="ValidationException">A field is invalid.</exception>
        Public Function SaveAsync(scenario As Scenario) As Task(Of Integer)
            Dim validation As ValidationResult = Validate(scenario)
            If Not validation.IsValid Then
                Throw New ValidationException(validation)
            End If

            scenario.Name = scenario.Name.Trim()
            scenario.Notes = If(String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(scenario.Notes), Nothing, scenario.Notes.Trim())
            Return _scenarios.InsertAsync(scenario)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Deletes a scenario.</summary>
        Public Function DeleteAsync(scenarioId As Integer) As Task
            Return _scenarios.DeleteAsync(scenarioId)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Checks a scenario before it is saved.</summary>
        Public Shared Function Validate(scenario As Scenario) As ValidationResult
            Dim result As New ValidationResult()
            If scenario Is Nothing Then
                result.Add("Scenario", "No scenario data was provided.")
                Return result
            End If

            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(scenario.Name) Then
                result.Add(NameOf(Scenario.Name), "Enter a name for the scenario.")
            ElseIf scenario.Name.Trim().Length > MaxNameLength Then
                result.Add(NameOf(Scenario.Name), String.Format("The name must be {0} characters or fewer.", MaxNameLength))
            End If

            If scenario.Notes IsNot Nothing AndAlso scenario.Notes.Trim().Length > MaxNotesLength Then
                result.Add(NameOf(Scenario.Notes), String.Format("Notes must be {0} characters or fewer.", MaxNotesLength))
            End If

            If scenario.SpeciesId <= 0 Then
                result.Add(NameOf(Scenario.SpeciesId), "Select a species first.")
            End If

            If scenario.TreeCount < YieldCalculator.MinTreeCount OrElse scenario.TreeCount > YieldCalculator.MaxTreeCount Then
                result.Add(NameOf(Scenario.TreeCount), String.Format("Tree count must be from {0:N0} to {1:N0}.",
                    YieldCalculator.MinTreeCount, YieldCalculator.MaxTreeCount))
            End If

            If scenario.ProjectionYears < YieldCalculator.MinProjectionYears OrElse scenario.ProjectionYears > YieldCalculator.MaxProjectionYears Then
                result.Add(NameOf(Scenario.ProjectionYears), String.Format("The projection must cover {0} to {1} years.",
                    YieldCalculator.MinProjectionYears, YieldCalculator.MaxProjectionYears))
            End If

            Return result
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 76 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\SpeciesService.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Globalization
Imports TreeYield.Core.Data
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Everything the UI can do with species records. Read operations are open to
    ''' everyone; changes require a signed-in administrator, are validated first,
    ''' and are written to the audit log.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class SpeciesService

        Private Const EntityName As String = "TreeSpecies"

        Private ReadOnly _species As SpeciesRepository
        Private ReadOnly _categories As CategoryRepository
        Private ReadOnly _scenarios As ScenarioRepository
        Private ReadOnly _audit As AuditLogRepository
        Private ReadOnly _initializer As DatabaseInitializer

        ''' <summary>Creates the service.</summary>
        Public Sub New(species As SpeciesRepository, categories As CategoryRepository, scenarios As ScenarioRepository,
                       audit As AuditLogRepository, initializer As DatabaseInitializer)
            If species Is Nothing Then Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(species))
            If categories Is Nothing Then Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(categories))
            If scenarios Is Nothing Then Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(scenarios))
            If audit Is Nothing Then Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(audit))
            If initializer Is Nothing Then Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(initializer))

            _species = species
            _categories = categories
            _scenarios = scenarios
            _audit = audit
            _initializer = initializer
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns all categories.</summary>
        Public Function GetCategoriesAsync() As Task(Of List(Of Category))
            Return _categories.GetAllAsync()
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns every species, sorted by name.</summary>
        Public Function GetAllAsync() As Task(Of List(Of TreeSpecies))
            Return _species.GetAllAsync()
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Searches species by text, optionally within one category.</summary>
        Public Function SearchAsync(searchText As String, categoryId As Integer?) As Task(Of List(Of TreeSpecies))
            Return _species.SearchAsync(searchText, categoryId)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns how many saved scenarios use the species.</summary>
        Public Function CountScenariosAsync(speciesId As Integer) As Task(Of Integer)
            Return _scenarios.CountBySpeciesAsync(speciesId)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Returns the recent audit log entries.</summary>
        Public Function GetAuditLogAsync(count As Integer) As Task(Of List(Of AuditEntry))
            Return _audit.GetRecentAsync(count)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Validates and saves a species: INSERT when <see cref="TreeSpecies.SpeciesId"/> is 0,
        ''' otherwise UPDATE. Returns the species ID.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <exception cref="UnauthorizedAccessException">No administrator is signed in.</exception>
        ''' <exception cref="ValidationException">A field is invalid.</exception>
        ''' <exception cref="DuplicateRecordException">The common name is already used.</exception>
        Public Async Function SaveAsync(species As TreeSpecies, actor As AppUser) As Task(Of Integer)
            RequireAdministrator(actor)

            Dim clean As TreeSpecies = Normalize(species)
            Dim validation As ValidationResult = SpeciesValidator.Validate(clean)
            If Not validation.IsValid Then
                Throw New ValidationException(validation)
            End If

            If clean.SpeciesId = 0 Then
                Dim newId As Integer = Await _species.InsertAsync(clean).ConfigureAwait(False)
                Await TryAuditAsync(actor, AuditActions.Create, newId, "Added species '" & clean.CommonName & "'.").ConfigureAwait(False)
                Return newId
            End If

            Await _species.UpdateAsync(clean).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Await TryAuditAsync(actor, AuditActions.Update, clean.SpeciesId, String.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                "Updated species '{0}' (yield {1} {2}/tree/yr, price {3}/{2}).",
                clean.CommonName, clean.YieldPerTreePerYear, clean.YieldUnit, clean.PricePerUnit)).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Return clean.SpeciesId
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Deletes a species and (through ON DELETE CASCADE) its saved scenarios.</summary>
        ''' <exception cref="UnauthorizedAccessException">No administrator is signed in.</exception>
        Public Async Function DeleteAsync(species As TreeSpecies, actor As AppUser) As Task
            RequireAdministrator(actor)
            If species Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(species))
            End If

            Dim scenarioCount As Integer = Await _scenarios.CountBySpeciesAsync(species.SpeciesId).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Await _species.DeleteAsync(species.SpeciesId).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Await TryAuditAsync(actor, AuditActions.Delete, species.SpeciesId, String.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                "Deleted species '{0}' and {1} saved scenario(s).", species.CommonName, scenarioCount)).ConfigureAwait(False)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Restores the original sample species (deletes all species and scenarios first).</summary>
        ''' <exception cref="UnauthorizedAccessException">No administrator is signed in.</exception>
        Public Async Function ResetSampleDataAsync(actor As AppUser) As Task
            RequireAdministrator(actor)
            Await _initializer.ResetSampleDataAsync().ConfigureAwait(False)
            Await TryAuditAsync(actor, AuditActions.ResetData, Nothing, "Restored the sample species data.").ConfigureAwait(False)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Returns a cleaned copy: text trimmed, empty optional text turned into Nothing,
        ''' and for rotation species "full production" set equal to the rotation age.
        ''' </summary>
        Public Shared Function Normalize(species As TreeSpecies) As TreeSpecies
            If species Is Nothing Then
                Return Nothing
            End If

            Dim copy As TreeSpecies = species.Clone()
            copy.CommonName = TrimToNothing(copy.CommonName)
            copy.ScientificName = TrimToNothing(copy.ScientificName)
            copy.ProductType = TrimToNothing(copy.ProductType)
            copy.YieldUnit = TrimToNothing(copy.YieldUnit)
            copy.HarvestSeason = TrimToNothing(copy.HarvestSeason)
            copy.Notes = TrimToNothing(copy.Notes)

            If copy.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation Then
                copy.YearsToFullProduction = copy.YearsToFirstHarvest
            End If

            Return copy
        End Function

        Private Shared Function TrimToNothing(value As String) As String
            If value Is Nothing Then
                Return Nothing
            End If

            Dim trimmed As String = value.Trim()
            Return If(trimmed.Length = 0, Nothing, trimmed)
        End Function

        Private Shared Sub RequireAdministrator(actor As AppUser)
            ' The UI hides the admin screens from guests; this check makes sure the
            ' rule also holds if a service method is ever called from somewhere else.
            If actor Is Nothing OrElse actor.UserId <= 0 Then
                Throw New UnauthorizedAccessException("You must be signed in as an administrator to change species records.")
            End If
        End Sub

        Private Async Function TryAuditAsync(actor As AppUser, action As String, entityId As Integer?, details As String) As Task
            Try
                Await _audit.AddAsync(actor.UserId, action, EntityName, entityId, details).ConfigureAwait(False)
            Catch ex As DataAccessException
                ' The data change already succeeded; a failed log entry must not undo it.
                Trace.TraceWarning("Audit log write failed: " & ex.ToString())
            End Try
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 77 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\SpeciesValidator.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Business rules for a tree species record. These checks run before anything is
    ''' sent to the database. The database repeats the most important rules as CHECK
    ''' and UNIQUE constraints, so bad data is blocked even if this code is bypassed -
    ''' for example when someone edits the table directly in Microsoft Access
    ''' (defense in depth).
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class SpeciesValidator

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of <see cref="TreeSpecies.CommonName"/> (matches TEXT(100)).</summary>
        Public Const MaxCommonNameLength As Integer = 100

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of <see cref="TreeSpecies.ScientificName"/>.</summary>
        Public Const MaxScientificNameLength As Integer = 150

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of <see cref="TreeSpecies.ProductType"/>.</summary>
        Public Const MaxProductTypeLength As Integer = 60

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of <see cref="TreeSpecies.YieldUnit"/>.</summary>
        Public Const MaxYieldUnitLength As Integer = 20

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of <see cref="TreeSpecies.HarvestSeason"/>.</summary>
        Public Const MaxHarvestSeasonLength As Integer = 100

        ''' <summary>Maximum length of <see cref="TreeSpecies.Notes"/>.</summary>
        Public Const MaxNotesLength As Integer = 1000

        ''' <summary>Largest yield per tree per year accepted.</summary>
        Public Const MaxYieldPerTree As Decimal = 100000D

        ''' <summary>Largest price per unit accepted, in pesos.</summary>
        Public Const MaxPricePerUnit As Decimal = 1000000D

        ''' <summary>Largest number of years accepted for first harvest / full production.</summary>
        Public Const MaxYears As Integer = 60

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Checks every field and returns all problems found.</summary>
        ''' <param name="species">The record to check.</param>
        Public Shared Function Validate(species As TreeSpecies) As ValidationResult
            Dim result As New ValidationResult()
            If species Is Nothing Then
                result.Add("Species", "No species data was provided.")
                Return result
            End If

            RequireText(result, NameOf(TreeSpecies.CommonName), "Common name", species.CommonName, MaxCommonNameLength)
            OptionalText(result, NameOf(TreeSpecies.ScientificName), "Scientific name", species.ScientificName, MaxScientificNameLength)

            If species.CategoryId <= 0 Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.CategoryId), "Select a category.")
            End If

            RequireText(result, NameOf(TreeSpecies.ProductType), "Product", species.ProductType, MaxProductTypeLength)
            RequireText(result, NameOf(TreeSpecies.YieldUnit), "Yield unit", species.YieldUnit, MaxYieldUnitLength)

            If species.YieldPerTreePerYear <= 0D Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YieldPerTreePerYear), "Yield per tree must be greater than zero.")
            ElseIf species.YieldPerTreePerYear > MaxYieldPerTree Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YieldPerTreePerYear),
                    String.Format("Yield per tree cannot be more than {0:N0}.", MaxYieldPerTree))
            ElseIf HasMoreThanTwoDecimals(species.YieldPerTreePerYear) Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YieldPerTreePerYear), "Yield per tree can have at most 2 decimal places.")
            End If

            If species.PricePerUnit < 0D Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.PricePerUnit), "Price cannot be negative.")
            ElseIf species.PricePerUnit > MaxPricePerUnit Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.PricePerUnit),
                    String.Format("Price cannot be more than {0:N0}.", MaxPricePerUnit))
            ElseIf HasMoreThanTwoDecimals(species.PricePerUnit) Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.PricePerUnit), "Price can have at most 2 decimal places.")
            End If

            If Not [Enum].IsDefined(GetType(HarvestType), species.HarvestType) Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.HarvestType), "Select a harvest type.")
            End If

            ValidateYears(result, species)

            OptionalText(result, NameOf(TreeSpecies.HarvestSeason), "Harvest season", species.HarvestSeason, MaxHarvestSeasonLength)
            OptionalText(result, NameOf(TreeSpecies.Notes), "Notes", species.Notes, MaxNotesLength)

            Return result
        End Function

        Private Shared Sub ValidateYears(result As ValidationResult, species As TreeSpecies)
            Dim isRotation As Boolean = species.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation
            Dim firstLabel As String = If(isRotation, "Rotation age", "Years to first harvest")

            If species.YearsToFirstHarvest < 0 OrElse species.YearsToFirstHarvest > MaxYears Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YearsToFirstHarvest),
                    String.Format("{0} must be from 0 to {1}.", firstLabel, MaxYears))
            ElseIf isRotation AndAlso species.YearsToFirstHarvest < 1 Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YearsToFirstHarvest), "Rotation age must be at least 1 year.")
            End If

            If species.YearsToFullProduction < 0 OrElse species.YearsToFullProduction > MaxYears Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YearsToFullProduction),
                    String.Format("Years to full production must be from 0 to {0}.", MaxYears))
            ElseIf species.YearsToFullProduction < species.YearsToFirstHarvest Then
                result.Add(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YearsToFullProduction),
                    "Full production cannot come before the first harvest.")
            End If
        End Sub

        Private Shared Sub RequireText(result As ValidationResult, field As String, label As String, value As String, maxLength As Integer)
            If String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) Then
                result.Add(field, label & " is required.")
                Return
            End If

            OptionalText(result, field, label, value, maxLength)
        End Sub

        Private Shared Sub OptionalText(result As ValidationResult, field As String, label As String, value As String, maxLength As Integer)
            If String.IsNullOrEmpty(value) Then
                Return
            End If

            If value.Trim().Length > maxLength Then
                result.Add(field, String.Format("{0} must be {1} characters or fewer.", label, maxLength))
            ElseIf value.Any(Function(c) Char.IsControl(c) AndAlso c <> ControlChars.Cr AndAlso c <> ControlChars.Lf AndAlso c <> ControlChars.Tab) Then
                ' Control characters (other than line breaks and tabs) are never typed on
                ' purpose; they usually come from pasted binary data.
                result.Add(field, label & " contains characters that are not allowed.")
            End If
        End Sub

        Private Shared Function HasMoreThanTwoDecimals(value As Decimal) As Boolean
            ' The database columns are DECIMAL(12,2); extra decimals would be rounded silently.
            Return Decimal.Round(value, 2) <> value
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace

            MyBase.New(If(result Is Nothing, "The data is not valid.", result.ToString()))
            Me.Result = If(result, New ValidationResult())
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>The individual field errors.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Result As ValidationResult

    End Class
