FILE 79 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\ValidationResult.vb
====================================================================================================
Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>One validation problem tied to a field (property name) of the object being checked.</summary>
    Public Class ValidationError

        ''' <summary>Creates an error for a field.</summary>
        Public Sub New(field As String, message As String)
            Me.Field = field
            Me.Message = message
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Property name, for example "CommonName". The UI uses it to find the input box.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Field As String

        ''' <summary>Message for the user, for example "Common name is required."</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Message As String

    End Class

    ''' <summary>
    ''' A list of validation errors. Validators return this instead of throwing on the
    ''' first problem, so the form can highlight every invalid field at once.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class ValidationResult

        Private ReadOnly _errors As New List(Of ValidationError)()

        ''' <summary>True when no errors were added.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property IsValid As Boolean
            Get
                Return _errors.Count = 0
            End Get
        End Property

        ''' <summary>All errors in the order they were found.</summary>
        Public ReadOnly Property Errors As IReadOnlyList(Of ValidationError)
            Get
                Return _errors
            End Get
        End Property

        ''' <summary>Adds an error for a field.</summary>
        Public Sub Add(field As String, message As String)
            _errors.Add(New ValidationError(field, message))
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Returns the first error message for a field, or Nothing if the field is valid.</summary>
        Public Function GetError(field As String) As String
            Dim found As ValidationError = _errors.FirstOrDefault(Function(e) e.Field = field)
            Return If(found Is Nothing, Nothing, found.Message)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>All messages, one per line.</summary>
        Public Overrides Function ToString() As String
            Return String.Join(Environment.NewLine, _errors.Select(Function(e) e.Message))
        End Function

    End Class

End Namespace


====================================================================================================
FILE 80 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Core\Services\YieldCalculator.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

Namespace Services

    ''' <summary>
    ''' The calculation engine. Every method is a pure function (no database, no UI),
    ''' so the same inputs always give the same outputs and the logic is unit-tested.
    ''' All money and quantities use <see cref="Decimal"/> to avoid floating-point
    ''' rounding errors (for example 0.1 + 0.2 &lt;&gt; 0.3 with Double).
    ''' </summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class YieldCalculator

        ''' <summary>Smallest tree count accepted.</summary>
        Public Const MinTreeCount As Integer = 1

        ''' <summary>Largest tree count accepted.</summary>
        Public Const MaxTreeCount As Integer = 100000

        ''' <summary>Shortest projection horizon in years.</summary>
        Public Const MinProjectionYears As Integer = 1

        ''' <summary>Longest projection horizon in years.</summary>
        Public Const MaxProjectionYears As Integer = 30

        ''' <summary>Horizon used when the user has not chosen one.</summary>
        Public Const DefaultProjectionYears As Integer = 10

        ''' <summary>Shown beside every result, in the UI, the CSV file and the printed report.</summary>
        Public Const EstimateDisclaimer As String =
            "Figures are planning estimates based on sample yield and farm-gate price data. " &
            "Actual results vary with variety, site, farm management, weather and market prices."

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members: this class is never instantiated.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Calculates the yearly yield and gross sales at full production.
        ''' <para>Annual yield = tree count x yield per tree per year.</para>
        ''' <para>Gross sales = annual yield x price per unit.</para>
        ''' Example: 25 Carabao Mango trees x 300 kg = 7,500 kg; 7,500 kg x PHP 80 = PHP 600,000.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="species">The species to calculate. Must not be Nothing.</param>
        ''' <param name="treeCount">Number of trees, from <see cref="MinTreeCount"/> to <see cref="MaxTreeCount"/>.</param>
        ''' <exception cref="ArgumentNullException">species is Nothing.</exception>
        ''' <exception cref="ArgumentOutOfRangeException">treeCount is outside the allowed range.</exception>
        Public Shared Function Calculate(species As TreeSpecies, treeCount As Integer) As YieldResult
            EnsureSpecies(species)
            EnsureTreeCount(treeCount)

            Dim annualYield As Decimal = treeCount * species.YieldPerTreePerYear
            Dim result As New YieldResult With {
                .Species = species,
                .TreeCount = treeCount,
                .AnnualYield = annualYield,
                .GrossSales = annualYield * species.PricePerUnit
            }

            If species.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation Then
                ' Timber is sold only once, at rotation age, so the "annual" numbers above
                ' are averages (mean annual increment). The real cash event is the harvest:
                ' everything the trees grew during the whole rotation is cut at once.
                Dim rotationAge As Integer = GetRotationAge(species)
                result.IsAnnualized = True
                result.HarvestYear = rotationAge
                result.HarvestYield = annualYield * rotationAge
                result.HarvestSales = result.HarvestYield * species.PricePerUnit
            End If

            Return result
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Returns how much of full production an annual species reaches in a given year
        ''' after planting, as a fraction from 0 to 1.
        ''' <list type="bullet">
        ''' <item>Before the first harvest year the trees are still growing: 0.</item>
        ''' <item>From the first harvest to full production the yield rises in equal steps (linear ramp).</item>
        ''' <item>From the full-production year onward: 1 (100%).</item>
        ''' </list>
        ''' Example: first harvest in year 5, full production in year 8 gives
        ''' 25%, 50%, 75% and 100% for years 5, 6, 7 and 8.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="species">The species. Must not be Nothing.</param>
        ''' <param name="year">Year after planting, starting at 1.</param>
        Public Shared Function GetProductionFactor(species As TreeSpecies, year As Integer) As Decimal
            EnsureSpecies(species)
            If year < 1 Then
                Throw New ArgumentOutOfRangeException(NameOf(year), "The year must be 1 or later.")
            End If

            ' A value of 0 years means "already bearing", which is the same as year 1.
            Dim firstYear As Integer = Math.Max(1, species.YearsToFirstHarvest)
            Dim fullYear As Integer = Math.Max(firstYear, species.YearsToFullProduction)

            If year < firstYear Then
                Return 0D
            End If

            If year >= fullYear Then
                Return 1D
            End If

            ' Number of bearing years so far divided by the length of the ramp-up period.
            ' CDec makes this a Decimal division (the / operator on Integers gives a Double).
            Return CDec(year - firstYear + 1) / (fullYear - firstYear + 1)
        End Function

        ''' <summary>
        ''' Builds a year-by-year projection of yield and gross sales.
        ''' Annual species follow <see cref="GetProductionFactor"/>. Rotation species
        ''' earn nothing until rotation age and then the whole stand is cut; the projection
        ''' assumes the land is replanted at once, so harvests repeat every rotation.
        ''' </summary>
        ''' <param name="species">The species. Must not be Nothing.</param>
        ''' <param name="treeCount">Number of trees.</param>
        ''' <param name="years">Projection horizon, from <see cref="MinProjectionYears"/> to <see cref="MaxProjectionYears"/>.</param>
        Public Shared Function Project(species As TreeSpecies, treeCount As Integer, years As Integer) As YieldProjection
            EnsureSpecies(species)
            EnsureTreeCount(treeCount)
            If years < MinProjectionYears OrElse years > MaxProjectionYears Then
                Throw New ArgumentOutOfRangeException(NameOf(years),
                    String.Format("The projection must cover {0} to {1} years.", MinProjectionYears, MaxProjectionYears))
            End If

            Dim fullAnnualYield As Decimal = treeCount * species.YieldPerTreePerYear
            Dim rotationAge As Integer = GetRotationAge(species)
            Dim isRotation As Boolean = species.HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation

            Dim rows As New List(Of ProjectionYear)(years)
            Dim cumulativeYield As Decimal = 0D
            Dim cumulativeSales As Decimal = 0D

            For year As Integer = 1 To years
                Dim factor As Decimal
                Dim yieldThisYear As Decimal

                If isRotation Then
                    ' Harvest happens in years that are multiples of the rotation age.
                    Dim harvestYear As Boolean = (year Mod rotationAge = 0)
                    factor = If(harvestYear, 1D, 0D)
                    yieldThisYear = If(harvestYear, fullAnnualYield * rotationAge, 0D)
                Else
                    factor = GetProductionFactor(species, year)
                    yieldThisYear = fullAnnualYield * factor
                End If

                Dim sales As Decimal = yieldThisYear * species.PricePerUnit
                cumulativeYield += yieldThisYear
                cumulativeSales += sales

                rows.Add(New ProjectionYear With {
                    .Year = year,
                    .ProductionFactor = factor,
                    .IsHarvestYear = factor > 0D,
                    .Yield = yieldThisYear,
                    .GrossSales = sales,
                    .CumulativeYield = cumulativeYield,
                    .CumulativeSales = cumulativeSales
                })
            Next

            Dim firstHarvestYear As Integer = If(isRotation, rotationAge, Math.Max(1, species.YearsToFirstHarvest))

            Return New YieldProjection With {
                .Species = species,
                .TreeCount = treeCount,
                .Years = years,
                .Rows = rows,
                .TotalYield = cumulativeYield,
                .TotalSales = cumulativeSales,
                .FirstHarvestYear = firstHarvestYear,
                .FirstHarvestBeyondHorizon = firstHarvestYear > years
            }
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Rotation age in years; never less than 1 so the Mod above is safe.</summary>
        Private Shared Function GetRotationAge(species As TreeSpecies) As Integer
            Return Math.Max(1, species.YearsToFirstHarvest)
        End Function

        Private Shared Sub EnsureSpecies(species As TreeSpecies)
            If species Is Nothing Then
                Throw New ArgumentNullException(NameOf(species))
            End If

            ' The database CHECK constraints already block these values; this guard
            ' protects the calculator if it is ever called with unsaved data.
            If species.YieldPerTreePerYear < 0D OrElse species.PricePerUnit < 0D Then
                Throw New ArgumentException("Yield and price cannot be negative.", NameOf(species))
            End If
        End Sub

        Private Shared Sub EnsureTreeCount(treeCount As Integer)
            If treeCount < MinTreeCount OrElse treeCount > MaxTreeCount Then
                Throw New ArgumentOutOfRangeException(NameOf(treeCount),
                    String.Format("Tree count must be from {0:N0} to {1:N0}.", MinTreeCount, MaxTreeCount))
            End If
        End Sub

    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 83 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Tests\CsvExporterTests.vb

Imports System.Text.RegularExpressions
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services
Imports TreeYield.Tests.Framework

''' <summary>Tests for CSV quoting and formula-injection protection in <see cref="CsvExporter"/>.</summary>
Public Class CsvExporterTests

    <Test>
    Public Sub PlainText_IsUnchanged()
        Assert.AreEqual("Carabao Mango", CsvExporter.EscapeField("Carabao Mango"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub TextWithComma_IsQuoted()
        Assert.AreEqual("""Mango, Carabao""", CsvExporter.EscapeField("Mango, Carabao"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub InnerQuotes_AreDoubled()
        Assert.AreEqual("""Mangifera indica """"Carabao""""""", CsvExporter.EscapeField("Mangifera indica ""Carabao"""))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub LineBreak_IsQuoted()
        Assert.AreEqual("""line1" & vbLf & "line2""", CsvExporter.EscapeField("line1" & vbLf & "line2"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub FormulaCharacters_ArePrefixedWithApostrophe()
        Assert.AreEqual("'=1+1", CsvExporter.EscapeField("=1+1"))
        Assert.AreEqual("'+63 912", CsvExporter.EscapeField("+63 912"))
        Assert.AreEqual("'-2", CsvExporter.EscapeField("-2"))
        Assert.AreEqual("'@SUM(A1)", CsvExporter.EscapeField("@SUM(A1)"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub FormulaWithComma_IsPrefixedAndQuoted()
        Assert.AreEqual("""'=HYPERLINK(""""http://x"""",""""click"""")""",
            CsvExporter.EscapeField("=HYPERLINK(""http://x"",""click"")"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub NothingOrEmpty_BecomesEmptyCell()
        Assert.AreEqual(String.Empty, CsvExporter.EscapeField(Nothing))
        Assert.AreEqual(String.Empty, CsvExporter.EscapeField(String.Empty))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub BuildReport_ContainsInputsResultsAndEveryYear()
        Dim mango As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        Dim result As YieldResult = YieldCalculator.Calculate(mango, 25)
        Dim projection As YieldProjection = YieldCalculator.Project(mango, 25, 10)

        Dim csv As String = CsvExporter.BuildReport(result, projection, New DateTime(2026, 10, 26, 9, 30, 0))
        Dim lines As String() = csv.Split(New String() {vbCrLf}, StringSplitOptions.None)

        Assert.IsTrue(lines.Contains("Species,Carabao Mango"))
        Assert.IsTrue(lines.Contains("Tree count,25"))
        Assert.IsTrue(lines.Contains("Estimated annual gross sales (PHP),600000.00"))
        Assert.IsTrue(lines.Contains("Generated,2026-10-26 09:30"))
        ' Year rows look like "5,16.7%,1250.00,..." - digits followed by a comma.
        Assert.AreEqual(10, lines.Count(Function(line) Regex.IsMatch(line, "^\d+,")), "one row per projected year")
    End Sub

End Class

FILE 84 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Tests\DatabaseIntegrationTests.vb

Imports System.Data
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports TreeYield.Core.Data
Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services
Imports TreeYield.Tests.Framework

''' <summary>
''' End-to-end tests against a real Microsoft Access database file (a throw-away .mdb
''' in the temp folder). They prove that the SQL, constraints, relationships, cascade
''' rules and security features work, not just the VB code.
''' </summary>
Public Class DatabaseIntegrationTests

    ''' <summary>Number of species in SeedData.</summary>
    Private Const SeedSpeciesCount As Integer = 22

    ' Low iteration count for throw-away test accounts keeps the run fast.
    Private Const TestIterations As Integer = 1000

    Private ReadOnly _factory As AccessConnectionFactory
    Private ReadOnly _speciesRepository As SpeciesRepository
    Private ReadOnly _users As UserRepository
    Private ReadOnly _audit As AuditLogRepository
    Private ReadOnly _speciesService As SpeciesService
    Private ReadOnly _scenarioService As ScenarioService
    Private ReadOnly _auth As AuthService

    Public Sub New()
        _factory = TestDatabase.Factory
        _speciesRepository = New SpeciesRepository(_factory)
        _users = New UserRepository(_factory)
        _audit = New AuditLogRepository(_factory)
        Dim scenarios As New ScenarioRepository(_factory)
        _speciesService = New SpeciesService(_speciesRepository, New CategoryRepository(_factory), scenarios, _audit,
                                             New DatabaseInitializer(_factory))
        _scenarioService = New ScenarioService(scenarios)
        _auth = New AuthService(_users, _audit)
    End Sub

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Sub NewDatabase_IsAnAccessFileWithTablesAndRelationships()
        Assert.IsTrue(_factory.DatabasePath.EndsWith(".mdb", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))

        Using connection As OleDbConnection = _factory.Open()
            Dim tables As DataTable = connection.GetOleDbSchemaTable(OleDbSchemaGuid.Tables, New Object() {Nothing, Nothing, Nothing, "TABLE"})
            Dim names As List(Of String) = tables.Rows.Cast(Of DataRow)().Select(Function(r) CStr(r("TABLE_NAME"))).ToList()
            For Each table As String In {"SchemaVersion", "Categories", "TreeSpecies", "Users", "Scenarios", "AuditLog"}
                Assert.IsTrue(names.Contains(table), "table " & table)
            Next

            ' The three foreign keys are real Access relationships (visible in Database Tools > Relationships).
            Dim keys As DataTable = connection.GetOleDbSchemaTable(OleDbSchemaGuid.Foreign_Keys, Nothing)
            Assert.AreEqual(3, keys.Rows.Count, "relationships")
        End Using
    End Sub

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Initialize_CreatesCategoriesAndSampleSpecies() As Task
        Dim categories As List(Of Category) = Await _speciesService.GetCategoriesAsync()
        Dim species As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.GetAllAsync()

        Assert.AreEqual(2, categories.Count)
        Assert.IsTrue(species.Count >= SeedSpeciesCount, "at least the seeded species")

        Dim mango As TreeSpecies = species.Single(Function(s) s.CommonName = "Carabao Mango")
        Assert.AreEqual(300D, mango.YieldPerTreePerYear)
        Assert.AreEqual(80D, mango.PricePerUnit)
        Assert.AreEqual(Category.FruitBearing, mango.CategoryName)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Initialize_RunTwice_DoesNotDuplicateData() As Task
        Dim initializer As New DatabaseInitializer(_factory)
        Await initializer.InitializeAsync()

        Dim species As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.GetAllAsync()
        Assert.AreEqual(1, species.Where(Function(s) s.CommonName = "Carabao Mango").Count())
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function DefaultAdmin_IsCreated_AndMustChangePassword() As Task
        Dim admin As AppUser = Await _users.GetByUsernameAsync(AuthService.DefaultAdminUsername)

        Assert.IsNotNull(admin)
        Assert.IsTrue(admin.MustChangePassword)
        Assert.IsFalse(admin.PasswordHash.SequenceEqual(Text.Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(AuthService.DefaultAdminPassword)),
                       "the password must never be stored as plain text")
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Search_FindsPartialNameIgnoringCase() As Task
        Dim results As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("mAnGo", Nothing)
        Assert.IsTrue(results.Any(Function(s) s.CommonName = "Carabao Mango"))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Search_ByCategory_ReturnsOnlyThatCategory() As Task
        Dim nonFruit As Category = (Await _speciesService.GetCategoriesAsync()).Single(Function(c) c.Name = Category.NonFruitBearing)

        Dim results As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.SearchAsync(Nothing, nonFruit.CategoryId)

        Assert.IsTrue(results.Count > 0)
        Assert.IsTrue(results.All(Function(s) s.CategoryId = nonFruit.CategoryId))
        Assert.IsTrue(results.Any(Function(s) s.CommonName = "Mahogany"))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Search_SqlInjectionText_IsTreatedAsPlainText() As Task
        Dim orTrick As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("' OR 1=1 --", Nothing)
        Dim dropTrick As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("'; DROP TABLE TreeSpecies; --", Nothing)

        Assert.AreEqual(0, orTrick.Count, "the OR 1=1 trick must not return every row")
        Assert.AreEqual(0, dropTrick.Count)
        Assert.IsTrue((Await _speciesService.GetAllAsync()).Count >= SeedSpeciesCount, "the table must still exist")
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Search_WildcardCharacters_AreMatchedLiterally() As Task
        Assert.AreEqual(0, (Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("%", Nothing)).Count)
        Assert.AreEqual(0, (Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("_", Nothing)).Count)
        Assert.AreEqual(0, (Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("[", Nothing)).Count)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Save_WithoutAdministrator_IsRefused() As Task
        Await Assert.ThrowsAsync(Of UnauthorizedAccessException)(Function() _speciesService.SaveAsync(NewSpecies("Guest Tree"), Nothing))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Save_InvalidSpecies_ThrowsValidationException() As Task
        Dim admin As AppUser = Await GetAdminAsync()
        Dim bad As TreeSpecies = NewSpecies("Invalid Tree")
        bad.PricePerUnit = -10D

        Dim ex As ValidationException = Await Assert.ThrowsAsync(Of ValidationException)(Function() _speciesService.SaveAsync(bad, admin))
        Assert.IsNotNull(ex.Result.GetError(NameOf(TreeSpecies.PricePerUnit)))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Save_InsertUpdateDelete_RoundTripAndAudit() As Task
        Dim admin As AppUser = Await GetAdminAsync()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = NewSpecies("Test Santol " & Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 6))

        Dim id As Integer = Await _speciesService.SaveAsync(species, admin)
        Dim saved As TreeSpecies = Await _speciesRepository.GetByIdAsync(id)
        Assert.AreEqual(species.CommonName, saved.CommonName)
        Assert.AreEqual(55.25D, saved.YieldPerTreePerYear)

        saved.PricePerUnit = 99.5D
        Await _speciesService.SaveAsync(saved, admin)
        Assert.AreEqual(99.5D, (Await _speciesRepository.GetByIdAsync(id)).PricePerUnit)

        Await _speciesService.DeleteAsync(saved, admin)
        Assert.IsNull(Await _speciesRepository.GetByIdAsync(id))

        Dim log As List(Of AuditEntry) = Await _audit.GetRecentAsync(20)
        Assert.IsTrue(log.Any(Function(e) e.Action = AuditActions.Create AndAlso e.EntityId.GetValueOrDefault() = id))
        Assert.IsTrue(log.Any(Function(e) e.Action = AuditActions.Update AndAlso e.EntityId.GetValueOrDefault() = id))
        Assert.IsTrue(log.Any(Function(e) e.Action = AuditActions.Delete AndAlso e.EntityId.GetValueOrDefault() = id))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Save_DuplicateName_ThrowsDuplicateRecordException() As Task
        Dim admin As AppUser = Await GetAdminAsync()
        ' Access compares text case-insensitively, so a different case is still a duplicate.
        Await Assert.ThrowsAsync(Of DuplicateRecordException)(Function() _speciesService.SaveAsync(NewSpecies("CARABAO MANGO"), admin))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Database_CheckConstraint_BlocksBadValueEvenWithoutValidator() As Task
        ' Goes straight to the repository, skipping the VB validator, to prove the
        ' Access file itself rejects the value (defense in depth).
        Dim bad As TreeSpecies = NewSpecies("Constraint Test")
        bad.YieldPerTreePerYear = -1D

        Dim ex As DataAccessException = Await Assert.ThrowsAsync(Of DataAccessException)(Function() _speciesRepository.InsertAsync(bad))
        Assert.IsFalse(TypeOf ex Is DuplicateRecordException)
        Assert.AreEqual(AccessErrorTranslator.ValidationRuleBroken, DirectCast(ex.InnerException, OleDbException).Errors(0).SQLState)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function DeleteSpecies_AlsoDeletesItsScenarios() As Task
        Dim admin As AppUser = Await GetAdminAsync()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = NewSpecies("Cascade Test " & Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 6))
        species.SpeciesId = Await _speciesService.SaveAsync(species, admin)

        Await _scenarioService.SaveAsync(New Scenario With {.Name = "Plan A", .SpeciesId = species.SpeciesId, .TreeCount = 10, .ProjectionYears = 10})
        Await _scenarioService.SaveAsync(New Scenario With {.Name = "Plan B", .SpeciesId = species.SpeciesId, .TreeCount = 20, .ProjectionYears = 5})
        Assert.AreEqual(2, Await _speciesService.CountScenariosAsync(species.SpeciesId))

        Await _speciesService.DeleteAsync(species, admin)

        Assert.AreEqual(0, Await _speciesService.CountScenariosAsync(species.SpeciesId))
        Assert.IsFalse((Await _scenarioService.GetAllAsync()).Any(Function(s) s.SpeciesId = species.SpeciesId))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Scenario_SaveAndList_IncludesSpeciesName() As Task
        Dim mango As TreeSpecies = (Await _speciesService.SearchAsync("Carabao Mango", Nothing)).Single()
        Dim id As Integer = Await _scenarioService.SaveAsync(New Scenario With {
            .Name = "  Backyard mango  ",
            .SpeciesId = mango.SpeciesId,
            .TreeCount = 25,
            .ProjectionYears = 10
        })

        Dim saved As Scenario = (Await _scenarioService.GetAllAsync()).Single(Function(s) s.ScenarioId = id)
        Assert.AreEqual("Backyard mango", saved.Name)
        Assert.AreEqual("Carabao Mango", saved.SpeciesName)

        Await _scenarioService.DeleteAsync(id)
        Assert.IsFalse((Await _scenarioService.GetAllAsync()).Any(Function(s) s.ScenarioId = id))
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Login_UnknownUser_GetsGenericMessage() As Task
        Dim result As LoginResult = Await _auth.LoginAsync("nobody_" & Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"), "whatever1")

        Assert.AreEqual(LoginStatus.InvalidCredentials, result.Status)
        Assert.AreEqual("Invalid username or password.", result.Message)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Login_EmptyInput_IsRejected() As Task
        Assert.AreEqual(LoginStatus.MissingInput, (Await _auth.LoginAsync("", "")).Status)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Login_CorrectPassword_Succeeds() As Task
        Dim username As String = Await CreateUserAsync("Correct123")

        Dim result As LoginResult = Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "Correct123")

        Assert.IsTrue(result.Succeeded, result.Message)
        Assert.AreEqual(username, result.User.Username)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function Login_FiveWrongPasswords_LocksAccountUntilExpiry() As Task
        Dim username As String = Await CreateUserAsync("Correct123")

        For attempt As Integer = 1 To AuthService.MaxFailedAttempts - 1
            Assert.AreEqual(LoginStatus.InvalidCredentials, (Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "wrong" & attempt.ToString())).Status)
        Next

        Assert.AreEqual(LoginStatus.LockedOut, (Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "wrong5")).Status, "5th failure locks")
        Assert.AreEqual(LoginStatus.LockedOut, (Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "Correct123")).Status,
                        "even the right password is refused while locked")

        ' Simulate the 5 minutes passing by moving the lock end time into the past.
        Using connection As OleDbConnection = _factory.Open()
            Using command As New OleDbCommand("UPDATE Users SET LockoutUntil = ? WHERE Username = ?", connection)
                command.Parameters.Add("?", OleDbType.Date).Value = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(-1)
                command.Parameters.Add("?", OleDbType.VarWChar, 50).Value = username
                command.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End Using
        End Using

        Assert.IsTrue((Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "Correct123")).Succeeded, "works again after the lock expires")
        Assert.AreEqual(0, (Await _users.GetByUsernameAsync(username)).FailedLoginCount)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function ChangePassword_EnforcesRulesAndStoresNewHash() As Task
        Dim username As String = Await CreateUserAsync("Correct123")
        Dim user As AppUser = (Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "Correct123")).User

        Assert.AreEqual("The current password is incorrect.", Await _auth.ChangePasswordAsync(user, "nope", "Newpass2026", "Newpass2026"))
        Assert.IsNotNull(Await _auth.ChangePasswordAsync(user, "Correct123", "short1", "short1"))
        Assert.IsNotNull(Await _auth.ChangePasswordAsync(user, "Correct123", "Newpass2026", "Different2026"))
        Assert.IsNull(Await _auth.ChangePasswordAsync(user, "Correct123", "Newpass2026", "Newpass2026"))

        Assert.IsFalse((Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "Correct123")).Succeeded, "old password no longer works")
        Assert.IsTrue((Await _auth.LoginAsync(username, "Newpass2026")).Succeeded)
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function FileOpenExclusivelyElsewhere_GivesFriendlyMessage() As Task
        ' Simulates Microsoft Access holding the file open in exclusive mode.
        OleDbConnection.ReleaseObjectPool()
        Using exclusive As New OleDbConnection(_factory.ConnectionString & ";Mode=Share Exclusive")
            exclusive.Open()
            Dim ex As DataAccessException = Await Assert.ThrowsAsync(Of DataAccessException)(Function() _speciesService.GetAllAsync())
            Assert.IsTrue(ex.Message.Contains("in use"), ex.Message)
        End Using

        OleDbConnection.ReleaseObjectPool()
        Assert.IsTrue((Await _speciesService.GetAllAsync()).Count > 0, "works again once the file is closed")
    End Function

    <IntegrationTest>
    Public Async Function ResetSampleData_RestoresOriginalSpecies() As Task
        Dim admin As AppUser = Await GetAdminAsync()
        Await _speciesService.SaveAsync(NewSpecies("Temporary Tree"), admin)

        Await _speciesService.ResetSampleDataAsync(admin)

        Dim species As List(Of TreeSpecies) = Await _speciesService.GetAllAsync()
        Assert.AreEqual(SeedSpeciesCount, species.Count)
        Assert.IsFalse(species.Any(Function(s) s.CommonName = "Temporary Tree"))
        Assert.AreEqual(0, (Await _scenarioService.GetAllAsync()).Count)
    End Function

    Private Function GetAdminAsync() As Task(Of AppUser)
        Return _users.GetByUsernameAsync(AuthService.DefaultAdminUsername)
    End Function

    Private Async Function CreateUserAsync(password As String) As Task(Of String)
        Dim username As String = "user_" & Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 10)
        Dim salt As Byte() = PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt()
        Await _users.CreateAsync(username, PasswordHasher.Hash(password, salt, TestIterations), salt, TestIterations, False)
        Return username
    End Function

    Private Shared Function NewSpecies(name As String) As TreeSpecies
        Return New TreeSpecies With {
            .CommonName = name,
            .ScientificName = "Testus arboris",
            .CategoryId = 1,
            .ProductType = "Fresh fruit",
            .YieldUnit = "kg",
            .YieldPerTreePerYear = 55.25D,
            .PricePerUnit = 40D,
            .HarvestType = HarvestType.Annual,
            .YearsToFirstHarvest = 3,
            .YearsToFullProduction = 6
        }
    End Function

End Class

    <Test>
    Public Sub EscapeLikePattern_PutsWildcardsInBrackets()
        Assert.AreEqual("50[%][_][[]x]", SqlText.EscapeLikePattern("50%_[x]"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub EscapeLikePattern_LeavesNormalTextAlone()
        Assert.AreEqual("mango", SqlText.EscapeLikePattern("mango"))
        Assert.AreEqual("' OR 1=1 --", SqlText.EscapeLikePattern("' OR 1=1 --"), "quotes are harmless inside a parameter")
        Assert.IsNull(SqlText.EscapeLikePattern(Nothing))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub SplitStatements_RemovesCommentsAndSplitsOnSemicolons()
        Dim script As String =
            "-- header comment" & vbCrLf &
            "CREATE TABLE A (Id INTEGER);" & vbCrLf &
            vbCrLf &
            "  -- indented comment" & vbCrLf &
            "CREATE INDEX IX_A ON A (Id);" & vbCrLf &
            "   ;" & vbCrLf

        Dim statements As IList(Of String) = SqlScriptRunner.SplitStatements(script)

        Assert.AreEqual(2, statements.Count)
        Assert.AreEqual("CREATE TABLE A (Id INTEGER)", statements(0))
        Assert.AreEqual("CREATE INDEX IX_A ON A (Id)", statements(1))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub EmbeddedSchema_IsFoundAndHasEveryTable()
        Dim schema As String = SqlScriptRunner.LoadEmbeddedScript(DatabaseInitializer.SchemaScriptName)

        For Each table As String In {"SchemaVersion", "Categories", "TreeSpecies", "Users", "Scenarios", "AuditLog"}
            Assert.IsTrue(schema.Contains("CREATE TABLE " & table & " ("), table)
        Next
    End Sub

    <Test>

        Assert.AreEqual(22, SeedData.Species.Count)

            Dim result = TreeYield.Core.Services.SpeciesValidator.Validate(New TreeYield.Core.Models.TreeSpecies With {
                .CommonName = species.CommonName, .ScientificName = species.ScientificName, .CategoryId = 1,
                .ProductType = species.ProductType, .YieldUnit = species.YieldUnit,
                .YieldPerTreePerYear = species.YieldPerTreePerYear, .PricePerUnit = species.PricePerUnit,
                .HarvestType = species.HarvestType, .YearsToFirstHarvest = species.YearsToFirstHarvest,
                .YearsToFullProduction = species.YearsToFullProduction, .HarvestSeason = species.HarvestSeason,
                .Notes = species.Notes})
            Assert.IsTrue(result.IsValid, species.CommonName & ": " & result.ToString())
        Next
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Locator_FindsProjectCopyWhenRunningFromBinDebug()
        Dim root As String = NewTempFolder()
        Dim project As String = Path.Combine(root, "MyApp")
        Dim binDebug As String = Path.Combine(project, "bin", "Debug")
        Directory.CreateDirectory(binDebug)
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(project, "Database"))
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(project, "Database", "TreeYieldDb.mdb"), "x")

        Dim found As String = DatabaseFileLocator.Locate(binDebug, "Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb")

        Assert.AreEqual(Path.Combine(project, "Database", "TreeYieldDb.mdb"), found)
        Directory.Delete(root, True)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Locator_PrefersCopyNextToTheProgram()
        Dim root As String = NewTempFolder()
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(root, "Database"))
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(root, "Database", "TreeYieldDb.mdb"), "x")

        Assert.AreEqual(Path.Combine(root, "Database", "TreeYieldDb.mdb"), DatabaseFileLocator.Locate(root, "Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb"))
        Directory.Delete(root, True)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Locator_MissingFile_PointsToProjectFolderFromBin()
        Dim root As String = NewTempFolder()
        Dim binRelease As String = Path.Combine(root, "MyApp", "bin", "Release")
        Directory.CreateDirectory(binRelease)

        Assert.AreEqual(Path.Combine(root, "MyApp", "Database", "TreeYieldDb.mdb"),
                        DatabaseFileLocator.Locate(binRelease, "Database\TreeYieldDb.mdb"))
        Assert.AreEqual("C:\Data\Trees.mdb", DatabaseFileLocator.Locate(binRelease, "C:\Data\Trees.mdb"), "absolute paths are used as they are")
        Directory.Delete(root, True)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ErrorCodes_AreTranslatedToFriendlyMessages()
        Assert.IsTrue(TypeOf AccessErrorTranslator.FromCode("save", "3022", Nothing, Nothing) Is DuplicateRecordException, "3022 = duplicate")
        Assert.IsTrue(AccessErrorTranslator.FromCode("save", "3316", Nothing, Nothing).Message.Contains("rules"), "3316 = CHECK rule")
        Assert.IsTrue(AccessErrorTranslator.FromCode("open", "3045", Nothing, Nothing).Message.Contains("in use"), "3045 = file in use")
        Assert.IsTrue(AccessErrorTranslator.FromCode("open", "3024", Nothing, Nothing).Message.Contains("could not be found"), "3024 = file not found")
        Assert.IsTrue(AccessErrorTranslator.FromCode("save", "9999", "Operation must use an updateable query.", Nothing).Message.Contains("read-only"))
        Assert.IsTrue(AccessErrorTranslator.FromCode("load the list", "9999", "x", Nothing).Message.Contains("load the list"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ConnectionFactory_RequiresPathAndDefaultsToJet()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentException)(Sub()
                                                Dim unused As New AccessConnectionFactory("")
                                            End Sub)
        Dim factory As New AccessConnectionFactory("C:\Temp\x.mdb", Nothing)
        Assert.AreEqual(AccessConnectionFactory.DefaultProvider, factory.Provider)
        Assert.AreEqual("x.mdb", factory.DatabaseFileName)
    End Sub

    Private Shared Function NewTempFolder() As String
        Dim folder As String = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "TreeYieldLocator_" & Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 8))
        Directory.CreateDirectory(folder)
        Return folder
    End Function

FILE 87 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Tests\Framework\Assert.vb
====================================================================================================
Namespace Framework

    ''' <summary>Raised by <see cref="Assert"/> when a check fails.</summary>
    Public Class AssertionException
        Inherits Exception

        ''' <summary>Creates the exception.</summary>
        Public Sub New(message As String)
            MyBase.New(message)
        End Sub

    End Class

    ''' <summary>Minimal assertion helpers, so the tests need no external framework (no NuGet).</summary>
    Public NotInheritable Class Assert

        Private Sub New()
            ' Only Shared members.
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Fails unless the two values are equal.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub AreEqual(Of T)(expected As T, actual As T, Optional message As String = Nothing)
            If Not Equals(expected, actual) Then
                Fail(String.Format("Expected <{0}> but was <{1}>. {2}", expected, actual, message))
            End If
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Fails unless the condition is True.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub IsTrue(condition As Boolean, Optional message As String = Nothing)
            If Not condition Then
                Fail("Expected True. " & message)
            End If
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Fails unless the condition is False.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub IsFalse(condition As Boolean, Optional message As String = Nothing)
            If condition Then
                Fail("Expected False. " & message)
            End If
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Fails unless the value is Nothing.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub IsNull(value As Object, Optional message As String = Nothing)
            If value IsNot Nothing Then
                Fail(String.Format("Expected Nothing but was <{0}>. {1}", value, message))
            End If
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Fails if the value is Nothing.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub IsNotNull(value As Object, Optional message As String = Nothing)
            If value Is Nothing Then
                Fail("Expected a value but was Nothing. " & message)
            End If
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Fails unless the action throws <typeparamref name="TException"/> (or a subclass).</summary>
        Public Shared Function Throws(Of TException As Exception)(action As Action) As TException
            Try
                action()
            Catch ex As TException
                Return ex
            Catch ex As Exception
                Fail(String.Format("Expected {0} but {1} was thrown: {2}", GetType(TException).Name, ex.GetType().Name, ex.Message))
            End Try

            Fail(String.Format("Expected {0} but nothing was thrown.", GetType(TException).Name))
            Return Nothing
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Async version of <see cref="Throws"/>.</summary>
        Public Shared Async Function ThrowsAsync(Of TException As Exception)(action As Func(Of Task)) As Task(Of TException)
            Dim caught As Exception = Nothing
            Try
                Await action()
            Catch ex As Exception
                caught = ex
            End Try

            If caught Is Nothing Then
                Fail(String.Format("Expected {0} but nothing was thrown.", GetType(TException).Name))
            End If

            Dim expected As TException = TryCast(caught, TException)
            If expected Is Nothing Then
                Fail(String.Format("Expected {0} but {1} was thrown: {2}", GetType(TException).Name, caught.GetType().Name, caught.Message))
            End If

            Return expected
        End Function

        ''' <summary>Fails the test with a message.</summary>
        Public Shared Sub Fail(message As String)
            Throw New AssertionException(message)
        End Sub

    End Class

End Namespace

Namespace Framework

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Marks a public method as a unit test (no database needed). The method must take
    ''' no parameters and be a Sub or a Function that returns a Task.
    ''' </summary>
    <AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Method)>
    Public NotInheritable Class TestAttribute
        Inherits Attribute
    End Class

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Marks a test that needs a real Access database file. Skipped when the runner is
    ''' started with --unit-only.
    ''' </summary>
    <AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Method)>
    Public NotInheritable Class IntegrationTestAttribute
        Inherits Attribute
    End Class

End Namespace

FILE 89 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Tests\Framework\TestRunner.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Reflection

Namespace Framework

    ''' <summary>
    ''' Finds every method marked &lt;Test&gt; or &lt;IntegrationTest&gt; in this assembly using
    ''' reflection, runs it, and prints PASS or FAIL. A tiny stand-in for NUnit/MSTest
    ''' so the solution builds in Visual Studio 2015 without downloading packages.
    ''' </summary>
    Public Class TestRunner

        Private ReadOnly _includeIntegrationTests As Boolean

        ''' <summary>Creates the runner.</summary>
        ''' <param name="includeIntegrationTests">False to skip tests that need the database.</param>
        Public Sub New(includeIntegrationTests As Boolean)
            _includeIntegrationTests = includeIntegrationTests
        End Sub

        ''' <summary>Number of tests that passed in the last run.</summary>
        Public Property Passed As Integer

        ''' <summary>Number of tests that failed in the last run.</summary>
        Public Property Failed As Integer

        ''' <summary>Number of tests skipped in the last run.</summary>
        Public Property Skipped As Integer

        ''' <summary>Runs all tests and prints the results to the console.</summary>
        Public Sub RunAll()
            Dim tests As List(Of MethodInfo) = Discover()
            Console.WriteLine("Running {0} tests...", tests.Count)
            Console.WriteLine()

            For Each test As MethodInfo In tests
                Dim isIntegration As Boolean = test.GetCustomAttribute(Of IntegrationTestAttribute)() IsNot Nothing
                Dim name As String = test.DeclaringType.Name & "." & test.Name

                If isIntegration AndAlso Not _includeIntegrationTests Then
                    Skipped += 1
                    Write(ConsoleColor.DarkGray, "SKIP ", name)
                    Continue For
                End If

                Dim stopwatch As Stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew()
                Try
                    Dim instance As Object = Activator.CreateInstance(test.DeclaringType)
                    Dim returned As Object = test.Invoke(instance, Nothing)

                    ' Async tests return a Task; wait for it so failures are caught here.
                    Dim pending As Task = TryCast(returned, Task)
                    If pending IsNot Nothing Then
                        pending.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
                    End If

                    Passed += 1
                    Write(ConsoleColor.Green, "PASS ", String.Format("{0} ({1} ms)", name, stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds))
                Catch ex As Exception
                    ' Reflection wraps exceptions thrown by the test in TargetInvocationException.
                    Dim actual As Exception = If(TypeOf ex Is TargetInvocationException AndAlso ex.InnerException IsNot Nothing, ex.InnerException, ex)
                    Failed += 1
                    Write(ConsoleColor.Red, "FAIL ", name)
                    Console.WriteLine("       " & actual.GetType().Name & ": " & actual.Message)
                End Try
            Next

            Console.WriteLine()
            Write(If(Failed = 0, ConsoleColor.Green, ConsoleColor.Red), String.Empty,
                String.Format("{0} passed, {1} failed, {2} skipped.", Passed, Failed, Skipped))
        End Sub

        Private Shared Function Discover() As List(Of MethodInfo)
            ' Classes in name order, methods in the order they are written in the file.
            Return GetType(TestRunner).Assembly.GetTypes().
                Where(Function(t) t.IsClass AndAlso Not t.IsAbstract).
                OrderBy(Function(t) t.Name, StringComparer.Ordinal).
                SelectMany(Function(t) t.GetMethods(BindingFlags.Public Or BindingFlags.Instance).
                    Where(Function(m) m.GetCustomAttribute(Of TestAttribute)() IsNot Nothing OrElse
                                      m.GetCustomAttribute(Of IntegrationTestAttribute)() IsNot Nothing).
                    OrderBy(Function(m) m.MetadataToken)).
                ToList()
        End Function

        Private Shared Sub Write(color As ConsoleColor, prefix As String, text As String)
            Dim previous As ConsoleColor = Console.ForegroundColor
            Console.ForegroundColor = color
            Console.Write(prefix)
            Console.ForegroundColor = previous
            Console.WriteLine(text)
        End Sub

    End Class

End Namespace

Imports TreeYield.Core.Services
Imports TreeYield.Tests.Framework

''' <summary>Tests for <see cref="PasswordHasher"/> and <see cref="PasswordPolicy"/>.</summary>
Public Class PasswordTests

    ' A low iteration count keeps the unit tests fast; the algorithm is the same.
    Private Const TestIterations As Integer = 1000

    <Test>
    Public Sub Hash_ThenVerify_Succeeds()
        Dim salt As Byte() = PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt()
        Dim hash As Byte() = PasswordHasher.Hash("Mango2026", salt, TestIterations)

        Assert.IsTrue(PasswordHasher.Verify("Mango2026", salt, TestIterations, hash))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Verify_WrongPassword_Fails()
        Dim salt As Byte() = PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt()
        Dim hash As Byte() = PasswordHasher.Hash("Mango2026", salt, TestIterations)

        Assert.IsFalse(PasswordHasher.Verify("mango2026", salt, TestIterations, hash), "passwords are case-sensitive")
        Assert.IsFalse(PasswordHasher.Verify("", salt, TestIterations, hash))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub SamePassword_DifferentSalt_GivesDifferentHash()
        Dim first As Byte() = PasswordHasher.Hash("Mango2026", PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt(), TestIterations)
        Dim second As Byte() = PasswordHasher.Hash("Mango2026", PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt(), TestIterations)

        Assert.IsFalse(PasswordHasher.FixedTimeEquals(first, second))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Hash_HasExpectedSizes()
        Dim salt As Byte() = PasswordHasher.GenerateSalt()
        Assert.AreEqual(PasswordHasher.SaltSize, salt.Length)
        Assert.AreEqual(PasswordHasher.HashSize, PasswordHasher.Hash("x", salt, TestIterations).Length)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub FixedTimeEquals_HandlesDifferentLengthsAndNothing()
        Assert.IsFalse(PasswordHasher.FixedTimeEquals(New Byte() {1, 2}, New Byte() {1, 2, 3}))
        Assert.IsFalse(PasswordHasher.FixedTimeEquals(Nothing, New Byte() {1}))
        Assert.IsTrue(PasswordHasher.FixedTimeEquals(New Byte() {1, 2, 3}, New Byte() {1, 2, 3}))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Policy_RejectsShortPassword()
        Assert.IsNotNull(PasswordPolicy.Validate("Ab1", "admin"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Policy_RejectsPasswordWithoutDigit()
        Assert.IsNotNull(PasswordPolicy.Validate("OnlyLetters", "admin"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Policy_RejectsPasswordWithoutLetter()
        Assert.IsNotNull(PasswordPolicy.Validate("12345678", "admin"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Policy_RejectsUsernameAsPassword()
        Assert.IsNotNull(PasswordPolicy.Validate("Treeadmin1", "treeadmin1"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Policy_RejectsDefaultPassword()
        Assert.IsNotNull(PasswordPolicy.Validate(AuthService.DefaultAdminPassword, "admin"))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Policy_AcceptsGoodPassword()
        Assert.IsNull(PasswordPolicy.Validate("Narra2026", "admin"))
    End Sub

End Class

FILE 92 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Tests\Program.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports TreeYield.Tests.Framework

''' <summary>
''' Entry point of the test runner.
''' <para>Usage: TreeYield.Tests.exe [--unit-only] [--no-pause]</para>
''' The exit code is 0 when every test passed and 1 otherwise.
''' </summary>
Module Program

    Function Main(args As String()) As Integer
        Dim unitOnly As Boolean = args.Contains("--unit-only", StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        Dim noPause As Boolean = args.Contains("--no-pause", StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)

        Console.WriteLine("Interactive Tree Database & Yield Calculator - automated tests")
        Console.WriteLine(If(unitOnly, "Mode: unit tests only", "Mode: unit + database integration tests (Microsoft Access .mdb)"))
        Console.WriteLine()

        Dim runner As New TestRunner(Not unitOnly)
        Try
            runner.RunAll()
        Finally
            ' Remove the throw-away test database file.
            TestDatabase.DeleteIfCreated()
        End Try

        ' Keep the console window open when started with F5 from Visual Studio.
        If Not noPause AndAlso Environment.UserInteractive AndAlso Not Console.IsInputRedirected Then
            Console.WriteLine()
            Console.WriteLine("Press any key to close...")
            Console.ReadKey(True)
        End If

        Return If(runner.Failed = 0, 0, 1)
    End Function

End Module

    <Test>
    Public Sub NameLongerThan100Characters_IsRejected()

    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ControlCharacters_AreRejected()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.CommonName = "Mango" & ChrW(7)

        Assert.IsNotNull(SpeciesValidator.Validate(species).GetError(NameOf(TreeSpecies.CommonName)))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub MissingCategory_IsRejected()

        Assert.IsNotNull(SpeciesValidator.Validate(species).GetError(NameOf(TreeSpecies.CategoryId)))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ZeroYield_IsRejected()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.YieldPerTreePerYear = 0D

        Assert.IsNotNull(SpeciesValidator.Validate(species).GetError(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YieldPerTreePerYear)))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub YieldWithThreeDecimals_IsRejected()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.YieldPerTreePerYear = 1.555D

        Assert.IsNotNull(SpeciesValidator.Validate(species).GetError(NameOf(TreeSpecies.YieldPerTreePerYear)))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub NegativePrice_IsRejected()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.PricePerUnit = -0.01D

        Assert.AreEqual("Price cannot be negative.", SpeciesValidator.Validate(species).GetError(NameOf(TreeSpecies.PricePerUnit)))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub FreePrice_IsAllowed()

    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub FullProductionBeforeFirstHarvest_IsRejected()

        species.YearsToFirstHarvest = 8
        species.YearsToFullProduction = 5

    End Sub

    Public Sub RotationAgeZero_IsRejected()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Gmelina()

        species.YearsToFullProduction = 0

    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub SeveralProblems_AreAllReported()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.CommonName = ""
        species.ProductType = ""
        species.PricePerUnit = -5D

        Assert.AreEqual(3, SpeciesValidator.Validate(species).Errors.Count)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Normalize_TrimsTextAndSyncsRotationYears()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Gmelina()
        species.CommonName = "  Gmelina  "
        species.Notes = "   "
        species.YearsToFullProduction = 3

        Dim clean As TreeSpecies = SpeciesService.Normalize(species)

        Assert.AreEqual("Gmelina", clean.CommonName)
        Assert.IsNull(clean.Notes)
        Assert.AreEqual(10, clean.YearsToFullProduction)
        Assert.AreEqual("  Gmelina  ", species.CommonName, "the original object must not change")
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Scenario_WithoutName_IsRejected()
        Dim scenario As New Scenario With {.Name = " ", .SpeciesId = 1, .TreeCount = 25, .ProjectionYears = 10}
        Assert.IsNotNull(ScenarioService.Validate(scenario).GetError(NameOf(Scenario.Name)))
    End Sub

    Public Sub Scenario_TreeCountOutOfRange_IsRejected()
        Dim scenario As New Scenario With {.Name = "Plan", .SpeciesId = 1, .TreeCount = 0, .ProjectionYears = 10}
        Assert.IsNotNull(ScenarioService.Validate(scenario).GetError(NameOf(Scenario.TreeCount)))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Scenario_Valid_HasNoErrors()
        Dim scenario As New Scenario With {.Name = "Backyard plan", .SpeciesId = 1, .TreeCount = 25, .ProjectionYears = 10}
        Assert.IsTrue(ScenarioService.Validate(scenario).IsValid)
    End Sub

End Class

Imports TreeYield.Core.Models

''' <summary>Ready-made species used by several test classes.</summary>
Friend NotInheritable Class TestData

    Private Sub New()
        ' Only Shared members.
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Same figures as the seeded Carabao Mango: 300 kg/tree, PHP 80/kg, bears at year 5, full at year 10.</summary>
    Public Shared Function Mango() As TreeSpecies
        Return New TreeSpecies With {
            .SpeciesId = 1,
            .CommonName = "Carabao Mango",
            .ScientificName = "Mangifera indica",
            .CategoryId = 1,
            .CategoryName = Category.FruitBearing,
            .ProductType = "Fresh fruit",
            .YieldUnit = "kg",
            .YieldPerTreePerYear = 300D,
            .PricePerUnit = 80D,
            .HarvestType = HarvestType.Annual,
            .YearsToFirstHarvest = 5,
            .YearsToFullProduction = 10
        }
    End Function

    ''' <summary>Rotation species like the seeded Gmelina: 15 board ft/tree/yr, PHP 35, cut at year 10.</summary>
    Public Shared Function Gmelina() As TreeSpecies
        Return New TreeSpecies With {
            .SpeciesId = 2,
            .CommonName = "Gmelina",
            .ScientificName = "Gmelina arborea",
            .CategoryId = 2,
            .CategoryName = Category.NonFruitBearing,
            .ProductType = "Timber",
            .YieldUnit = "board ft",
            .YieldPerTreePerYear = 15D,
            .PricePerUnit = 35D,
            .HarvestType = HarvestType.Rotation,
            .YearsToFirstHarvest = 10,
            .YearsToFullProduction = 10
        }
    End Function

End Class

FILE 95 of 97: Interactive Tree Database\TreeYield.Tests\TestDatabase.vb
====================================================================================================
Imports System.Data.OleDb
Imports System.IO
Imports TreeYield.Core.Data
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services

''' <summary>
''' Creates a fresh, throw-away Access database file in the Windows temp folder the
''' first time an integration test needs it, and deletes it when the run ends. The
''' project's real TreeYieldDb.mdb is never touched.
''' </summary>
Friend NotInheritable Class TestDatabase

    Private Shared _factory As AccessConnectionFactory

    Private Sub New()
        ' Only Shared members.
    End Sub

    ''' <summary>Connection factory for the test database (created on first use).</summary>
    Public Shared ReadOnly Property Factory As AccessConnectionFactory
        Get
            If _factory Is Nothing Then
                _factory = Create()
            End If

            Return _factory
        End Get
    End Property

    ''' <summary>Deletes the test database file if this run created it.</summary>
    Public Shared Sub DeleteIfCreated()
        If _factory Is Nothing Then
            Return
        End If

        Try
            ' Pooled connections keep the file open; release them before deleting.
            OleDbConnection.ReleaseObjectPool()
            File.Delete(_factory.DatabasePath)
        Catch ex As IOException
            Console.WriteLine("Note: could not delete " & _factory.DatabasePath & ": " & ex.Message)
        End Try

        _factory = Nothing
    End Sub

    Private Shared Function Create() As AccessConnectionFactory
        Dim folder As String = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "TreeYieldTests")
        Dim fileName As String = "TreeYieldDb_Test_" & Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 8) & ".mdb"
        Dim factory As New AccessConnectionFactory(Path.Combine(folder, fileName))

        ' This also tests the "no database file yet" path: ADOX creates the .mdb,
        ' Schema.sql creates the tables and the sample data is inserted.
        Dim initializer As New DatabaseInitializer(factory)
        initializer.Initialize()

        Dim auth As New AuthService(New UserRepository(factory), New AuditLogRepository(factory))
        auth.EnsureDefaultAdminAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult()
        Return factory
    End Function

End Class

Imports TreeYield.Core.Models
Imports TreeYield.Core.Services
Imports TreeYield.Tests.Framework

''' <summary>Tests for the calculation engine (<see cref="YieldCalculator"/>).</summary>
Public Class YieldCalculatorTests

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_25CarabaoMangoTrees_Gives7500KgAnd600000Pesos()
        ' The example from the original design mock-up.
        Dim result As YieldResult = YieldCalculator.Calculate(TestData.Mango(), 25)

        Assert.AreEqual(7500D, result.AnnualYield)
        Assert.AreEqual(600000D, result.GrossSales)
        Assert.IsFalse(result.IsAnnualized)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_DecimalYield_KeepsExactDecimals()
        Dim cacao As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        cacao.YieldPerTreePerYear = 1.5D
        cacao.PricePerUnit = 180D

        Dim result As YieldResult = YieldCalculator.Calculate(cacao, 25)

        Assert.AreEqual(37.5D, result.AnnualYield)
        Assert.AreEqual(6750D, result.GrossSales)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_ZeroTrees_IsRejected()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentOutOfRangeException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Calculate(TestData.Mango(), 0))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_NegativeTrees_IsRejected()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentOutOfRangeException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Calculate(TestData.Mango(), -5))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_MoreThanMaximumTrees_IsRejected()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentOutOfRangeException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Calculate(TestData.Mango(), YieldCalculator.MaxTreeCount + 1))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_NullSpecies_IsRejected()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentNullException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Calculate(Nothing, 10))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_NegativePrice_IsRejected()
        Dim bad As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        bad.PricePerUnit = -1D
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Calculate(bad, 10))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_LargestAllowedValues_DoNotOverflow()
        Dim huge As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        huge.YieldPerTreePerYear = SpeciesValidator.MaxYieldPerTree
        huge.PricePerUnit = SpeciesValidator.MaxPricePerUnit

        Dim result As YieldResult = YieldCalculator.Calculate(huge, YieldCalculator.MaxTreeCount)

        Assert.AreEqual(10000000000D, result.AnnualYield)
        Assert.AreEqual(10000000000000000D, result.GrossSales)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Calculate_RotationSpecies_ReportsOneTimeHarvest()
        Dim result As YieldResult = YieldCalculator.Calculate(TestData.Gmelina(), 25)

        Assert.IsTrue(result.IsAnnualized)
        Assert.AreEqual(375D, result.AnnualYield, "25 trees x 15 board ft")
        Assert.AreEqual(10, result.HarvestYear)
        Assert.AreEqual(3750D, result.HarvestYield, "375 board ft x 10 years")
        Assert.AreEqual(131250D, result.HarvestSales, "3,750 board ft x PHP 35")
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ProductionFactor_BeforeFirstHarvest_IsZero()
        Assert.AreEqual(0D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(TestData.Mango(), 1))
        Assert.AreEqual(0D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(TestData.Mango(), 4))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ProductionFactor_RampsUpInEqualSteps()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.YearsToFirstHarvest = 5
        species.YearsToFullProduction = 8

        Assert.AreEqual(0.25D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 5))
        Assert.AreEqual(0.5D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 6))
        Assert.AreEqual(0.75D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 7))
        Assert.AreEqual(1D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 8))
        Assert.AreEqual(1D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 20))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ProductionFactor_SameFirstAndFullYear_JumpsToFull()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.YearsToFirstHarvest = 3
        species.YearsToFullProduction = 3

        Assert.AreEqual(0D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 2))
        Assert.AreEqual(1D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 3))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ProductionFactor_ZeroYears_MeansBearingFromYearOne()
        Dim species As TreeSpecies = TestData.Mango()
        species.YearsToFirstHarvest = 0
        species.YearsToFullProduction = 0

        Assert.AreEqual(1D, YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(species, 1))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub ProductionFactor_YearZero_IsRejected()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentOutOfRangeException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.GetProductionFactor(TestData.Mango(), 0))
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Project_Mango10Years_FollowsRampAndTotals()
        ' First harvest year 5, full production year 10: years 5..10 give 1/6, 2/6 ... 6/6.
        Dim projection As YieldProjection = YieldCalculator.Project(TestData.Mango(), 25, 10)

        Assert.AreEqual(10, projection.Rows.Count)
        Assert.AreEqual(0D, projection.Rows(3).Yield, "year 4 is not bearing yet")
        Assert.AreEqual(1250D, Decimal.Round(projection.Rows(4).Yield, 6), "year 5 = 7,500 / 6")
        Assert.AreEqual(7500D, projection.Rows(9).Yield, "year 10 = full production")

        ' 7,500 x (1+2+3+4+5+6)/6 = 26,250 kg; x PHP 80 = PHP 2,100,000.
        Assert.AreEqual(26250D, Decimal.Round(projection.TotalYield, 6))
        Assert.AreEqual(2100000D, Decimal.Round(projection.TotalSales, 4))
        Assert.AreEqual(projection.TotalSales, projection.Rows(9).CumulativeSales)
        Assert.AreEqual(5, projection.FirstHarvestYear)
        Assert.IsFalse(projection.FirstHarvestBeyondHorizon)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Project_RotationSpecies_HarvestsOnlyAtRotationAge()
        Dim projection As YieldProjection = YieldCalculator.Project(TestData.Gmelina(), 25, 10)

        For i As Integer = 0 To 8
            Assert.AreEqual(0D, projection.Rows(i).GrossSales, "year " & (i + 1).ToString())
        Next

        Assert.IsTrue(projection.Rows(9).IsHarvestYear)
        Assert.AreEqual(3750D, projection.Rows(9).Yield)
        Assert.AreEqual(131250D, projection.TotalSales)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Project_RotationSpecies_RepeatsAfterReplanting()
        Dim projection As YieldProjection = YieldCalculator.Project(TestData.Gmelina(), 25, 20)

        Assert.IsTrue(projection.Rows(9).IsHarvestYear, "year 10")
        Assert.IsTrue(projection.Rows(19).IsHarvestYear, "year 20")
        Assert.AreEqual(262500D, projection.TotalSales)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Project_HarvestAfterHorizon_IsFlagged()
        Dim mahogany As TreeSpecies = TestData.Gmelina()
        mahogany.YearsToFirstHarvest = 25
        mahogany.YearsToFullProduction = 25

        Dim projection As YieldProjection = YieldCalculator.Project(mahogany, 10, 10)

        Assert.IsTrue(projection.FirstHarvestBeyondHorizon)
        Assert.AreEqual(25, projection.FirstHarvestYear)
        Assert.AreEqual(0D, projection.TotalSales)
    End Sub

    <Test>
    Public Sub Project_HorizonOutsideRange_IsRejected()
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentOutOfRangeException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Project(TestData.Mango(), 10, 0))
        Assert.Throws(Of ArgumentOutOfRangeException)(Sub() YieldCalculator.Project(TestData.Mango(), 10, YieldCalculator.MaxProjectionYears + 1))
    End Sub

End Class
